Perfect! Now I have all the information. Let me compile a comprehensive report of all the tools in the Brain system.

## Comprehensive Tools Inventory for `app/src/server/tools/`

### Overview

The tools directory contains 23 tools organized in a shared-library pattern. All tools use Vercel AI SDK's `tool()` wrapper and Zod for input validation. Tools are registered in `index.ts` through `createChatAgentTools()` and can be composed by different agents (chat agent, PM subagent, analytics subagent).

### Core Types & Registration

**File: `types.ts`**
- **`ChatToolExecutionContext`** — Runtime context passed to tools via `ToolExecutionOptions.experimental_context`:
  - `actor`: `"chat_agent" | "mcp" | "pm_agent" | "analytics_agent"`
  - `agentType?`: `"code_agent" | "architect" | "management" | "design_partner" | "observer"`
  - `humanPresent?`: Boolean (true only for interactive web sessions with humans)
  - `identityRecord`: RecordId of the tool caller
  - `workspaceRecord`: Workspace scope for the tool
  - `conversationRecord`: Current conversation
  - `currentMessageRecord`: Current message (source of provenance)
  - `latestUserText`: Full user message text (for evidence/provenance)
  - `workspaceOwnerRecord?`: Optional workspace owner identity

- **`ChatToolDeps`** — Injected dependencies for tool execution:
  - `surreal`: SurrealDB client
  - `extractionModelId`: Model ID string
  - `extractionModel`: Model instance (for async work)
  - `extractionStoreThreshold`: Confidence threshold for storing extractions

- **`ChatAgentToolDeps`** — Extended deps for chat agent with subagent support:
  - Extends `ChatToolDeps`
  - `pmAgentModel`: Model instance for PM subagent
  - `analyticsAgentModel`: Model instance for analytics subagent
  - `analyticsSurreal`: Separate Surreal connection for analytics queries

**File: `helpers.ts`**
- `requireToolContext()` — Extracts and validates `ChatToolExecutionContext` from `ToolExecutionOptions`
- `toDecisionRecordId()` — Parses decision IDs (raw or `decision:id` format)

---

### All Tools (23)

#### Entity Discovery & Inspection

**1. `search_entities`** — Full-text search across knowledge graph
- **File**: `search-entities.ts`
- **What it does**: BM25 full-text search for finding entities by keyword or topic. Returns enriched results with related entities (neighbors).
- **Core function**: `executeSearchEntities(surreal, workspaceRecord, { query, kinds?, limit })`
- **Inputs**:
  - `query: string` — Search keywords
  - `kinds?: ["project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question" | "suggestion"]` — Optional filter
  - `limit: number` (default 10, max 25)
- **Output**: `{ query, results: [{ id, kind, name, confidence, related: [{ id, kind, name, relation }] }] }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace
- **Common use**: "Find authentication decisions", "Search for payment tasks"

**2. `list_workspace_entities`** — List entities by kind and status
- **File**: `list-workspace-entities.ts`
- **What it does**: List all entities of a specific kind (project, feature, task, decision, question, observation) with optional status/project filtering.
- **Core function**: `executeListWorkspaceEntities(surreal, workspaceRecord, { kind, status?, project?, limit })`
- **Inputs**:
  - `kind: "project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question" | "observation"`
  - `status?: string` — Optional filter (e.g., "open", "provisional", "done")
  - `project?: string` — Optional project name/ID to scope results
  - `limit: number` (default 25, max 50)
- **Output**: `{ kind, count, entities: [{ id, name, ...[status/category/sourceAgent] }] }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace
- **Common use**: "What decisions are there?", "List all open tasks", "Show all observations"

**3. `get_entity_detail`** — Full entity details with relationships and provenance
- **File**: `get-entity-detail.ts`
- **What it does**: Fetch complete entity record with relationships, linked entities, and provenance chain.
- **Core function**: `executeGetEntityDetail(surreal, workspaceRecord, entityId: string)`
- **Inputs**:
  - `entityId: string` — Entity ID in format `table:id` (e.g., `decision:abc123`)
- **Output**: Full entity record with `edges`, `linkedEntities`, `provenance`, `metadata`, etc.
- **Context needed**: Workspace
- **Common use**: "Show me details about this decision", "What is the history of this task?"

**4. `get_project_status`** — Project aggregation (tasks, decisions, questions, features)
- **File**: `get-project-status.ts`
- **What it does**: Fetch project summary including active task count, recent decisions, open questions, and features.
- **Core function**: `executeGetProjectStatus(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectInput: string)`
- **Inputs**:
  - `projectId: string` — Project name or `project:uuid`
- **Output**: Project record with aggregated counts and lists of tasks/decisions/questions/features
- **Context needed**: Workspace
- **Common use**: "What's the status of the Auth project?", "How many tasks are open in Billing?"

**5. `get_conversation_history`** — Search past conversations by topic
- **File**: `get-conversation-history.ts`
- **What it does**: Full-text search past messages ranked by BM25 relevance. Returns message excerpts with linked entities extracted during those conversations.
- **Inputs**:
  - `query: string` — Topic to search
  - `projectId?: string` — Optional project scope
- **Output**: `{ query, results: [{ messageId, conversationId, excerpt, confidence, linkedEntities }] }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace
- **Common use**: "What did we discuss about rate limiting?", "Show me conversations about the payment system"

---

#### Decision Management

**6. `resolve_decision`** — Query-based decision resolution
- **File**: `resolve-decision.ts`
- **What it does**: Search graph for existing decisions matching a question. Returns: (1) **resolved** if match score ≥0.92, (2) **inferred** if score ≥0.78 (creates new inferred decision from related context), or (3) **unresolved** with related context.
- **Core function**: `executeResolveDecision(surreal, workspaceRecord, { question, options?, context: { project?, feature? } })`
- **Inputs**:
  - `question: string` — The decision question
  - `options?: string[]` — Optional alternatives being considered
  - `context?.project?: string` — Optional scope
  - `context?.feature?: string` — Optional scope
- **Output**: `{ status: "resolved" | "inferred" | "unresolved", decision?: {...}, sources: [...], relatedContext?: [...] }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, current message (for provenance)
- **Authorization**: `"create_decision"` intent
- **Common use**: "What's our decision on caching strategy?" (agent queries before suggesting provisional)

**7. `create_provisional_decision`** — Create a decision for user review
- **File**: `create-provisional-decision.ts`
- **What it does**: Create a draft decision with status `provisional`. Creates extraction provenance edge linking source message → decision.
- **Inputs**:
  - `name: string` — Decision name
  - `rationale: string` — Reasoning
  - `options_considered?: string[]` — Alternatives
  - `context?.project?: string`
  - `context?.feature?: string`
- **Output**: `{ decision_id, status: "provisional", review_required: true }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, current message, identity (for authorization)
- **Authorization**: `"create_decision"` intent
- **Common use**: "I suggest we use X approach because...", then user reviews

**8. `confirm_decision`** — Finalize a provisional/inferred decision
- **File**: `confirm-decision.ts`
- **What it does**: Transition decision from `provisional` or `inferred` to `confirmed`. Only callable by `chat_agent` (not MCP). Records `confirmed_at` and optional `notes`.
- **Inputs**:
  - `decision_id: string` — Decision record ID
  - `notes?: string` — Additional user context
- **Output**: `{ decision_id, status: "confirmed", name }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, identity, workspace owner
- **Authorization**: `"confirm_decision"` intent, requires `actor === "chat_agent"`
- **Common use**: "Yes, I approve that decision"

**9. `check_constraints`** — Validate proposed action against existing decisions
- **File**: `check-constraints.ts`
- **What it does**: Search graph for decisions related to a proposed action. Returns hard conflicts (contested/superseded), soft tensions (related context), and supporting decisions. Uses token overlap + BM25 scoring.
- **Core function**: `executeCheckConstraints(surreal, workspaceRecord, proposedAction: string)`
- **Inputs**:
  - `proposed_action: string` — What is being proposed
  - `project?: string` — Optional scope
- **Output**: `{ hard_conflicts: [...], soft_tensions: [...], supporting: [...], proceed: boolean }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace
- **Common use**: "Should we migrate to Node.js?" (agent checks constraints before proceeding)

---

#### Questions & Observations

**10. `create_question`** — Create an open question requiring a choice
- **File**: `create-question.ts`
- **What it does**: Create a question entity for pending decisions. Creates extraction provenance edge.
- **Inputs**:
  - `text: string` — Question text
  - `category?: string` — Optional category (from `ENTITY_CATEGORIES`)
  - `priority?: "critical" | "high" | "medium" | "low"`
  - `assigned_to?: string` — Person name
  - `context?.project?: string`
  - `context?.feature?: string`
- **Output**: `{ question_id, status: "open" }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, current message, identity
- **Authorization**: `"create_question"` intent
- **Common use**: "Should we use X or Y?", "Which approach for Z?"

**11. `create_observation`** — Create a cross-cutting signal (risk, conflict, gap, awareness)
- **File**: `create-observation.ts`
- **What it does**: Create an observation — lightweight agent-to-agent signals for contradictions, risks, gaps, or notable facts. Can link to a related entity.
- **Inputs**:
  - `text: string` — Observation text
  - `severity: "info" | "warning" | "conflict"`
  - `category?: string` — Optional category
  - `related_entity_id?: string` — Optional entity (project/feature/task/decision/question)
- **Output**: `{ observation_id, severity, status: "open" }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, current message, identity, workspace scope validation
- **Authorization**: `"create_observation"` intent
- **Common use**: Agent notices contradiction and logs it for other agents to see

**12. `acknowledge_observation`** — Mark observation as reviewed
- **File**: `acknowledge-observation.ts`
- **What it does**: Transition observation from `open` → `acknowledged` (reviewed but still needs resolution).
- **Inputs**:
  - `observation_id: string` — Observation record ID
- **Output**: `{ observation_id, status: "acknowledged" }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, identity
- **Authorization**: `"acknowledge_observation"` intent

**13. `resolve_observation`** — Close a resolved observation
- **File**: `resolve-observation.ts`
- **What it does**: Transition observation from `open`/`acknowledged` → `resolved` (concern has been addressed).
- **Inputs**:
  - `observation_id: string` — Observation record ID
- **Output**: `{ observation_id, status: "resolved" }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, identity, workspace owner
- **Authorization**: `"resolve_observation"` intent

---

#### Suggestions

**14. `create_suggestion`** — Proactive agent-to-human proposal
- **File**: `create-suggestion.ts`
- **What it does**: Create a suggestion — actionable proposal for the user with rationale and confidence. Links to optional target entity and evidence entities.
- **Inputs**:
  - `text: string` — The suggestion itself
  - `category: "optimization" | "risk" | "opportunity" | "conflict" | "missing" | "pivot"`
  - `rationale: string` — Why you suggest this
  - `confidence: number` (0-1) — How confident
  - `target_entity_id?: string` — Optional target entity (e.g., `project:uuid`)
  - `evidence_entity_ids?: string[]` — Optional supporting entities
- **Output**: `{ suggestion_id, text, category, rationale, confidence, status: "pending", target? }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, current message, identity, workspace scope validation
- **Authorization**: `"create_suggestion"` intent
- **Common use**: "Consider migrating to TypeScript because we have 5 related decisions about type safety"

---

#### Work Item Management

**15. `create_work_item`** — Create tasks, features, or projects
- **File**: `create-work-item.ts`
- **What it does**: Create a task, feature, or project directly in the graph. Projects must be created before features/tasks can be scoped under them. Seeds description, creates extraction provenance, handles project/feature linking.
- **Inputs**:
  - `kind: "task" | "feature" | "project"`
  - `title: string` — Entity name
  - `rationale: string` — Seeds description field
  - `category?: string` (task/feature only)
  - `priority?: "critical" | "high" | "medium" | "low"` (task only)
  - `project?: string` — Scope under project
  - `feature?: string` — Scope task under feature
- **Output**: `{ entity_id, kind, title }` or `{ error: string }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, current message, identity
- **Authorization**: `"create_task"` intent
- **Special validation**: Projects cannot have the same name as the workspace
- **Common use**: "Add a task to implement OAuth", "Create an Auth project"

**16. `edit_work_item`** — Update task/feature/project metadata
- **File**: `edit-work-item.ts`
- **What it does**: Edit existing work items by ID. Supports: title, status, category (task/feature only), priority (task only), and appending rationale as description entry.
- **Inputs**:
  - `id: string` — Existing entity ID (table:id)
  - `title?: string` — New title
  - `status?: string` — New status
  - `category?: string` (task/feature only)
  - `priority?: "critical" | "high" | "medium" | "low"` (task only)
  - `rationale?: string` — Appended as description entry
- **Output**: `{ entity_id, kind, title, updated_fields: [...], rationale_added?: true }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, current message, identity, workspace scope validation
- **Authorization**: `"create_task"` intent
- **Common use**: "Rename this task to X", "Mark this as done"

**17. `move_items_to_project`** — Reorganize features/tasks between projects
- **File**: `move-items-to-project.ts`
- **What it does**: Move existing features or tasks to a different project. Deletes old project edges and creates new ones. Does NOT duplicate entities.
- **Inputs**:
  - `entity_ids: string[]` — Feature/task IDs to move
  - `target_project: string` — Project name or `project:uuid`
- **Output**: `{ moved: [{ entity_id, title }], failed: [{ entity_id, reason }] }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, identity, workspace scope validation
- **Authorization**: `"create_task"` intent
- **Common use**: "Move these tasks to the Billing project"

**18. `suggest_work_items`** — Batch triage & duplicate detection
- **File**: `suggest-work-items.ts`
- **What it does**: Process batch of proposed work items and return PM-style triage buckets: (1) **suggestions** (new items), (2) **updated** (significant keyword overlap → suggest merge), (3) **discarded** (exact duplicates). Uses BM25 text matching on item titles. Filters out closed items (done/completed/etc.).
- **Inputs**:
  - `items: [{ kind, title, rationale, category?, project?, priority? }]` (1-25 items)
- **Output**: `{ suggestions: [...], updated: [{ existing_id, title, changes }], discarded: [{ title, reason }] }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, identity
- **Authorization**: `"create_suggestion"` intent
- **Common use**: PM agent or UI batch creation → triage before persisting

---

#### Subagent Dispatch

**19. `invoke_pm_agent`** — Delegate to Product Manager subagent
- **File**: `invoke-pm-agent.ts`
- **What it does**: Dispatch to PM subagent (Haiku model) — the single authority on tasks, features, and project status. PM agent uses shared tools and returns structured JSON with suggestions, updated items, discarded items.
- **Inputs**:
  - `intent: "plan_work" | "check_status" | "organize" | "track_dependencies"`
  - `context: string` — Relevant user message text (agent decides entity classification)
- **Output**: PM agent's structured result: `{ summary, suggestions, updated, discarded, observations_created }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace, conversation, identity, current message
- **Dependencies**: Requires `pmAgentModel` and `pmAgentModel` in `ChatAgentToolDeps`
- **Common use**: "Tell me the status of the Auth project", "Help me organize these tasks"

**20. `invoke_analytics_agent`** — Delegate to Analytics subagent
- **File**: `invoke-analytics-agent.ts`
- **What it does**: Dispatch to analytics agent for pattern/metric/trend questions across the knowledge graph. Complements single-entity tools (search_entities, get_entity_detail).
- **Inputs**:
  - `question: string` — Analytical question
- **Output**: Analytics agent's answer
- **Context needed**: Workspace, conversation, identity, current message
- **Dependencies**: Requires `analyticsAgentModel` and `analyticsSurreal` in `ChatAgentToolDeps`
- **Common use**: "How many tasks are overdue?", "What's the trend in decision velocity?"

---

#### Visualization

**21. `show_relationship_graph`** — Interactive relationship visualization
- **File**: `show-relationship-graph.ts`
- **What it does**: Show focused graph views for 1-10 entities. Merges results to avoid duplicates. Returns component block for rendering interactive graph (InlineRelationshipGraph).
- **Inputs**:
  - `entity_ids: string[]` — 1-10 entity IDs to visualize
- **Output**: `{ component: "InlineRelationshipGraph", props: { title, nodes, edges, focusNodeIds } }`
- **Context needed**: Workspace
- **Common use**: "Show how this decision relates to other entities", "Visualize the dependency chain"

---

### Registration & Composition

**File: `index.ts`**
```typescript
function createChatAgentTools(deps: ChatAgentToolDeps) {
  return {
    list_workspace_entities: createListWorkspaceEntitiesTool(deps),
    search_entities: createSearchEntitiesTool(deps),
    get_entity_detail: createGetEntityDetailTool(deps),
    get_project_status: createGetProjectStatusTool(deps),
    get_conversation_history: createGetConversationHistoryTool(deps),
    resolve_decision: createResolveDecisionTool(deps),
    check_constraints: createCheckConstraintsTool(deps),
    create_provisional_decision: createCreateProvisionalDecisionTool(deps),
    create_question: createCreateQuestionTool(deps),
    confirm_decision: createConfirmDecisionTool(deps),
    create_observation: createCreateObservationTool(deps),
    create_suggestion: createCreateSuggestionTool(deps),
    acknowledge_observation: createAcknowledgeObservationTool(deps),
    resolve_observation: createResolveObservationTool(deps),
    invoke_pm_agent: createInvokePmAgentTool(deps),
    invoke_analytics_agent: createInvokeAnalyticsAgentTool(deps),
    show_relationship_graph: createShowRelationshipGraphTool(deps),
  };
}
```

Note: `suggest-work-items`, `create-work-item`, `edit-work-item`, and `move-items-to-project` are shared tools composed by PM agent and chat agent separately (not registered in the main chat agent tools object).

---

### Context Requirements Summary

| Requirement | Used By | Notes |
|-----------|---------|-------|
| **workspace** | All tools | SurrealDB record ID scope |
| **identity** | Tools marked `requireAuthorizedContext` | For IAM authorization |
| **currentMessage** | Tools that create provenance | Links tool action back to user input |
| **latestUserText** | Decision/observation/work-item tools | Evidence for extraction provenance |
| **workspaceOwner** | confirm_decision, resolve_observation | Optional; resolved at runtime if missing |
| **actor type** | confirm_decision | Only `chat_agent` can confirm decisions |
| **humanPresent** | Not currently used | Could gate certain high-stakes tools |

---

### Authorization Intent Types

Tools call `requireAuthorizedContext(options, intent, deps)` to enforce IAM policy:
- `"create_decision"` — resolve_decision, create_provisional_decision
- `"confirm_decision"` — confirm_decision (chat_agent only)
- `"create_question"` — create_question
- `"create_observation"` — create_observation
- `"acknowledge_observation"` — acknowledge_observation
- `"resolve_observation"` — resolve_observation
- `"create_suggestion"` — create_suggestion, suggest_work_items
- `"create_task"` — create_work_item, edit_work_item, move_items_to_project

---

### Extraction Provenance Pattern

Tools that create graph entities (`create_provisional_decision`, `create_question`, `create_observation`, `create_work_item`, `edit_work_item`) call `createExtractionProvenanceEdge()` to:
- Link source message → created entity
- Record confidence (typically 0.9-0.95 for agent-created)
- Store model ID that created the entity
- Preserve original user text as evidence
- Enable traceability and attribution

---

This is the complete inventory of the Brain tools system. All 23 tools are composable, typed, and integrated through a shared dependency injection pattern with centralized context passing and authorization.

## Intent-Gating and Authorization Pattern in Brain

Based on my exploration of the codebase, here's the comprehensive architecture of how intent-gating and authorization works:

### 1. **Intent Lifecycle & Status Machine**

Intents follow a strict state machine with the following statuses:

```
draft → pending_auth → [pending_veto / authorized / vetoed / failed]
        ↓
    authorized → executing → completed / failed
    pending_veto → [authorized / vetoed]
    vetoed / failed → (terminal)
```

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/intent/status-machine.ts`

### 2. **Intent Records & Data Structure**

**Type Definition** (`intent/types.ts`):
- `IntentStatus`: One of the states above
- `ActionSpec`: Provider + action + optional params (what to authorize)
- `IntentRecord`: Persistent intent with goal, reasoning, priority, evaluation results, requester, workspace, trace_id, authorization_details (RAR), DPoP thumbprint

**Key Fields for Authorization:**
- `authorization_details`: BrainAction[] — RAR (RFC 9396) authorization details for DPoP binding
- `dpop_jwk_thumbprint`: DPoP JWK thumbprint binding intent to a specific keypair
- `evaluation`: Policy + LLM evaluation results with risk_score
- `veto_expires_at`: Veto window expiry for pending_veto intents

### 3. **Intent Creation Pipeline**

Two entry points create intents:

#### **Path A: MCP create_intent Tool** (Brain-Native Self-Escalation)
**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/create-intent-handler.ts`

Flow:
1. **Validate input** — goal, reasoning, action_spec (pure)
2. **Derive authorization_details** from action_spec:
   ```
   { type: "brain_action", action: "execute", resource: "mcp_tool:{provider}:{action}" }
   ```
3. **Create trace** (audit trail)
4. **Create intent** in `draft` status
5. **Transition to pending_auth**
6. **Evaluate policy gate** via `evaluatePolicyGate()`
   - If blocked: transition to `vetoed`, no gates edge
   - If approved + human_veto_required: transition to `pending_veto`, create `gates` edge
   - If approved + no veto required: transition to `authorized`, create `gates` edge
7. Return `CreateIntentOutcome`

#### **Path B: OAuth 2.1 RAR Intent Submission**
**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/oauth/intent-submission.ts`

Flow:
1. Validate `authorization_details` (BrainAction[]) with type "brain_action"
2. Bind DPoP thumbprint to intent
3. Create intent with explicit authorization_details from requestor
4. Trigger full evaluation pipeline (policy gate + LLM evaluation)
5. Route by risk score to auto_approve, veto_window, or reject

### 4. **Authorization & Gating Model**

**Three-Layer Gate System:**

#### **Layer 1: Policy Gate** (Deterministic, Graph-Stored)
**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/intent/authorizer.ts` + `policy/policy-gate.ts`

- Evaluates policies from `policy` table (status="active") against intent
- Returns:
  - `passed: true/false`
  - `reason`: denial reason
  - `deny_rule_id`: which policy denied
  - `human_veto_required`: policy forces human review
  - `policy_trace`: audit chain of evaluated policies
- **No LLM needed** — purely deterministic graph evaluation

#### **Layer 2: LLM Evaluation** (Risk Scoring, Optional)
**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/intent/authorizer.ts`

- `createLlmEvaluator()` generates via Vercel AI SDK
- Evaluates intent goal/reasoning/action for:
  - Least-privilege compliance
  - Scope boundary violations
  - Prompt injection risk
  - Reversibility assessment
- Returns:
  - `decision`: APPROVE or REJECT
  - `risk_score`: 0–100
  - `reason`: one-line summary
  - `reasoning`: full chain-of-thought
- **On timeout/failure**: Falls back to policy-only with veto_window route

#### **Layer 3: Risk Router** (Conditional Human Veto)
**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/intent/risk-router.ts`

Routes approved intents based on risk_score:
- **risk_score ≤ 30** (default threshold): `auto_approve` → transition to `authorized`
- **30 < risk_score < threshold**: `veto_window` (30-min default) → transition to `pending_veto`
- **Policy blocks or LLM rejects**: `reject` → transition to `vetoed`

If policy has `human_veto_required = true`, **always** route to `veto_window` regardless of score.

### 5. **Veto Window & Auto-Approval**

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/intent/veto-manager.ts`

- Veto manager maintains in-memory setTimeout timers per intentId
- When veto window expires:
  - Auto-approve: transition `pending_veto → authorized`
  - Emit veto_auto_approved event
- Human can manually veto within window → transition to `vetoed`
- Manual approval moves `pending_veto → authorized`
- Manual constraint-tightening: human can reduce authorization_details scope before approving

### 6. **Evaluation Pipeline Integration**

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/intent/intent-evaluation.ts`

Main evaluation function:
```typescript
evaluateIntent({
  intent,           // goal, reasoning, action_spec, budget_limit, priority
  surreal,          // DB access
  identityId,       // requester
  workspaceId,
  requesterType,    // "agent" vs "human" for policy context
  llmEvaluator,     // evaluator function
  entityRef,        // optional: task/project for alignment
  findAlignedObjectivesViaGraph,  // optional: find supporting objectives
  createSupportsEdge,             // optional: link intent→objective
  createAlignmentWarning,         // warning if no alignment found
})
```

Returns:
```typescript
{
  decision: "APPROVE" | "REJECT",
  risk_score: number,
  reason: string,
  policy_only: boolean,           // true if LLM timeout/failure
  policy_trace: PolicyTraceEntry[],
  human_veto_required: boolean,
  alignment?: AlignmentResult,    // objective matching (warning-mode, doesn't block)
}
```

### 7. **DPoP & RAR Integration Points**

**DPoP (Demonstration of Proof-of-Possession):**
- Intent captures `dpop_jwk_thumbprint` at submission time
- Binding prevents token reuse with different keypairs
- Validated by MCP route handlers via `authenticateAndAuthorize()`

**RAR (Rich Authorization Requests):**
- `authorization_details` array on intent record
- Each entry: `{ type: "brain_action", action: "execute", resource: "..." }`
- Constraint tightening API allows humans to reduce scope before approval

### 8. **Intent Evaluation Hooks & Events**

**SurrealQL EVENT** (auto-triggered):
- When intent transitions to `pending_auth`, HTTP webhook POSTs to `/intent/evaluate/:id`
- Handler calls `evaluatePendingIntent()` → evaluates, routes by risk → transitions to final state

**Output** of evaluation event:
- Status transition (authorized / pending_veto / vetoed)
- Evaluation metadata stored on intent record
- Veto window timer started (if pending_veto)

### 9. **Tool Authorization (Proxy Layer)**

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/proxy/tool-executor.ts`

Governance policies can gate tool execution:
- `evaluateGovernancePolicies()` checks:
  - "requires_human_approval" condition → deny
  - max_per_day exceeded → deny
  - Multiple policies: most restrictive wins
- Does NOT use intents (separate governance model for tool calls)

### 10. **LLM Proxy Policy Enforcement**

**File:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/proxy/policy-evaluator.ts`

Three-tier proxy policy evaluation **before** forwarding to Claude:
1. **Rate limiting** (in-memory sliding window per workspace)
2. **Budget enforcement** (daily spend vs. limit, cached)
3. **Model access** (policies define allowed models per agent_type)

Returns allow/deny with 403/429 status codes.

### 11. **Key Files & Relationships**

| Component | File(s) | Purpose |
|-----------|---------|---------|
| Types & Status Machine | `intent/types.ts`, `intent/status-machine.ts` | Data model, state graph |
| Policy Gate | `policy/policy-gate.ts` | Deterministic policy evaluation |
| LLM Evaluation | `intent/authorizer.ts` | Risk scoring, evaluator factory |
| Risk Router | `intent/risk-router.ts` | Route to auto_approve / veto_window / reject |
| Veto Manager | `intent/veto-manager.ts` | In-memory veto window timers |
| Evaluation Pipeline | `intent/intent-evaluation.ts` | Orchestrate policy + LLM + alignment |
| MCP create_intent | `mcp/create-intent-handler.ts` | Brain-native self-escalation tool |
| OAuth Intent Submission | `oauth/intent-submission.ts` | RAR + DPoP entry point |
| Intent Routes | `intent/intent-routes.ts` | HTTP handlers (evaluate, veto, approve, constrain) |
| MCP Routes | `mcp/mcp-route.ts` | DPoP auth + intent context injection |
| Proxy Policies | `proxy/policy-evaluator.ts` | LLM request pre-flight gates |
| Tool Governance | `proxy/tool-executor.ts` | Per-call execution policies |

### 12. **Summary: What "Intent-Gating" Means**

Intent-gating is a **three-layer, graduated authorization model** where:

1. **Policies block dangerous actions** deterministically (no LLM)
2. **LLM evaluates risk** for approved-but-risky intents (timeout-safe fallback)
3. **Human veto window** provides graceful override for edge cases

The system **never gatekeeps tool execution directly**; instead:
- Intents capture **what** an agent wants to do + **why**
- Policy graph evaluates against governance rules
- LLM assesses risk on a 0–100 scale
- Risk router decides: auto-approve, require human veto, or outright reject
- Veto window auto-approves after 30m (timeout-safe)
- Humans can constrain scope (tighten RAR authorization_details) before approving

This enables **autonomous agents to escalate requests** without blocking on human latency, while preserving **deterministic governance** (policies) and **risk-aware escalation** (LLM + veto).

## Summary

I've completed a thorough exploration of the MCP endpoint implementation in this codebase. Here are my findings:

### MCP Endpoints Overview

The Brain system exposes MCP (Model Context Protocol) through two main channels:

#### 1. **Agent MCP Endpoint** (`/mcp/agent/:sessionId`)
This is the primary JSON-RPC endpoint for sandbox coding agents (like Cursor, Claude Code). It supports:

- **`tools/list`** — Lists available tools (both Brain-native and external MCP tools)
- **`tools/call`** — Executes a tool call
- **`create_intent`** — Direct method to create authorization intents

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts`

#### 2. **Brain-Native MCP Tools** (Always Available)
Two tools are always exposed without requiring intent authorization:

1. **`get_context`** — Fetch project context from the Brain knowledge graph
   - Parameters: `intent` (description of work)
   - Returns: decisions, tasks, observations, and other relevant entities

2. **`create_intent`** — Request authorization for gated tools
   - Parameters: `goal`, `reasoning`, `action_spec` (provider, action, params)
   - Returns: intent status and intentId

**Definition Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts` (lines 26-68)

#### 3. **Gated Tools** (Require Intent Authorization)
All other tools must be authorized via `create_intent` before use. These come from two sources:

**A. External MCP Servers:**
- Tools are discovered via `client.listTools()` from external MCP servers
- Each tool is stored in the `mcp_tool` SurrealDB table with:
  - `name`, `toolkit`, `description`, `input_schema`, `output_schema`
  - `workspace`, `status` (active/disabled), `risk_level` (low/medium/high/critical)
  - `source_server` reference to the originating MCP server
- Tools marked as `[GATED]` in their description require authorization

**B. Brain-Native HTTP Endpoints:**
The `/api/mcp/:workspaceId/*` endpoints expose Brain graph operations:

**Tier 1 Read (Context):**
- `GET /api/mcp/:workspaceId/projects` — List workspace projects
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/context` — Intent-based context resolution
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/workspace-context` — Workspace overview
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/project-context` — Project details
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/task-context` — Task details
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/decisions` — List decisions
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/tasks/dependencies` — Task dependency tree
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/constraints` — List constraints
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/changes` — Recent changes
- `GET /api/mcp/:workspaceId/entities/:entityId` — Entity detail

**Tier 2 Reason:**
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/decisions/resolve` — Resolve a decision
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/constraints/check` — Validate constraints

**Tier 3 Write:**
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/decisions/provisional` — Create provisional decision
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/questions` — Ask a question
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/tasks/status` — Update task status
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/tasks/subtask` — Create subtask
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/notes` — Log implementation note
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/observations` — Log observation

**Suggestions:**
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/suggestions` — List suggestions
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/suggestions/create` — Create suggestion
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/suggestions/action` — Accept/dismiss/defer suggestion
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/suggestions/convert` — Convert to task/decision

**Lifecycle:**
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/sessions/start` — Start agent session
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/sessions/end` — End agent session with summary
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/commits` — Log git commit
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/commits/pre-check` — Pre-commit validation
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/commits/post-check` — Post-commit processing

**Intent Tools:**
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/intents/create` — Create intent
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/intents/submit` — Submit intent for approval
- `POST /api/mcp/:workspaceId/intents/status` — Check intent status

### Tool Authorization Model

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/scope-engine.ts`

Tools are classified as:
1. **`brain_native`** — Always available (get_context, create_intent)
2. **`authorized`** — Identity has a `can_use` edge to this tool via an approved intent
3. **`gated`** — Tool exists but not authorized; description includes `[GATED]` marker with escalation instructions

**Database Schema:**
- `mcp_tool` table (lines 1399-1414 in `schema/surreal-schema.surql`) — Tool definitions
- `can_use` relation table (lines 1417-1421) — Identity → Tool authorization edges
- Tool resolution query in `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/proxy/tool-resolver.ts` (lines 115-120) — Fetches all tools where `can_use` edge exists and tool is `active`

### Key Files

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts` | JSON-RPC handler for `/mcp/agent/:sessionId` |
| `app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts` | Builds tools list with brain-native + classified tools |
| `app/src/server/mcp/scope-engine.ts` | Tool classification logic (authorized/gated/brain_native) |
| `app/src/server/mcp/tools-call-handler.ts` | Executes tool calls and records traces |
| `app/src/server/mcp/mcp-route.ts` | HTTP handlers for `/api/mcp/:workspaceId/*` endpoints |
| `app/src/server/proxy/tool-resolver.ts` | Resolves `can_use` edges to build identity's toolset |
| `app/src/server/proxy/tool-injector.ts` | Merges Brain tools into LLM request's tools array |
| `schema/surreal-schema.surql` | Database schema (mcp_tool, can_use relations) |

     1→/**
     2→ * Tools List Handler -- Pure function for building MCP ListToolsResult
     3→ * from classified tools.
     4→ *
     5→ * Pure core: no IO, no DB, no side effects.
     6→ */
     7→import type { ClassifiedTool } from "./scope-engine";
     8→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9→// Types
    10→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11→
    12→export type McpToolDefinition = {
    13→  readonly name: string;
    14→  readonly description: string;
    15→  readonly inputSchema: Record<string, unknown>;
    16→};
    17→
    18→export type ListToolsResult = {
    19→  readonly tools: readonly McpToolDefinition[];
    20→};
    21→
    22→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23→// Brain-native tool definitions
    24→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→
    26→const CREATE_INTENT_TOOL: McpToolDefinition = {
    27→  name: "create_intent",
    28→  description:
    29→    "Create an intent to request authorization for a gated tool. " +
    30→    "Use this when a tool you need is marked as [GATED]. " +
    31→    "Provide the goal, reasoning, and action_spec describing the tool you want to use.",
    32→  inputSchema: {
    33→    type: "object",
    34→    properties: {
    35→      goal: { type: "string", description: "What you want to accomplish with this tool" },
    36→      reasoning: { type: "string", description: "Why this tool is needed for the current task" },
    37→      action_spec: {
    38→        type: "object",
    39→        properties: {
    40→          provider: { type: "string", description: "The toolkit/provider name (e.g. github, stripe)" },
    41→          action: { type: "string", description: "The tool action name" },
    42→          params: { type: "object", description: "Parameters for the tool call" },
    43→        },
    44→        required: ["provider", "action"],
    45→      },
    46→    },
    47→    required: ["goal", "reasoning", "action_spec"],
    48→  },
    49→};
    50→
    51→const GET_CONTEXT_TOOL: McpToolDefinition = {
    52→  name: "get_context",
    53→  description:
    54→    "Get project context from the Brain knowledge graph. " +
    55→    "Returns decisions, tasks, observations, and other entities relevant to your current work.",
    56→  inputSchema: {
    57→    type: "object",
    58→    properties: {
    59→      intent: { type: "string", description: "Description of what you are working on" },
    60→    },
    61→    required: ["intent"],
    62→  },
    63→};
    64→
    65→const BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOLS: readonly McpToolDefinition[] = [
    66→  CREATE_INTENT_TOOL,
    67→  GET_CONTEXT_TOOL,
    68→];
    69→
    70→/** Tool names that are always available without intent authorization. */
    71→export const BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(
    72→  BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOLS.map((t) => t.name),
    73→);
    74→
    75→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76→// Pure function: buildToolsList
    77→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→
    79→/**
    80→ * Build a description for a gated tool that includes escalation instructions
    81→ * directing the agent to call create_intent.
    82→ */
    83→function buildGatedToolDescription(
    84→  originalDescription: string,
    85→  toolkit: string,
    86→  toolName: string,
    87→): string {
    88→  return (
    89→    `[GATED] This tool requires an approved intent. ` +
    90→    `Call create_intent with provider="${toolkit}" and action="${toolName}" to request authorization.\n\n` +
    91→    originalDescription
    92→  );
    93→}
    94→
    95→/** Convert a classified tool to an MCP tool definition. */
    96→function classifiedToolToDefinition(classified: ClassifiedTool): McpToolDefinition {
    97→  const { tool, classification } = classified;
    98→
    99→  switch (classification.kind) {
   100→    case "authorized":
   101→      return {
   102→        name: tool.name,
   103→        description: tool.description,
   104→        inputSchema: tool.input_schema,
   105→      };
   106→    case "gated":
   107→      return {
   108→        name: tool.name,
   109→        description: buildGatedToolDescription(tool.description, tool.toolkit, tool.name),
   110→        inputSchema: tool.input_schema,
   111→      };
   112→    case "brain_native":
   113→      // Brain-native tools from the classified list are replaced by canonical definitions
   114→      return BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOLS.find((t) => t.name === tool.name) ?? {
   115→        name: tool.name,
   116→        description: tool.description,
   117→        inputSchema: tool.input_schema,
   118→      };
   119→  }
   120→}
   121→
   122→/**
   123→ * Build the MCP ListToolsResult from classified tools.
   124→ *
   125→ * Pipeline:
   126→ *   classifiedTools -> filter out brain_native -> map to definitions -> append brain-native tools
   127→ */
   128→export function buildToolsList(
   129→  classifiedTools: readonly ClassifiedTool[],
   130→): ListToolsResult {
   131→  // Convert granted tools (authorized + gated), excluding brain_native from the classified set
   132→  // because we append canonical brain-native definitions separately
   133→  const grantedToolDefinitions = classifiedTools
   134→    .filter((ct) => ct.classification.kind !== "brain_native")
   135→    .map(classifiedToolToDefinition);
   136→
   137→  return {
   138→    tools: [...grantedToolDefinitions, ...BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOLS],
   139→  };
   140→}
   141→

     1→/**
     2→ * Scope Engine — Pure functions for computing effective tool scope
     3→ * from intent authorization details and classifying tools.
     4→ *
     5→ * Pure core: no IO, no DB, no side effects.
     6→ */
     7→import type { BrainAction } from "../oauth/types";
     8→import type { ResolvedTool } from "../proxy/tool-injector";
     9→
    10→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11→// Types
    12→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→
    14→export type AuthorizedIntentSummary = {
    15→  readonly intentId: string;
    16→  readonly authorizationDetails: readonly BrainAction[];
    17→};
    18→
    19→export type ToolClassification =
    20→  | { readonly kind: "authorized"; readonly matchingIntent: AuthorizedIntentSummary }
    21→  | { readonly kind: "gated" }
    22→  | { readonly kind: "brain_native" };
    23→
    24→export type EffectiveScope = {
    25→  readonly authorizedActions: readonly BrainAction[];
    26→  readonly intents: readonly AuthorizedIntentSummary[];
    27→};
    28→
    29→export type ClassifiedTool = {
    30→  readonly tool: ResolvedTool;
    31→  readonly classification: ToolClassification;
    32→};
    33→
    34→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35→// Pure functions
    36→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    37→
    38→/** Unions authorization_details from multiple intents into a flat EffectiveScope. */
    39→export function computeEffectiveScope(
    40→  intents: readonly AuthorizedIntentSummary[],
    41→): EffectiveScope {
    42→  const authorizedActions = intents.flatMap(
    43→    (intent) => intent.authorizationDetails,
    44→  );
    45→  return { authorizedActions, intents };
    46→}
    47→
    48→/**
    49→ * Build the resource string used for matching actions to tools.
    50→ * Format: `mcp_tool:{toolkit}:{toolName}`
    51→ */
    52→function buildToolResource(toolkit: string, toolName: string): string {
    53→  return `mcp_tool:${toolkit}:${toolName}`;
    54→}
    55→
    56→/** Find the first intent whose authorization_details contain a matching resource. */
    57→function findMatchingIntent(
    58→  toolResource: string,
    59→  effectiveScope: EffectiveScope,
    60→): AuthorizedIntentSummary | undefined {
    61→  return effectiveScope.intents.find((intent) =>
    62→    intent.authorizationDetails.some(
    63→      (action) => action.resource === toolResource,
    64→    ),
    65→  );
    66→}
    67→
    68→/** Classify a single tool against the effective scope and brain-native set. */
    69→function classifyTool(
    70→  tool: ResolvedTool,
    71→  effectiveScope: EffectiveScope,
    72→  brainNativeToolNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
    73→): ToolClassification {
    74→  if (brainNativeToolNames.has(tool.name)) {
    75→    return { kind: "brain_native" };
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  const toolResource = buildToolResource(tool.toolkit, tool.name);
    79→  const matchingIntent = findMatchingIntent(toolResource, effectiveScope);
    80→
    81→  if (matchingIntent) {
    82→    return { kind: "authorized", matchingIntent };
    83→  }
    84→
    85→  return { kind: "gated" };
    86→}
    87→
    88→/** Classifies each granted tool as authorized, gated, or brain_native. */
    89→export function classifyTools(
    90→  grantedTools: readonly ResolvedTool[],
    91→  effectiveScope: EffectiveScope,
    92→  brainNativeToolNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
    93→): readonly ClassifiedTool[] {
    94→  return grantedTools.map((tool) => ({
    95→    tool,
    96→    classification: classifyTool(tool, effectiveScope, brainNativeToolNames),
    97→  }));
    98→}
    99→
```

     1→/**
     2→ * Tools Call Handler -- Effect boundary for tools/call dispatch
     3→ *
     4→ * Receives an MCP tool call request, verifies scope against
     5→ * classified tools, forwards authorized calls to upstream MCP,
     6→ * and records trace records for every outcome.
     7→ *
     8→ * Effect boundary: performs IO (upstream MCP calls, SurrealDB trace writes).
     9→ * Pure logic delegated to scope-engine.ts and error-response-builder.ts.
    10→ */
    11→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
    12→import type { Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    13→import type { McpClientFactory } from "../tool-registry/mcp-client";
    14→import type { ClassifiedTool } from "./scope-engine";
    15→import { buildIntentRequiredError } from "./error-response-builder";
    16→import { handleCreateIntent } from "./create-intent-handler";
    17→import type { InflightTracker } from "../runtime/types";
    18→import { log } from "../telemetry/logger";
    19→
    20→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    21→// Types
    22→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23→
    24→export type ToolCallOutcome = "success" | "error" | "intent_required" | "unknown_tool";
    25→
    26→export type ToolCallParams = {
    27→  readonly name: string;
    28→  readonly arguments?: Record<string, unknown>;
    29→};
    30→
    31→export type ToolCallSuccess = {
    32→  readonly kind: "success";
    33→  readonly result: unknown;
    34→};
    35→
    36→export type ToolCallError = {
    37→  readonly kind: "error";
    38→  readonly code: number;
    39→  readonly message: string;
    40→  readonly data?: unknown;
    41→};
    42→
    43→export type ToolCallResult = ToolCallSuccess | ToolCallError;
    44→
    45→export type ToolCallContext = {
    46→  readonly workspaceId: string;
    47→  readonly identityId: string;
    48→  readonly sessionId: string;
    49→};
    50→
    51→export type ToolCallDependencies = {
    52→  readonly surreal: Surreal;
    53→  readonly mcpClientFactory: McpClientFactory;
    54→  readonly inflight: InflightTracker;
    55→};
    56→
    57→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    58→// Trace recording (effect boundary)
    59→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→
    61→async function recordToolTrace(
    62→  surreal: Surreal,
    63→  context: ToolCallContext,
    64→  toolName: string,
    65→  outcome: ToolCallOutcome,
    66→  durationMs: number,
    67→  intentId?: string,
    68→  input?: Record<string, unknown>,
    69→  output?: Record<string, unknown>,
    70→): Promise<void> {
    71→  const traceId = crypto.randomUUID();
    72→  const traceRecord = new RecordId("trace", traceId);
    73→
    74→  const content: Record<string, unknown> = {
    75→    type: "tool_call",
    76→    tool_name: toolName,
    77→    outcome,
    78→    workspace: new RecordId("workspace", context.workspaceId),
    79→    actor: new RecordId("identity", context.identityId),
    80→    session: new RecordId("agent_session", context.sessionId),
    81→    duration_ms: Math.round(durationMs),
    82→    created_at: new Date(),
    83→  };
    84→
    85→  if (intentId) {
    86→    content.intent = new RecordId("intent", intentId);
    87→  }
    88→
    89→  if (input) {
    90→    content.input = input;
    91→  }
    92→
    93→  if (output) {
    94→    content.output = output;
    95→  }
    96→
    97→  try {
    98→    await surreal.query(`CREATE $trace CONTENT $content;`, {
    99→      trace: traceRecord,
   100→      content,
   101→    });
   102→
   103→    log.info("agent_mcp.tool_trace.captured", "Tool call trace captured", {
   104→      trace_id: traceId,
   105→      tool_name: toolName,
   106→      outcome,
   107→      duration_ms: Math.round(durationMs),
   108→      workspace_id: context.workspaceId,
   109→    });
   110→  } catch (error) {
   111→    log.error("agent_mcp.tool_trace.write_failed", "Failed to write tool trace", error);
   112→  }
   113→}
   114→
   115→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→// Upstream forwarding (effect boundary)
   117→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→
   119→async function forwardToUpstream(
   120→  mcpClientFactory: McpClientFactory,
   121→  serverUrl: string,
   122→  toolName: string,
   123→  toolArgs: Record<string, unknown>,
   124→): Promise<unknown> {
   125→  // The mcpClientFactory may be a real factory or a test mock (cast as never).
   126→  // Real factory: connect() returns McpConnectionResult with .client, separate callTool/disconnect.
   127→  // Test mock: connect() returns {callTool(name, args), close()} directly.
   128→  // We handle both patterns via duck-typing since the mock is cast as `never`.
   129→  const connection = await mcpClientFactory.connect(serverUrl, "streamable-http");
   130→
   131→  try {
   132→    if (typeof mcpClientFactory.callTool === "function") {
   133→      // Real McpClientFactory: use factory methods with the client object
   134→      const result = await mcpClientFactory.callTool(
   135→        (connection as { client: unknown }).client as never,
   136→        toolName,
   137→        toolArgs,
   138→      );
   139→      return result;
   140→    }
   141→    // Mock/simplified path: connection itself has callTool
   142→    return await (connection as unknown as { callTool: (name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<unknown> }).callTool(
   143→      toolName,
   144→      toolArgs,
   145→    );
   146→  } finally {
   147→    if (typeof mcpClientFactory.disconnect === "function") {
   148→      await mcpClientFactory.disconnect(
   149→        (connection as { client: unknown }).client as never,
   150→      ).catch(() => {});
   151→    } else if (typeof (connection as unknown as { close?: () => Promise<void> }).close === "function") {
   152→      await (connection as unknown as { close: () => Promise<void> }).close().catch(() => {});
   153→    }
   154→  }
   155→}
   156→
   157→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   158→// Tool lookup (pure)
   159→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   160→
   161→function findClassifiedTool(
   162→  classifiedTools: readonly ClassifiedTool[],
   163→  toolName: string,
   164→): ClassifiedTool | undefined {
   165→  return classifiedTools.find((ct) => ct.tool.name === toolName);
   166→}
   167→
   168→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   169→// Public API
   170→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   171→
   172→/**
   173→ * Handle a tools/call JSON-RPC request.
   174→ *
   175→ * Pipeline:
   176→ *   1. Look up tool in classified list
   177→ *   2. Branch on classification: authorized -> forward, gated -> 403, brain_native -> 501
   178→ *   3. Record trace for ALL outcomes
   179→ *   4. Return result or error
   180→ */
   181→export async function handleToolCall(
   182→  params: ToolCallParams,
   183→  classifiedTools: readonly ClassifiedTool[],
   184→  context: ToolCallContext,
   185→  deps: ToolCallDependencies,
   186→): Promise<ToolCallResult> {
   187→  const startTime = performance.now();
   188→  const toolName = params.name;
   189→  const toolArgs = params.arguments ?? {};
   190→
   191→  // 1. Look up the tool
   192→  const classified = findClassifiedTool(classifiedTools, toolName);
   193→
   194→  if (!classified) {
   195→    const durationMs = performance.now() - startTime;
   196→    deps.inflight.track(
   197→      recordToolTrace(deps.surreal, context, toolName, "unknown_tool", durationMs),
   198→    );
   199→    return {
   200→      kind: "error",
   201→      code: -32602,
   202→      message: "Unknown tool",
   203→    };
   204→  }
   205→
   206→  // 2. Branch on classification
   207→  switch (classified.classification.kind) {
   208→    case "authorized": {
   209→      const intentId = classified.classification.matchingIntent.intentId;
   210→      try {
   211→        // Resolve upstream server URL from source_server_id
   212→        const serverUrl = classified.tool.source_server_id
   213→          ? await resolveServerUrl(deps.surreal, classified.tool.source_server_id)
   214→          : undefined;
   215→
   216→        const result = await forwardToUpstream(
   217→          deps.mcpClientFactory,
   218→          serverUrl ?? "unknown",
   219→          toolName,
   220→          toolArgs,
   221→        );
   222→
   223→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startTime;
   224→        deps.inflight.track(
   225→          recordToolTrace(
   226→            deps.surreal, context, toolName, "success", durationMs, intentId,
   227→            toolArgs, { outcome: "success" },
   228→          ),
   229→        );
   230→
   231→        return { kind: "success", result };
   232→      } catch (error) {
   233→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startTime;
   234→        const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
   235→        deps.inflight.track(
   236→          recordToolTrace(
   237→            deps.surreal, context, toolName, "error", durationMs, intentId,
   238→            toolArgs, { outcome: "error", error: errorMessage },
   239→          ),
   240→        );
   241→
   242→        return {
   243→          kind: "error",
   244→          code: -32000,
   245→          message: `Tool call failed: ${errorMessage}`,
   246→        };
   247→      }
   248→    }
   249→
   250→    case "gated": {
   251→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startTime;
   252→      const intentRequiredError = buildIntentRequiredError(
   253→        toolName,
   254→        classified.tool.toolkit,
   255→        classified.tool.input_schema,
   256→      );
   257→
   258→      deps.inflight.track(
   259→        recordToolTrace(
   260→          deps.surreal, context, toolName, "intent_required", durationMs,
   261→          undefined, toolArgs, { outcome: "intent_required" },
   262→        ),
   263→      );
   264→
   265→      return {
   266→        kind: "error",
   267→        code: intentRequiredError.code,
   268→        message: intentRequiredError.message,
   269→        data: intentRequiredError.data,
   270→      };
   271→    }
   272→
   273→    case "brain_native": {
   274→      if (toolName === "create_intent") {
   275→        const outcome = await handleCreateIntent(toolArgs, context, deps.surreal);
   276→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startTime;
   277→
   278→        if (outcome.status === "authorized") {
   279→          deps.inflight.track(
   280→            recordToolTrace(
   281→              deps.surreal, context, toolName, "success", durationMs,
   282→              outcome.intentId, toolArgs, { outcome: "success", intent_id: outcome.intentId },
   283→            ),
   284→          );
   285→          return {
   286→            kind: "success",
   287→            result: { status: outcome.status, intentId: outcome.intentId },
   288→          };
   289→        }
   290→
   291→        if (outcome.status === "pending_veto") {
   292→          deps.inflight.track(
   293→            recordToolTrace(
   294→              deps.surreal, context, toolName, "success", durationMs,
   295→              outcome.intentId, toolArgs, { outcome: "pending_veto", intent_id: outcome.intentId },
   296→            ),
   297→          );
   298→          return {
   299→            kind: "success",
   300→            result: { status: outcome.status, intentId: outcome.intentId },
   301→          };
   302→        }
   303→
   304→        if (outcome.status === "vetoed") {
   305→          deps.inflight.track(
   306→            recordToolTrace(
   307→              deps.surreal, context, toolName, "error", durationMs,
   308→              undefined, toolArgs, { outcome: "vetoed", reason: outcome.reason },
   309→            ),
   310→          );
   311→          return {
   312→            kind: "error",
   313→            code: -32000,
   314→            message: outcome.reason,
   315→          };
   316→        }
   317→
   318→        deps.inflight.track(
   319→          recordToolTrace(
   320→            deps.surreal, context, toolName, "error", durationMs,
   321→            undefined, toolArgs, { outcome: "error", reason: outcome.reason },
   322→          ),
   323→        );
   324→        return {
   325→          kind: "error",
   326→          code: -32000,
   327→          message: outcome.reason,
   328→        };
   329→      }
   330→
   331→      // Other brain-native tools (get_context, etc.) not yet implemented
   332→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startTime;
   333→      deps.inflight.track(
   334→        recordToolTrace(deps.surreal, context, toolName, "error", durationMs),
   335→      );
   336→      return {
   337→        kind: "error",
   338→        code: -32601,
   339→        message: `Not implemented: brain-native tool '${toolName}'`,
   340→      };
   341→    }
   342→  }
   343→}
   344→
   345→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   346→// Server URL resolution (effect boundary)
   347→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   348→
   349→async function resolveServerUrl(
   350→  surreal: Surreal,
   351→  sourceServerId: string,
   352→): Promise<string | undefined> {
   353→  const serverRecord = new RecordId("mcp_server", sourceServerId);
   354→  const [rows] = await surreal.query<[Array<{ url: string }>]>(
   355→    `SELECT url FROM $record;`,
   356→    { record: serverRecord },
   357→  );
   358→  return rows?.[0]?.url;
   359→}
   360→
```

     1→/**
     2→ * Agent MCP Route -- Effect boundary for /mcp/agent/:sessionId
     3→ *
     4→ * Handles JSON-RPC requests from sandbox coding agents. Parses the
     5→ * JSON-RPC envelope, resolves auth context, and dispatches to
     6→ * method-specific handlers.
     7→ *
     8→ * Effect boundary: this module performs IO (DB queries, request parsing).
     9→ * Pure logic lives in scope-engine.ts, tools-list-handler.ts, and
    10→ * error-response-builder.ts.
    11→ */
    12→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    13→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
    14→import { HttpError } from "../http/errors";
    15→import { jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
    16→import { resolveAgentSession, type AgentSessionContext } from "./agent-mcp-auth";
    17→import {
    18→  computeEffectiveScope,
    19→  classifyTools,
    20→  type AuthorizedIntentSummary,
    21→  type ClassifiedTool,
    22→} from "./scope-engine";
    23→import { buildToolsList, BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOL_NAMES } from "./tools-list-handler";
    24→import { handleToolCall, type ToolCallParams } from "./tools-call-handler";
    25→import { handleCreateIntent } from "./create-intent-handler";
    26→import {
    27→  resolveToolsForIdentity,
    28→  createQueryGrantedTools,
    29→  createToolResolutionCache,
    30→  type ToolResolutionCache,
    31→  type QueryGrantedTools,
    32→} from "../proxy/tool-resolver";
    33→import type { BrainAction } from "../oauth/types";
    34→
    35→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→// Types
    37→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38→
    39→type JsonRpcRequest = {
    40→  readonly jsonrpc: string;
    41→  readonly id: string | number | null;
    42→  readonly method: string;
    43→  readonly params?: Record<string, unknown>;
    44→};
    45→
    46→type JsonRpcResponse = {
    47→  readonly jsonrpc: "2.0";
    48→  readonly id: string | number | null;
    49→  readonly result?: unknown;
    50→  readonly error?: {
    51→    readonly code: number;
    52→    readonly message: string;
    53→    readonly data?: unknown;
    54→  };
    55→};
    56→
    57→type IntentRow = {
    58→  id: RecordId<"intent">;
    59→  authorization_details?: readonly BrainAction[];
    60→};
    61→
    62→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→// JSON-RPC helpers (pure)
    64→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65→
    66→function jsonRpcSuccess(id: string | number | null, result: unknown): JsonRpcResponse {
    67→  return { jsonrpc: "2.0", id, result };
    68→}
    69→
    70→function jsonRpcError(
    71→  id: string | number | null,
    72→  code: number,
    73→  message: string,
    74→  data?: unknown,
    75→): JsonRpcResponse {
    76→  return { jsonrpc: "2.0", id, error: { code, message, ...(data !== undefined ? { data } : {}) } };
    77→}
    78→
    79→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    80→// Query ports (effect boundary)
    81→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    82→
    83→/**
    84→ * Query authorized intents gating a specific agent session.
    85→ * Returns intent summaries with authorization_details for scope computation.
    86→ */
    87→async function querySessionIntents(
    88→  surreal: Surreal,
    89→  sessionId: string,
    90→): Promise<readonly AuthorizedIntentSummary[]> {
    91→  const sessionRecord = new RecordId("agent_session", sessionId);
    92→
    93→  const [rows] = await surreal.query<[IntentRow[]]>(
    94→    `SELECT id, authorization_details
    95→     FROM intent
    96→     WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE in FROM gates WHERE out = $sess)
    97→       AND status = 'authorized';`,
    98→    { sess: sessionRecord },
    99→  );
   100→
   101→  return (rows ?? []).map((row) => ({
   102→    intentId: row.id.id as string,
   103→    authorizationDetails: row.authorization_details ?? [],
   104→  }));
   105→}
   106→
   107→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   108→// Shared scope computation
   109→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   110→
   111→/**
   112→ * Resolve intents, compute scope, and classify tools for a session.
   113→ * Shared between tools/list and tools/call handlers.
   114→ */
   115→async function resolveClassifiedTools(
   116→  context: AgentSessionContext,
   117→  surreal: Surreal,
   118→  queryTools: QueryGrantedTools,
   119→  toolCache: ToolResolutionCache,
   120→): Promise<readonly ClassifiedTool[]> {
   121→  const intents = await querySessionIntents(surreal, context.sessionId);
   122→  const effectiveScope = computeEffectiveScope(intents);
   123→  const grantedTools = await resolveToolsForIdentity(
   124→    context.identityId,
   125→    context.workspaceId,
   126→    queryTools,
   127→    toolCache,
   128→  );
   129→  return classifyTools(grantedTools, effectiveScope, BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOL_NAMES);
   130→}
   131→
   132→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   133→// Method handlers
   134→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   135→
   136→async function handleToolsList(
   137→  context: AgentSessionContext,
   138→  surreal: Surreal,
   139→  queryTools: QueryGrantedTools,
   140→  toolCache: ToolResolutionCache,
   141→): Promise<unknown> {
   142→  const classifiedTools = await resolveClassifiedTools(context, surreal, queryTools, toolCache);
   143→  return buildToolsList(classifiedTools);
   144→}
   145→
   146→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→// Route Handler Factory
   148→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→
   150→export function createAgentMcpHandler(deps: ServerDependencies) {
   151→  const queryTools = createQueryGrantedTools(deps.surreal);
   152→  const toolCache = createToolResolutionCache(0); // No cache for agent MCP (fresh every request)
   153→
   154→  return async (sessionId: string, request: Request): Promise<Response> => {
   155→    let requestId: string | number | null = null;
   156→
   157→    try {
   158→      // Parse JSON-RPC envelope
   159→      const body = (await request.json()) as JsonRpcRequest;
   160→      requestId = body.id ?? null;
   161→
   162→      if (body.jsonrpc !== "2.0" || !body.method) {
   163→        return jsonResponse(
   164→          jsonRpcError(requestId, -32600, "Invalid Request"),
   165→          400,
   166→        );
   167→      }
   168→
   169→      // Resolve auth context (throws HttpError on failure)
   170→      const context = await resolveAgentSession(request, deps.surreal);
   171→
   172→      // Verify the URL session ID matches the resolved session
   173→      if (context.sessionId !== sessionId) {
   174→        throw new HttpError(404, "Session ID mismatch");
   175→      }
   176→
   177→      // Dispatch by method
   178→      switch (body.method) {
   179→        case "tools/list": {
   180→          const result = await handleToolsList(context, deps.surreal, queryTools, toolCache);
   181→          return jsonResponse(jsonRpcSuccess(requestId, result), 200);
   182→        }
   183→
   184→        case "tools/call": {
   185→          const toolName = (body.params?.name as string) ?? "";
   186→          const toolArgs = body.params?.arguments as Record<string, unknown> ?? {};
   187→
   188→          // Brain-native tools are handled directly, not via classified tool lookup
   189→          if (toolName === "create_intent") {
   190→            const outcome = await handleCreateIntent(
   191→              toolArgs,
   192→              {
   193→                workspaceId: context.workspaceId,
   194→                identityId: context.identityId,
   195→                sessionId: context.sessionId,
   196→              },
   197→              deps.surreal,
   198→            );
   199→
   200→            if (outcome.status === "authorized") {
   201→              return jsonResponse(
   202→                jsonRpcSuccess(requestId, { status: outcome.status, intentId: outcome.intentId }),
   203→                200,
   204→              );
   205→            }
   206→            if (outcome.status === "pending_veto") {
   207→              return jsonResponse(
   208→                jsonRpcSuccess(requestId, { status: outcome.status, intentId: outcome.intentId }),
   209→                200,
   210→              );
   211→            }
   212→            if (outcome.status === "vetoed") {
   213→              return jsonResponse(
   214→                jsonRpcSuccess(requestId, { status: outcome.status, intentId: outcome.intentId, reason: outcome.reason }),
   215→                200,
   216→              );
   217→            }
   218→            return jsonResponse(
   219→              jsonRpcError(requestId, -32000, outcome.reason),
   220→              500,
   221→            );
   222→          }
   223→
   224→          // Non-brain-native tools: classify and dispatch
   225→          const classifiedTools = await resolveClassifiedTools(
   226→            context, deps.surreal, queryTools, toolCache,
   227→          );
   228→          const callParams: ToolCallParams = {
   229→            name: toolName,
   230→            arguments: body.params?.arguments as Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
   231→          };
   232→          const callResult = await handleToolCall(
   233→            callParams,
   234→            classifiedTools,
   235→            {
   236→              workspaceId: context.workspaceId,
   237→              identityId: context.identityId,
   238→              sessionId: context.sessionId,
   239→            },
   240→            {
   241→              surreal: deps.surreal,
   242→              mcpClientFactory: deps.mcpClientFactory,
   243→              inflight: deps.inflight,
   244→            },
   245→          );
   246→
   247→          if (callResult.kind === "success") {
   248→            return jsonResponse(jsonRpcSuccess(requestId, callResult.result), 200);
   249→          }
   250→          // Error response: use appropriate HTTP status
   251→          const httpStatus = callResult.code === -32403 ? 403
   252→            : callResult.code === -32602 ? 400
   253→            : callResult.code === -32601 ? 501
   254→            : 500;
   255→          return jsonResponse(
   256→            jsonRpcError(requestId, callResult.code, callResult.message, callResult.data),
   257→            httpStatus,
   258→          );
   259→        }
   260→
   261→        case "create_intent": {
   262→          // Also handle create_intent as a top-level JSON-RPC method
   263→          const intentArgs = body.params ?? {};
   264→          const outcome = await handleCreateIntent(
   265→            intentArgs,
   266→            {
   267→              workspaceId: context.workspaceId,
   268→              identityId: context.identityId,
   269→              sessionId: context.sessionId,
   270→            },
   271→            deps.surreal,
   272→          );
   273→
   274→          if (outcome.status === "authorized") {
   275→            return jsonResponse(
   276→              jsonRpcSuccess(requestId, { status: outcome.status, intentId: outcome.intentId }),
   277→              200,
   278→            );
   279→          }
   280→          if (outcome.status === "pending_veto") {
   281→            return jsonResponse(
   282→              jsonRpcSuccess(requestId, { status: outcome.status, intentId: outcome.intentId }),
   283→              200,
   284→            );
   285→          }
   286→          if (outcome.status === "vetoed") {
   287→            return jsonResponse(
   288→              jsonRpcSuccess(requestId, { status: outcome.status, intentId: outcome.intentId, reason: outcome.reason }),
   289→              200,
   290→            );
   291→          }
   292→          return jsonResponse(
   293→            jsonRpcError(requestId, -32000, outcome.reason),
   294→            500,
   295→          );
   296→        }
   297→
   298→        default:
   299→          return jsonResponse(
   300→            jsonRpcError(requestId, -32601, "Method not found"),
   301→            404,
   302→          );
   303→      }
   304→    } catch (error) {
   305→      if (error instanceof HttpError) {
   306→        return jsonResponse(
   307→          jsonRpcError(requestId, -32000, error.message),
   308→          error.status,
   309→        );
   310→      }
   311→      throw error;
   312→    }
   313→  };
   314→}
   315→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
     5→import {
     6→  listEntityNeighbors,
     7→  parseRecordIdString,
     8→  type GraphEntityTable,
     9→  type SearchEntityKind,
    10→} from "../graph/queries";
    11→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    12→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    13→
    14→/** Core search logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */
    15→export async function executeSearchEntities(
    16→  surreal: Surreal,
    17→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    18→  input: { query: string; kinds?: SearchEntityKind[]; limit: number },
    19→) {
    20→  const results = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    21→    surreal,
    22→    workspaceRecord,
    23→    query: input.query,
    24→    ...(input.kinds ? { kinds: input.kinds } : {}),
    25→    limit: input.limit,
    26→  });
    27→
    28→  const enriched = await Promise.all(
    29→    results.map(async (row) => {
    30→      const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(row.id, [row.kind as GraphEntityTable], row.kind as GraphEntityTable);
    31→      const neighbors = await listEntityNeighbors({
    32→        surreal,
    33→        workspaceRecord,
    34→        entityRecord,
    35→        limit: 8,
    36→      });
    37→
    38→      return {
    39→        id: `${row.kind}:${row.id}`,
    40→        kind: row.kind,
    41→        name: row.name,
    42→        confidence: Number(row.score.toFixed(4)),
    43→        ...(row.status ? { status: row.status } : {}),
    44→        related: neighbors.slice(0, 4).map((neighbor) => ({
    45→          id: `${neighbor.kind}:${neighbor.id}`,
    46→          kind: neighbor.kind,
    47→          name: neighbor.name,
    48→          relation: neighbor.relationKind,
    49→        })),
    50→      };
    51→    }),
    52→  );
    53→
    54→  return { query: input.query, results: enriched };
    55→}
    56→
    57→export function createSearchEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    58→  return tool({
    59→    description:
    60→      "Full-text search across the knowledge graph. Use for finding entities by keyword or topic (e.g. \"authentication decisions\", \"payment tasks\"). For listing entities by kind or status, use list_workspace_entities instead.",
    61→    inputSchema: z.object({
    62→      query: z.string().min(1).describe("Search query (keywords matched via BM25 full-text search)"),
    63→      kinds: z.array(z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "suggestion"]))
    64→        .optional()
    65→        .describe("Optional filter by entity kinds"),
    66→      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(25).default(10).describe("Maximum number of results"),
    67→    }),
    68→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    69→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    70→      return executeSearchEntities(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, {
    71→        query: input.query,
    72→        kinds: input.kinds as SearchEntityKind[] | undefined,
    73→        limit: input.limit,
    74→      });
    75→    },
    76→  });
    77→}
    78→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     5→import { isEntityInWorkspace, parseRecordIdString } from "../graph/queries";
     6→import { createObservation } from "../observation/queries";
     7→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
     8→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     9→
    10→export function createCreateObservationTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    11→  return tool({
    12→    description:
    13→      "Create an observation in the workspace graph. Use proactively when you notice cross-cutting concerns — risks, conflicts, gaps, or notable facts — even if the user didn't ask.",
    14→    inputSchema: z.object({
    15→      text: z.string().min(1).describe("Observation text"),
    16→      severity: z
    17→        .enum(["info", "warning", "conflict"])
    18→        .describe(
    19→          "info: awareness-level signals. warning: risks to address soon. conflict: contradictions needing human resolution.",
    20→        ),
    21→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional observation category"),
    22→      related_entity_id: z
    23→        .string()
    24→        .optional()
    25→        .describe("Optional related entity id (project/feature/task/decision/question)"),
    26→    }),
    27→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    28→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_observation", deps);
    29→
    30→      const relatedRecord = input.related_entity_id
    31→        ? parseRecordIdString(
    32→            input.related_entity_id,
    33→            ["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"],
    34→          ) as RecordId<"project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question", string>
    35→        : undefined;
    36→
    37→      if (relatedRecord) {
    38→        const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, relatedRecord);
    39→        if (!scoped) {
    40→          throw new Error("related entity is outside the current workspace scope");
    41→        }
    42→      }
    43→
    44→      const observationRecord = await createObservation({
    45→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    46→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    47→        text: input.text,
    48→        severity: input.severity,
    49→        ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
    50→        sourceAgent: context.actor,
    51→        now: new Date(),
    52→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    53→        ...(relatedRecord ? { relatedRecords: [relatedRecord] } : {}),
    54→      });
    55→
    56→      return {
    57→        observation_id: `observation:${observationRecord.id as string}`,
    58→        severity: input.severity,
    59→        status: "open",
    60→      };
    61→    },
    62→  });
    63→}
    64→
```
