     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import {
     5→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
     6→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
     7→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     9→} from "../graph/queries";
    10→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../observation/queries";
    11→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    12→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    13→
    14→const entityKindEnum = z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"]);
    15→
    16→/** Core logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */
    17→export async function executeListWorkspaceEntities(
    18→  surreal: Surreal,
    19→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    20→  input: { kind: string; status?: string; project?: string; limit: number },
    21→) {
    22→  const projectRecord = input.project
    23→    ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord, projectInput: input.project })
    24→    : undefined;
    25→
    26→  if (input.kind === "project") {
    27→    const projects = await listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });
    28→    return {
    29→      kind: "project",
    30→      count: projects.length,
    31→      entities: projects.map((p) => ({ id: `project:${p.id}`, name: p.name, activeTaskCount: p.activeTaskCount })),
    32→    };
    33→  }
    34→
    35→  if (input.kind === "task") {
    36→    return listTasks(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);
    37→  }
    38→
    39→  if (input.kind === "feature") {
    40→    return listFeatures(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  if (input.kind === "decision") {
    44→    const decisions = await listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });
    45→    const filtered = input.status ? decisions.filter((d) => d.status === input.status) : decisions;
    46→    return {
    47→      kind: "decision",
    48→      count: filtered.length,
    49→      entities: filtered.map((d) => ({
    50→        id: `decision:${d.id}`, name: d.name, status: d.status,
    51→        ...(d.priority ? { priority: d.priority } : {}),
    52→        ...(d.project ? { project: d.project } : {}),
    53→      })),
    54→    };
    55→  }
    56→
    57→  if (input.kind === "question") {
    58→    const questions = await listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });
    59→    return {
    60→      kind: "question",
    61→      count: questions.length,
    62→      entities: questions.map((q) => ({
    63→        id: `question:${q.id}`, name: q.name,
    64→        ...(q.priority ? { priority: q.priority } : {}),
    65→        ...(q.project ? { project: q.project } : {}),
    66→      })),
    67→    };
    68→  }
    69→
    70→  if (input.kind === "observation") {
    71→    const observations = await listWorkspaceOpenObservations({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });
    72→    return {
    73→      kind: "observation",
    74→      count: observations.length,
    75→      entities: observations.map((o) => ({
    76→        id: `observation:${o.id}`, text: o.text, severity: o.severity, status: o.status,
    77→        ...(o.category ? { category: o.category } : {}),
    78→        sourceAgent: o.sourceAgent,
    79→      })),
    80→    };
    81→  }
    82→
    83→  throw new Error(`unsupported entity kind: ${input.kind}`);
    84→}
    85→
    86→export function createListWorkspaceEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    87→  return tool({
    88→    description:
    89→      "List workspace entities by kind. Use this to answer questions about what entities exist (e.g. \"what decisions are there?\", \"list open questions\", \"show all tasks\"). For semantic search by topic, use search_entities instead.",
    90→    inputSchema: z.object({
    91→      kind: entityKindEnum.describe("Entity kind to list."),
    92→      status: z.string().optional().describe("Optional status filter, e.g. 'provisional', 'confirmed', 'open', 'done'."),
    93→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project name or ID to scope results. Only use when the user explicitly mentions a project."),
    94→      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).default(25).describe("Maximum number of results."),
    95→    }),
    96→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    97→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    98→      return executeListWorkspaceEntities(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, {
    99→        kind: input.kind,
   100→        status: input.status,
   101→        project: input.project,
   102→        limit: input.limit,
   103→      });
   104→    },
   105→  });
   106→}
   107→
   108→async function listTasks(
   109→  surreal: Surreal,
   110→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   111→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string> | undefined,
   112→  status: string | undefined,
   113→  limit: number,
   114→) {
   115→  const conditions = ["workspace = $workspace"];
   116→  const params: Record<string, unknown> = { workspace: workspaceRecord, limit };
   117→
   118→  if (projectRecord) {
   119→    conditions.push(
   120→      "(id IN (SELECT VALUE `in` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project)"
   121→      + " OR id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_task WHERE `in` IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_feature WHERE `in` = $project)))",
   122→    );
   123→    params.project = projectRecord;
   124→  }
   125→
   126→  if (status) {
   127→    conditions.push("status = $status");
   128→    params.status = status;
   129→  }
   130→
   131→  const query = [
   132→    "SELECT id, title, status, priority, created_at",
   133→    "FROM task",
   134→    `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}`,
   135→    "ORDER BY created_at DESC",
   136→    "LIMIT $limit;",
   137→  ].join(" ");
   138→
   139→  const [rows] = await surreal
   140→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task", string>; title: string; status: string; priority?: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>(query, params)
   141→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task", string>; title: string; status: string; priority?: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>();
   142→
   143→  return {
   144→    kind: "task",
   145→    count: rows.length,
   146→    entities: rows.map((t) => ({
   147→      id: `task:${(t.id.id as string)}`,
   148→      name: t.title,
   149→      status: t.status,
   150→      ...(t.priority ? { priority: t.priority } : {}),
   151→    })),
   152→  };
   153→}
   154→
   155→async function listFeatures(
   156→  surreal: Surreal,
   157→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   158→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string> | undefined,
   159→  status: string | undefined,
   160→  limit: number,
   161→) {
   162→  const conditions = ["workspace = $workspace"];
   163→  const params: Record<string, unknown> = { workspace: workspaceRecord, limit };
   164→
   165→  if (projectRecord) {
   166→    conditions.push("id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_feature WHERE `in` = $project)");
   167→    params.project = projectRecord;
   168→  }
   169→
   170→  if (status) {
   171→    conditions.push("status = $status");
   172→    params.status = status;
   173→  }
   174→
   175→  const query = [
   176→    "SELECT id, name, status, created_at",
   177→    "FROM feature",
   178→    `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}`,
   179→    "ORDER BY created_at DESC",
   180→    "LIMIT $limit;",
   181→  ].join(" ");
   182→
   183→  const [rows] = await surreal
   184→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string>; name: string; status: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>(query, params)
   185→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string>; name: string; status: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>();
   186→
   187→  return {
   188→    kind: "feature",
   189→    count: rows.length,
   190→    entities: rows.map((f) => ({
   191→      id: `feature:${(f.id.id as string)}`,
   192→      name: f.name,
   193→      status: f.status,
   194→    })),
   195→  };
   196→}
   197→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { getEntityDetail, parseRecordIdString, type GraphEntityTable } from "../graph/queries";
     5→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     6→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     7→
     8→/** Core logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */
     9→export async function executeGetEntityDetail(
    10→  surreal: Surreal,
    11→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    12→  entityId: string,
    13→) {
    14→  const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(
    15→    entityId,
    16→    ["workspace", "project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"],
    17→  ) as unknown as ReturnType<typeof parseRecordIdString<GraphEntityTable>>;
    18→
    19→  return getEntityDetail({ surreal, workspaceRecord, entityRecord });
    20→}
    21→
    22→export function createGetEntityDetailTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    23→  return tool({
    24→    description:
    25→      "Get full details about a specific entity including relationships, provenance, and related entities.",
    26→    inputSchema: z.object({
    27→      entityId: z.string().min(1).describe("Entity record ID, e.g. decision:abc123"),
    28→    }),
    29→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    30→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    31→      return executeGetEntityDetail(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, input.entityId);
    32→    },
    33→  });
    34→}
    35→

     1→import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { tool } from "ai";
     4→import { z } from "zod";
     5→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     6→import {
     7→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     8→  createProjectRecord,
     9→  resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord,
    10→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
    11→} from "../graph/queries";
    12→import { seedDescriptionEntry } from "../descriptions/persist";
    13→import { fireDescriptionUpdates } from "../descriptions/triggers";
    14→import { ensureProjectFeatureEdge } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    15→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    16→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    17→import { log } from "../telemetry/logger";
    18→
    19→export function createCreateWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    20→  return tool({
    21→    description:
    22→      "Create a task, feature, or project directly in the knowledge graph. Use when the user explicitly requests creation (\"add a task for X\") or during onboarding entity seeding. For uncertain/brainstormed items, prefer suggest_work_items instead.",
    23→    inputSchema: z.object({
    24→      kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe(
    25→        "project: named product area or workstream (MUST create before features/tasks can belong to it). feature: capability within an existing project. task: concrete executable work with action verb. If no projects exist yet, create a project first.",
    26→      ),
    27→      title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise entity title"),
    28→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this entity is needed — seeds the description"),
    29→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Category classification"),
    30→      priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important, needs attention soon. medium: normal priority. low: nice-to-have, deferred."),
    31→      project: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("Project name to scope the entity under"),
    32→      feature: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("Feature name or record id to scope a task under"),
    33→    }),
    34→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    35→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_task", deps);
    36→      const now = new Date();
    37→
    38→      const entityId = randomUUID();
    39→
    40→      // Hard guard: never create a project with the same name as the workspace
    41→      if (input.kind === "project") {
    42→        const [ws] = await deps.surreal.query<[{ name: string } | undefined]>(
    43→          "SELECT name FROM ONLY $ws;",
    44→          { ws: context.workspaceRecord },
    45→        );
    46→        if (ws && input.title.toLowerCase().trim() === ws.name.toLowerCase().trim()) {
    47→          return {
    48→            error: `"${input.title}" is the workspace name, not a project. Create the user's described items (e.g. Dashboard, Inventory) as projects instead.`,
    49→          };
    50→        }
    51→
    52→        const projectRecord = await createProjectRecord({
    53→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    54→          name: input.title,
    55→          status: "active",
    56→          now,
    57→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    58→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    59→        });
    60→
    61→        await seedDescriptionEntry({
    62→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    63→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    64→          text: input.rationale,
    65→        }).catch(() => undefined);
    66→
    67→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    68→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    69→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    70→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    71→          now,
    72→          confidence: 0.95,
    73→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
    74→          fromText: input.title,
    75→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
    76→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    77→        });
    78→
    79→        return {
    80→          entity_id: `project:${projectRecord.id as string}`,
    81→          kind: "project" as const,
    82→          title: input.title,
    83→        };
    84→      }
    85→
    86→      if (input.kind === "task") {
    87→        const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", entityId);
    88→        await deps.surreal.create(taskRecord).content({
    89→          title: input.title,
    90→          status: "open",
    91→          workspace: context.workspaceRecord,
    92→          ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
    93→          ...(input.priority ? { priority: input.priority } : {}),
    94→          created_at: now,
    95→          updated_at: now,
    96→        });
    97→
    98→        if (input.project) {
    99→          try {
   100→            const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
   101→              surreal: deps.surreal,
   102→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   103→              projectInput: input.project,
   104→            });
   105→            await deps.surreal
   106→              .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {
   107→                added_at: now,
   108→              })
   109→              .output("after");
   110→          } catch (err) {
   111→            log.error("create_work_item", `failed to link task to project "${input.project}"`, err);
   112→          }
   113→        }
   114→
   115→        if (input.feature) {
   116→          try {
   117→            const featureRecord = await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({
   118→              surreal: deps.surreal,
   119→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   120→              featureInput: input.feature,
   121→            });
   122→
   123→            await deps.surreal
   124→              .relate(featureRecord, new RecordId("has_task", randomUUID()), taskRecord, {
   125→                added_at: now,
   126→              })
   127→              .output("after");
   128→
   129→            await deps.surreal
   130→              .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), featureRecord, {
   131→                added_at: now,
   132→              })
   133→              .output("after");
   134→          } catch (err) {
   135→            log.error("create_work_item", `failed to link task to feature "${input.feature}"`, err);
   136→          }
   137→        }
   138→
   139→        await seedDescriptionEntry({
   140→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   141→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   142→          text: input.rationale,
   143→        }).catch(() => undefined);
   144→
   145→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   146→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   147→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   148→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   149→          now,
   150→          confidence: 0.95,
   151→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   152→          fromText: input.title,
   153→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   154→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   155→        });
   156→
   157→        return {
   158→          entity_id: `task:${entityId}`,
   159→          kind: "task" as const,
   160→          title: input.title,
   161→        };
   162→      }
   163→
   164→      // feature
   165→      const featureRecord = new RecordId("feature", entityId);
   166→      await deps.surreal.create(featureRecord).content({
   167→        name: input.title,
   168→        status: "active",
   169→        workspace: context.workspaceRecord,
   170→        ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
   171→        created_at: now,
   172→        updated_at: now,
   173→      });
   174→
   175→      if (input.project) {
   176→        try {
   177→          const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
   178→            surreal: deps.surreal,
   179→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   180→            projectInput: input.project,
   181→          });
   182→          await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(deps.surreal, projectRecord, featureRecord, now);
   183→
   184→          await fireDescriptionUpdates({
   185→            surreal: deps.surreal,
   186→            extractionModel: deps.extractionModel,
   187→            trigger: {
   188→              kind: "feature_created",
   189→              entity: featureRecord,
   190→              summary: `Feature added: ${input.title}`,
   191→            },
   192→          }).catch(() => undefined);
   193→        } catch (err) {
   194→          log.error("create_work_item", `failed to link feature to project "${input.project}"`, err);
   195→        }
   196→      }
   197→
   198→      await seedDescriptionEntry({
   199→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   200→        targetRecord: featureRecord,
   201→        text: input.rationale,
   202→      }).catch(() => undefined);
   203→
   204→      await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   205→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   206→        sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   207→        targetRecord: featureRecord,
   208→        now,

## Implementation Plan: Brain-Native Tools in MCP Agent Endpoint

### Overview

Expose 13 Brain tools directly in the `/mcp/agent/:sessionId` endpoint. Read tools (7) are always available as `brain_native`. Write tools (6) are gated behind intent authorization using the existing `create_intent` flow. Four tools are excluded (subagent invocation, UI rendering, human-only confirmation).

### Tool Classification

**Read tools (brain_native -- always available, no intent required):**
1. `search_entities` -- has `executeSearchEntities`
2. `list_workspace_entities` -- has `executeListWorkspaceEntities`
3. `get_entity_detail` -- has `executeGetEntityDetail`
4. `get_project_status` -- has `executeGetProjectStatus`
5. `get_conversation_history` -- no extracted execute fn, needs extraction
6. `check_constraints` -- has `executeCheckConstraints`
7. `resolve_decision` -- no extracted execute fn, complex (creates records), treat as read

**Write tools (brain_native_gated -- require intent authorization):**
1. `create_provisional_decision`
2. `create_question`
3. `create_observation`
4. `create_suggestion`
5. `create_work_item`
6. `edit_work_item`
7. `acknowledge_observation`
8. `resolve_observation`
9. `suggest_work_items`
10. `move_items_to_project`

**Excluded:**
- `invoke_pm_agent` -- needs `pmAgentModel`
- `invoke_analytics_agent` -- needs `analyticsAgentModel` + `analyticsSurreal`
- `confirm_decision` -- requires `humanPresent` + `chat_agent` actor
- `show_relationship_graph` -- UI component rendering

### Key Design Decisions

**1. New classification kind: `brain_native_gated`**

The scope engine currently has three kinds: `authorized`, `gated`, `brain_native`. We need a fourth kind `brain_native_gated` for write tools that are built into Brain but still need intent authorization. This preserves the existing `gated` kind for external MCP tools.

The resource format for brain-native gated tools will be `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}` (e.g. `mcp_tool:brain:create_observation`), consistent with how `create_intent` derives authorization_details from `action_spec`.

**2. Provenance handling for write tools**

Write tools in the chat agent use `context.currentMessageRecord` and `context.latestUserText` for `createExtractionProvenanceEdge`. In the MCP agent context, there is no message/conversation. Two strategies:

- **For `createExtractionProvenanceEdge` calls**: Skip provenance edges entirely in MCP context. The CLI MCP route (`mcp-route.ts`) already does this -- its write handlers (lines 978-1040 for observations, 621-676 for decisions) call the underlying query functions directly without creating provenance edges. This is the established pattern.
- **For `sourceMessageRecord` in entity creation**: Already optional in `createObservation`, `createDecisionRecord`, `createQuestionRecord`, `createSuggestion`. Omit it.
- **For `sourceAgent`**: Use `"mcp"` as the actor, matching the existing `ChatToolExecutionContext.actor` value for MCP contexts.

**3. Authority checks for write tools**

Write tools currently use `requireAuthorizedContext` which goes through `requireToolContext` (AI SDK `ToolExecutionOptions`). In MCP agent context, we bypass the AI SDK and call the underlying query functions directly -- just like the CLI MCP route does. Authority is enforced by the intent authorization system (policy gate evaluation), not by the IAM authority scopes. The intent flow already handles policy evaluation.

However, we should still run `checkAuthority` for defense-in-depth, using the identity from `AgentSessionContext`. The CLI MCP route shows this pattern (lines 626-628, 983-985).

**4. Handler architecture**

Create a new module `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tools-handler.ts` following the pure/effect boundary split:
- Pure: tool definition registry (descriptions, input schemas as plain JSON Schema)
- Effect: dispatch function that routes tool calls to the appropriate `execute*` function or direct query call

### Step-by-Step Implementation

#### Step 1: Define brain-native tool registry

Create `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tool-definitions.ts` (pure module, no IO).

This module exports:
- `BRAIN_READ_TOOL_DEFINITIONS: readonly McpToolDefinition[]` -- the 7 read tools with name, description, and JSON Schema `inputSchema` (converted from the Zod schemas in each tool file)
- `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_DEFINITIONS: readonly McpToolDefinition[]` -- the 10 write tools
- `BRAIN_NATIVE_READ_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string>` -- for scope engine classification
- `BRAIN_NATIVE_GATED_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string>` -- for scope engine classification

Each definition mirrors the Zod schemas from the tool files but expressed as plain JSON Schema (since this is the MCP protocol format, not AI SDK). The descriptions are copied from each tool's `description` string.

#### Step 2: Extend scope engine with `brain_native_gated` classification

Modify `app/src/server/mcp/scope-engine.ts`:

Add `brain_native_gated` to `ToolClassification`:
```typescript
export type ToolClassification =
  | { readonly kind: "authorized"; readonly matchingIntent: AuthorizedIntentSummary }
  | { readonly kind: "gated" }
  | { readonly kind: "brain_native" }
  | { readonly kind: "brain_native_gated" }
  | { readonly kind: "brain_native_authorized"; readonly matchingIntent: AuthorizedIntentSummary };
```

Wait -- this needs more thought. Brain-native gated tools need to transition from "gated" to "authorized" once an intent is approved. The classification should check whether there's an approved intent for `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}`:

- If a matching intent exists: `brain_native_authorized` (can execute)
- If no matching intent: `brain_native_gated` (show [GATED] description)

Update `classifyTool` to accept two sets: `brainNativeToolNames` (read) and `brainNativeGatedToolNames` (write). For gated tools, check the effective scope for `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}`.

Actually, simpler approach: use the existing `authorized`/`gated` distinction but with a different dispatch path. The key insight is that for brain-native gated tools, we need to:
1. In `tools/list`: show them with `[GATED]` prefix when no intent, show normally when intent exists
2. In `tools/call`: execute them locally (not forward to upstream MCP) when authorized

Let me reconsider. The cleanest approach uses two new classification kinds but keeps the scope-checking logic unified:

```typescript
export type ToolClassification =
  | { readonly kind: "authorized"; readonly matchingIntent: AuthorizedIntentSummary }
  | { readonly kind: "gated" }
  | { readonly kind: "brain_native" }
  | { readonly kind: "brain_native_gated" }
  | { readonly kind: "brain_native_authorized"; readonly matchingIntent: AuthorizedIntentSummary };
```

In `classifyTool`:
- If `brainNativeReadNames.has(tool.name)`: return `brain_native`
- If `brainNativeGatedNames.has(tool.name)`: check scope for `mcp_tool:brain:{tool.name}`. If intent found, return `brain_native_authorized`. Otherwise return `brain_native_gated`.
- Otherwise: existing `authorized`/`gated` logic

But wait -- `classifyTool` currently takes `ResolvedTool` which comes from external MCP tool discovery. Brain-native tools don't exist as `ResolvedTool` objects. The classification happens on a separate path.

Better approach: keep the brain-native tools completely separate from the `classifiedTools` pipeline. They don't come from `resolveToolsForIdentity`. Instead:

1. `tools/list`: append brain read tool definitions (always). Append brain write tool definitions with `[GATED]` prefix when no matching intent, or clean description when intent exists.
2. `tools/call`: check if tool name is in brain-native sets. If read: execute directly. If gated write: check intent, execute if authorized, return intent_required error if not.

This is cleaner because it doesn't pollute the external tool classification pipeline.

#### Step 2 (revised): Extend tools-list-handler

Modify `app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts`:

- Import `BRAIN_READ_TOOL_DEFINITIONS` and `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_DEFINITIONS` from the new definitions module
- Update `BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOL_NAMES` to include all brain-native tool names (read + write + existing `get_context` and `create_intent`)
- Update `buildToolsList` to accept an `authorizedBrainWriteTools: ReadonlySet<string>` parameter
- For each write tool: if name is in `authorizedBrainWriteTools`, include with clean description; otherwise include with `[GATED]` prefix (using `buildGatedToolDescription` with `toolkit="brain"`)
- Read tools always included with clean description

New signature:
```typescript
export function buildToolsList(
  classifiedTools: readonly ClassifiedTool[],
  authorizedBrainWriteTools: ReadonlySet<string>,
): ListToolsResult
```

#### Step 3: Create brain tools dispatch handler

Create `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tools-handler.ts` (effect boundary).

This module exports a `handleBrainToolCall` function:

```typescript
export type BrainToolCallContext = {
  readonly workspaceId: string;
  readonly identityId: string;
  readonly sessionId: string;
  readonly workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace">;
  readonly identityRecord: RecordId<"identity">;
};

export type BrainToolCallDeps = {
  readonly surreal: Surreal;
  readonly inflight: InflightTracker;
  readonly config: { extractionModelId: string };
};

export async function handleBrainToolCall(
  toolName: string,
  args: Record<string, unknown>,
  context: BrainToolCallContext,
  deps: BrainToolCallDeps,
): Promise<ToolCallResult>
```

Dispatch by tool name:

**Read tools** -- call extracted `execute*` functions directly:
- `search_entities` -> `executeSearchEntities(surreal, workspaceRecord, args)`
- `list_workspace_entities` -> `executeListWorkspaceEntities(surreal, workspaceRecord, args)`
- `get_entity_detail` -> `executeGetEntityDetail(surreal, workspaceRecord, args.entityId)`
- `get_project_status` -> `executeGetProjectStatus(surreal, workspaceRecord, args.projectId)`
- `check_constraints` -> `executeCheckConstraints(surreal, workspaceRecord, args.proposed_action)`
- `get_conversation_history` -> extract the query logic inline (BM25 message search + entity enrichment); or better, extract an `executeGetConversationHistory` from the tool file first
- `resolve_decision` -> needs special handling (it creates records). For MCP, use the simpler pattern from CLI MCP route's `handleResolveDecision` (BM25 search + return matches, no record creation). This is what the CLI already does.

**Write tools** -- call underlying query functions directly (pattern from CLI MCP route):
- `create_observation` -> `createObservation({ surreal, workspaceRecord, text, severity, category, sourceAgent: "mcp", now })`. No provenance edge.
- `create_provisional_decision` -> `createDecisionRecord({ surreal, summary, status: "provisional", now, workspaceRecord })`. No provenance edge.
- `create_question` -> `createQuestionRecord({ surreal, text, status: "open", now, workspaceRecord })`. No provenance edge.
- `create_suggestion` -> `createSuggestion({ surreal, workspaceRecord, text, category, rationale, suggestedBy: "mcp", confidence, now })`. No provenance edge.
- `create_work_item` -> Direct SurrealDB create (task/feature/project) + relation edges. Follow the CLI MCP route pattern.
- `edit_work_item` -> Direct `surreal.update().merge()` + workspace scope check.
- `acknowledge_observation` -> `acknowledgeObservation({ surreal, workspaceRecord, observationRecord, now })`
- `resolve_observation` -> `resolveObservation({ surreal, workspaceRecord, observationRecord, now })`
- `suggest_work_items` -> BM25 search for duplicate detection, return triage buckets. No writes.
- `move_items_to_project` -> Direct edge manipulation (delete old belongs_to/has_feature, create new).

For write tools, before executing, verify the tool is authorized by checking the effective scope for `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}`. This is a pure function call to `findMatchingIntent` from scope-engine.

#### Step 4: Extract `executeGetConversationHistory` and `executeResolveDecision`

Two tools lack extracted `execute*` functions. Extract them:

`get-conversation-history.ts`: Extract the BM25 message search + entity enrichment logic into `executeGetConversationHistory(surreal, workspaceRecord, query)`.

`resolve-decision.ts`: For MCP context, use a simplified version that only does BM25 search and returns matches (no record creation, no provenance edges). This matches the CLI MCP route's `handleResolveDecision` pattern. Extract as `executeResolveDecisionReadOnly(surreal, workspaceRecord, question)`.

#### Step 5: Wire into agent-mcp-route

Modify `app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts`:

In the `tools/list` handler:
1. Compute `authorizedBrainWriteTools` from session intents (scan for `mcp_tool:brain:*` resources)
2. Pass to `buildToolsList(classifiedTools, authorizedBrainWriteTools)`

In the `tools/call` handler, before the external tool classification:
1. Check if `toolName` is in brain read tools set -> call `handleBrainToolCall` directly
2. Check if `toolName` is in brain write tools set -> verify intent authorization from scope, then call `handleBrainToolCall`
3. Otherwise fall through to existing external tool dispatch

Update the `create_intent` handler to accept `provider: "brain"` in the action_spec, which produces `mcp_tool:brain:{action}` resources.

#### Step 6: Compute authorized brain write tools from intents

Add a pure helper in `scope-engine.ts` or a new utility:

```typescript
export function computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools(
  effectiveScope: EffectiveScope,
  brainWriteToolNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
): ReadonlySet<string> {
  const authorized = new Set<string>();
  for (const toolName of brainWriteToolNames) {
    const resource = `mcp_tool:brain:${toolName}`;
    if (effectiveScope.authorizedActions.some(a => a.resource === resource)) {
      authorized.add(toolName);
    }
  }
  return authorized;
}
```

#### Step 7: Acceptance tests

Add tests to `tests/acceptance/agent-mcp-governance.test.ts` (or a new file in the same directory):

1. **Read tool always available**: `tools/list` includes brain read tools. `tools/call` for `search_entities` succeeds without any intent.
2. **Write tool gated**: `tools/list` shows write tools with `[GATED]` prefix. `tools/call` for `create_observation` returns intent_required error.
3. **Write tool after intent**: Create intent with `provider: "brain"`, `action: "create_observation"`. Then `tools/call` for `create_observation` succeeds.
4. **Read tool execution**: Call `get_entity_detail` with a seeded task, verify correct result.

### File Change Summary

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tool-definitions.ts` | **NEW** -- tool definitions (pure, JSON Schema) |
| `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tools-handler.ts` | **NEW** -- dispatch handler (effect boundary) |
| `app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts` | Extend `buildToolsList` to include brain tools |
| `app/src/server/mcp/scope-engine.ts` | Add `computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools` helper |
| `app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts` | Wire brain tool dispatch into tools/list and tools/call |
| `app/src/server/tools/get-conversation-history.ts` | Extract `executeGetConversationHistory` |
| `app/src/server/tools/resolve-decision.ts` | Extract `executeResolveDecisionReadOnly` (search-only) |
| `tests/acceptance/agent-mcp-governance.test.ts` | Add brain-native tool test cases |

### Sequencing

1. Extract `executeGetConversationHistory` and `executeResolveDecisionReadOnly` (prerequisite, no behavior change)
2. Create `brain-tool-definitions.ts` (pure, testable independently)
3. Add `computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools` to scope engine (pure, testable)
4. Update `tools-list-handler.ts` to accept and render brain tools
5. Create `brain-tools-handler.ts` with dispatch logic
6. Wire everything in `agent-mcp-route.ts`
7. Write acceptance tests

### Risks and Mitigations

**Risk**: Write tools skip provenance edges, losing traceability.
**Mitigation**: Tool call traces are already recorded by `recordToolTrace` in `tools-call-handler.ts`. The trace records input/output, tool name, workspace, and session. This provides audit traceability even without extraction provenance edges. Provenance edges are a chat-agent concern (linking messages to entities), not relevant for MCP tool calls.

**Risk**: `resolve_decision` in MCP is read-only but the chat version creates inferred decisions.
**Mitigation**: The CLI MCP route already uses the read-only pattern. Document this difference. If agents need to create decisions, they use `create_provisional_decision` explicitly.

**Risk**: Write tools that use `extractionModel`/`extractionModelId` for provenance edges.
**Mitigation**: Since we skip provenance edges in MCP context, `extractionModelId` is only needed for `config` field logging. Pass `deps.config.extractionModel` for trace metadata but don't create provenance records.

- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts` - Main wiring point: add brain tool dispatch to tools/list and tools/call handlers
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts` - Extend buildToolsList to render brain read + gated write tool definitions
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/tools-call-handler.ts` - Add brain_native dispatch path alongside existing authorized/gated/brain_native cases
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/tools/search-entities.ts` - Reference pattern for extracted execute* functions that brain-tools-handler will call
- `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/mcp-route.ts` - Reference pattern for MCP-context writes (no provenance edges, direct query function calls)

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import {
     4→  parseRecordIdString,
     5→  readEntityName,
     6→  type GraphEntityRecord,
     7→} from "../graph/queries";
     8→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     9→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    10→import type { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
    11→
    12→type RankedMessage = {
    13→  id: string;
    14→  conversationId: string;
    15→  text: string;
    16→  score: number;
    17→};
    18→
    19→export function createGetConversationHistoryTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    20→  return tool({
    21→    description:
    22→      "Search past conversations for discussions about a topic and return relevant message excerpts with linked entities.",
    23→    inputSchema: z.object({
    24→      query: z.string().min(1).describe("Topic to search for"),
    25→      projectId: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope filter"),
    26→    }),
    27→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    28→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    29→
    30→      const trimmed = input.query.trim();
    31→      if (trimmed.length === 0) return { query: input.query, results: [] };
    32→
    33→      const [messageRows] = await deps.surreal
    34→        .query<[Array<{
    35→          id: RecordId<"message", string>;
    36→          conversation: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    37→          text: string;
    38→          score: number;
    39→        }>]>(
    40→          `SELECT id, conversation, text, search::score(1) AS score
    41→           FROM message
    42→           WHERE text @1@ $query
    43→           AND conversation IN (SELECT VALUE id FROM conversation WHERE workspace = $workspace)
    44→           ORDER BY score DESC
    45→           LIMIT 8;`,
    46→          { workspace: context.workspaceRecord, query: trimmed },
    47→        )
    48→        .collect<[Array<{
    49→          id: RecordId<"message", string>;
    50→          conversation: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    51→          text: string;
    52→          score: number;
    53→        }>]>();
    54→
    55→      const messages: RankedMessage[] = (messageRows ?? []).map((row) => ({
    56→        id: row.id.id as string,
    57→        conversationId: row.conversation.id as string,
    58→        text: row.text,
    59→        score: row.score,
    60→      }));
    61→
    62→      const enrichedMessages = await Promise.all(
    63→        messages.map(async (message) => {
    64→          const messageRecord = parseRecordIdString(message.id, ["message"], "message");
    65→          const [entityRows] = await deps.surreal
    66→            .query<[Array<GraphEntityRecord>]>(
    67→              "SELECT VALUE out FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` = $message LIMIT 8;",
    68→              { message: messageRecord },
    69→            )
    70→            .collect<[Array<GraphEntityRecord>]>() ;
    71→
    72→          const linkedEntities = await Promise.all(
    73→            entityRows.map(async (entityRecord) => {
    74→              const table = entityRecord.table.name;
    75→              if (
    76→                table !== "workspace" &&
    77→                table !== "project" &&
    78→                table !== "person" &&
    79→                table !== "feature" &&
    80→                table !== "task" &&
    81→                table !== "decision" &&
    82→                table !== "question"
    83→              ) {
    84→                return undefined;
    85→              }
    86→
    87→              const name = await readEntityName(deps.surreal, entityRecord);
    88→              if (!name) {
    89→                return undefined;
    90→              }
    91→
    92→              return {
    93→                id: `${table}:${entityRecord.id as string}`,
    94→                kind: table,
    95→                name,
    96→              };
    97→            }),
    98→          );
    99→
   100→          return {
   101→            messageId: `message:${message.id}`,
   102→            conversationId: `conversation:${message.conversationId}`,
   103→            excerpt: message.text.slice(0, 280),
   104→            confidence: Number(message.score.toFixed(4)),
   105→            linkedEntities: linkedEntities.filter((value) => value !== undefined),
   106→          };
   107→        }),
   108→      );
   109→
   110→      return {
   111→        query: input.query,
   112→        results: enrichedMessages,
   113→      };
   114→    },
   115→  });
   116→}
   117→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import {
     4→  createDecisionRecord,
     5→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     6→  getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage,
     7→  parseRecordIdString,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord,
     9→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
    10→  type GraphEntityRecord,
    11→} from "../graph/queries";
    12→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
    13→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    14→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    15→
    16→const resolveDecisionInputSchema = z.object({
    17→  question: z.string().min(1).describe("The decision question"),
    18→  options: z.array(z.string().min(1)).optional().describe("Known options being considered"),
    19→  context: z
    20→    .object({
    21→      project: z.string().optional(),
    22→      feature: z.string().optional(),
    23→    })
    24→    .optional()
    25→    .describe("Optional project/feature scope for searching decisions"),
    26→});
    27→
    28→export function createResolveDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    29→  return tool({
    30→    description:
    31→      "Infer an answer to a decision question from graph context. Returns resolved/inferred/unresolved with rationale and sources.",
    32→    inputSchema: resolveDecisionInputSchema,
    33→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    34→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_decision", deps);
    35→
    36→      const projectRecord = input.context?.project
    37→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    38→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    39→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    40→            projectInput: input.context.project,
    41→          })
    42→        : undefined;
    43→
    44→      const featureRecord = input.context?.feature
    45→        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({
    46→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    47→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    48→            featureInput: input.context.feature,
    49→          })
    50→        : undefined;
    51→
    52→      const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    53→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    54→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    55→        query: input.question,
    56→        kinds: ["decision"],
    57→        limit: 10,
    58→      });
    59→
    60→      const decisionCandidates = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === "decision");
    61→      const topDecision = decisionCandidates[0];
    62→
    63→      if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.92) {
    64→        const existingDecision = parseRecordIdString(topDecision.id, ["decision"], "decision");
    65→        const resolvedFrom = await getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage({
    66→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    67→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    68→          decisionRecord: existingDecision,
    69→        });
    70→
    71→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    72→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    73→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    74→          targetRecord: existingDecision,
    75→          now: new Date(),
    76→          confidence: Math.max(topDecision.score, 0.6),
    77→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
    78→          fromText: input.question,
    79→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
    80→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    81→          ...(resolvedFrom ? { resolvedFromRecord: resolvedFrom } : {}),
    82→        });
    83→
    84→        return {
    85→          status: "resolved",
    86→          decision: {
    87→            id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`,
    88→            name: topDecision.name,
    89→          },
    90→          confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
    91→          sources: [
    92→            {
    93→              id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`,
    94→              name: topDecision.name,
    95→              score: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
    96→            },
    97→          ],
    98→        };
    99→      }
   100→
   101→      if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.78) {
   102→        const now = new Date();
   103→        const sources = candidates.slice(0, 5);
   104→        const basedOn = sources
   105→          .map((source) => {
   106→            try {
   107→              return parseRecordIdString(source.id, [source.kind as "project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question"], source.kind as any);
   108→            } catch {
   109→              return undefined;
   110→            }
   111→          })
   112→          .filter((value): value is GraphEntityRecord => value !== undefined);
   113→
   114→        const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({
   115→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   116→          summary: input.question,
   117→          status: "inferred",
   118→          now,
   119→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   120→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   121→          rationale: `Inferred from existing graph context with ${sources.length} related entities.`,
   122→          ...(input.options && input.options.length > 0 ? { optionsConsidered: input.options } : {}),
   123→          ...(basedOn.length > 0 ? { basedOn } : {}),
   124→          inferredBy: "chat_agent",
   125→          decidedByName: "chat_agent",
   126→          ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
   127→          ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),
   128→        });
   129→
   130→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   131→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   132→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   133→          targetRecord: decisionRecord,
   134→          now,
   135→          confidence: Math.max(topDecision.score, 0.6),
   136→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   137→          fromText: input.question,
   138→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   139→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   140→        });
   141→
   142→        return {
   143→          status: "inferred",
   144→          decision: {
   145→            id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
   146→            name: input.question,
   147→          },
   148→          confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
   149→          rationale: `Top matching decision: ${topDecision.name}. Related context was strong enough to infer a recommendation.`,
   150→          sources: sources.map((source) => ({
   151→            id: `${source.kind}:${source.id}`,
   152→            name: source.name,
   153→            score: Number(source.score.toFixed(4)),
   154→          })),
   155→        };
   156→      }
   157→
   158→      return {
   159→        status: "unresolved",
   160→        relatedContext: candidates.slice(0, 6).map((source) => ({
   161→          id: `${source.kind}:${source.id}`,
   162→          kind: source.kind,
   163→          name: source.name,
   164→          score: Number(source.score.toFixed(4)),
   165→        })),
   166→        suggestProvisional: true,
   167→      };
   168→    },
   169→  });
   170→}
   171→

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
     5→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     6→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     7→
     8→function normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {
     9→  return new Set(
    10→    value
    11→      .toLowerCase()
    12→      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
    13→      .split(/\s+/)
    14→      .map((token) => token.trim())
    15→      .filter((token) => token.length > 2),
    16→  );
    17→}
    18→
    19→function hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {
    20→  for (const value of a) {
    21→    if (b.has(value)) return true;
    22→  }
    23→  return false;
    24→}
    25→
    26→/** Core logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */
    27→export async function executeCheckConstraints(
    28→  surreal: Surreal,
    29→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    30→  proposedAction: string,
    31→) {
    32→  const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    33→    surreal,
    34→    workspaceRecord,
    35→    query: proposedAction,
    36→    kinds: ["decision"],
    37→    limit: 14,
    38→  });
    39→
    40→  const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(proposedAction);
    41→  const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    42→  const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    43→  const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    44→
    45→  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    46→    const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);
    47→    const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);
    48→    const status = candidate.status?.toLowerCase();
    49→
    50→    if (candidate.kind === "decision" && overlap && (status === "contested" || status === "superseded")) {
    51→      hardConflicts.push({
    52→        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,
    53→        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)), reason: `Decision is marked ${status}.`,
    54→      });
    55→      continue;
    56→    }
    57→
    58→    if (candidate.kind === "decision" && candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {
    59→      supporting.push({
    60→        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,
    61→        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)), reason: "High-similarity decision aligns with proposed action.",
    62→      });
    63→      continue;
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {
    67→      softTensions.push({
    68→        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,
    69→        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    70→        reason: overlap
    71→          ? "Related decision/question may require consistency checks."
    72→          : "Semantically related context may be affected.",
    73→      });
    74→    }
    75→  }
    76→
    77→  return { hard_conflicts: hardConflicts, soft_tensions: softTensions, supporting, proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0 };
    78→}
    79→
    80→export function createCheckConstraintsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    81→  return tool({
    82→    description:
    83→      "Check if a proposed action conflicts with existing decisions or constraints. Returns hard conflicts, soft tensions, supporting context, and proceed flag.",
    84→    inputSchema: z.object({
    85→      proposed_action: z.string().min(1).describe("What is being proposed"),
    86→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    87→    }),
    88→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    89→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    90→      return executeCheckConstraints(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, input.proposed_action);
    91→    },
    92→  });
    93→}
    94→

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/.claude/plans/elegant-wobbling-river.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Expose Brain-Native Tools in MCP Agent Endpoint\n\n## Context\n\nThe MCP agent endpoint (`/mcp/agent/:sessionId`) only exposes 2 brain-native tools (`get_context`, `create_intent`). All other tools come from external MCP server discovery. Meanwhile, `app/src/server/tools/` has 17 tools the chat agent uses but coding agents can't access via MCP. This makes the MCP agent far less capable than the web chat agent.\n\nThe fix: expose Brain tools directly in the MCP agent endpoint. Read tools are always available. Write tools require intent authorization (the existing `create_intent` flow).\n\n## Tool Tiers\n\n**Read (always available, no intent needed) — 7 tools:**\n- `search_entities` — has `executeSearchEntities`\n- `list_workspace_entities` — has `executeListWorkspaceEntities`\n- `get_entity_detail` — has `executeGetEntityDetail`\n- `get_project_status` — has `executeGetProjectStatus`\n- `get_conversation_history` — needs `execute*` extraction\n- `check_constraints` — has `executeCheckConstraints`\n- `resolve_decision` — read-only variant (search only, no record creation)\n\n**Write (gated, require intent via `create_intent provider=\"brain\"`) — 10 tools:**\n- `create_provisional_decision`, `create_question`, `create_observation`, `create_suggestion`\n- `create_work_item`, `edit_work_item`, `move_items_to_project`\n- `acknowledge_observation`, `resolve_observation`\n- `suggest_work_items`\n\n**Excluded (chat-only) — 4 tools:**\n- `invoke_pm_agent` / `invoke_analytics_agent` — need subagent models\n- `confirm_decision` — requires `humanPresent` + `chat_agent` actor\n- `show_relationship_graph` — UI component rendering\n\n## Key Design Decisions\n\n1. **Brain tools bypass the external tool pipeline.** They don't go through `resolveToolsForIdentity` / `can_use` edges. They're appended separately to the tools list and dispatched in the call handler before external tool classification.\n\n2. **Write tools skip extraction provenance.** MCP sessions lack `conversationRecord`/`currentMessageRecord`. This matches the existing CLI MCP route pattern (`mcp-route.ts`), which calls underlying query functions directly without provenance edges. Tool traces (`recordToolTrace`) still provide audit traceability.\n\n3. **Intent resource format:** `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}` — e.g. `mcp_tool:brain:create_observation`. Agents call `create_intent` with `provider: \"brain\"`, `action: \"create_observation\"`.\n\n4. **`resolve_decision` is read-only in MCP.** The chat version creates inferred decisions (with provenance). The MCP version returns search results only. Agents use `create_provisional_decision` explicitly for writes.\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### Step 1: Extract `executeGetConversationHistory`\n\n**File:** `app/src/server/tools/get-conversation-history.ts`\n\nExtract the inline execute logic into a standalone function:\n```ts\nexport async function executeGetConversationHistory(\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n  input: { query: string },\n)\n```\nMove the BM25 message search + entity enrichment logic. The AI SDK wrapper calls this function.\n\n### Step 2: Extract `executeResolveDecision` (read-only)\n\n**File:** `app/src/server/tools/resolve-decision.ts`\n\nExtract a read-only variant that does BM25 search and returns matches without creating records or provenance edges:\n```ts\nexport async function executeResolveDecisionReadOnly(\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n  input: { question: string; kinds?: string[]; project?: string; feature?: string },\n)\n```\nReturns `{ status: \"resolved\"|\"unresolved\", candidates, suggestProvisional }`. No record creation.\n\n### Step 3: Create brain tool definitions module\n\n**New file:** `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tool-definitions.ts` (pure, no IO)\n\nExports:\n- `BRAIN_READ_TOOLS: readonly McpToolDefinition[]` — 7 read tool definitions with JSON Schema inputSchema (mirrored from Zod schemas in each tool file)\n- `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOLS: readonly McpToolDefinition[]` — 10 write tool definitions\n- `BRAIN_READ_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string>`\n- `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string>`\n- `ALL_BRAIN_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string>` — union of read + write + `get_context` + `create_intent`\n\n### Step 4: Add `computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools` to scope engine\n\n**File:** `app/src/server/mcp/scope-engine.ts`\n\nAdd pure helper:\n```ts\nexport function computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools(\n  effectiveScope: EffectiveScope,\n  brainWriteToolNames: ReadonlySet<string>,\n): ReadonlySet<string>\n```\nScans `effectiveScope.authorizedActions` for resources matching `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}` and returns the set of authorized write tool names.\n\n### Step 5: Extend `buildToolsList` to include brain tools\n\n**File:** `app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts`\n\n- Import brain tool definitions from step 3\n- Update `BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOL_NAMES` to include all brain tool names\n- Change `buildToolsList` signature to accept `authorizedBrainWriteTools: ReadonlySet<string>`\n- Append read tools (always, clean description)\n- Append write tools: clean description if authorized, `[GATED]` prefix if not\n- Keep existing `get_context` and `create_intent` as-is\n\n### Step 6: Create brain tools dispatch handler\n\n**New file:** `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tools-handler.ts` (effect boundary)\n\n```ts\nexport type BrainToolCallContext = {\n  workspaceId: string;\n  identityId: string;\n  sessionId: string;\n};\n\nexport async function handleBrainToolCall(\n  toolName: string,\n  args: Record<string, unknown>,\n  context: BrainToolCallContext,\n  deps: { surreal: Surreal; inflight: InflightTracker },\n): Promise<ToolCallResult>\n```\n\nDispatches by tool name:\n\n**Read tools** → call `execute*` functions with `new RecordId(\"workspace\", context.workspaceId)`:\n- `search_entities` → `executeSearchEntities(surreal, ws, args)`\n- `list_workspace_entities` → `executeListWorkspaceEntities(surreal, ws, args)`\n- `get_entity_detail` → `executeGetEntityDetail(surreal, ws, args.entityId)`\n- `get_project_status` → `executeGetProjectStatus(surreal, ws, args.projectId)`\n- `get_conversation_history` → `executeGetConversationHistory(surreal, ws, args)`\n- `check_constraints` → `executeCheckConstraints(surreal, ws, args.proposed_action)`\n- `resolve_decision` → `executeResolveDecisionReadOnly(surreal, ws, args)`\n\n**Write tools** → call underlying query functions directly (pattern from `mcp-route.ts`):\n- `create_observation` → `createObservation({ surreal, workspaceRecord, text, severity, sourceAgent: \"mcp\", now })`\n- `create_provisional_decision` → `createDecisionRecord({ surreal, summary, status: \"provisional\", now, workspaceRecord })`\n- `create_question` → `createQuestionRecord({ surreal, text, status: \"open\", now, workspaceRecord })`\n- `create_suggestion` → `createSuggestion({ surreal, workspaceRecord, ... })`\n- `create_work_item` → direct SurrealDB create + relation edges\n- `edit_work_item` → direct `surreal.query` update + workspace scope check\n- `move_items_to_project` → edge manipulation (delete/create belongs_to)\n- `acknowledge_observation` / `resolve_observation` → status update queries\n- `suggest_work_items` → BM25 duplicate detection, return triage buckets\n\n### Step 7: Wire into agent-mcp-route\n\n**File:** `app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts`\n\n**In `handleToolsList`:**\n1. After computing `classifiedTools`, also compute `effectiveScope` (already done in `resolveClassifiedTools`)\n2. Call `computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools(effectiveScope, BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_NAMES)`\n3. Pass result to `buildToolsList(classifiedTools, authorizedBrainWriteTools)`\n\n**In `tools/call` handler:**\n1. Before the existing `create_intent` special case, add brain tool dispatch:\n   - If `toolName` is in `BRAIN_READ_TOOL_NAMES` → call `handleBrainToolCall` directly, record trace\n   - If `toolName` is in `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_NAMES` → compute scope, check for matching intent (`mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}`). If authorized → call `handleBrainToolCall`, record trace with intentId. If not authorized → return `intent_required` error\n   - Otherwise → fall through to existing external tool dispatch\n\nRefactor `resolveClassifiedTools` to also return `effectiveScope` so it can be reused.\n\n### Step 8: Acceptance tests\n\n**File:** `tests/acceptance/agent-mcp-brain-tools.test.ts` (new)\n\nUsing existing `acceptance-test-kit.ts` patterns:\n\n1. **Read tool in tools/list**: Verify `tools/list` includes `search_entities`, `list_workspace_entities`, etc.\n2. **Write tool gated in tools/list**: Verify write tools appear with `[GATED]` prefix when no intent exists\n3. **Read tool execution**: Seed a task, call `search_entities` via `tools/call`, verify results\n4. **Write tool blocked without intent**: Call `create_observation` via `tools/call`, verify `intent_required` error\n5. **Write tool succeeds after intent**: Call `create_intent` with `provider: \"brain\"`, `action: \"create_observation\"`, then call `create_observation`, verify success\n6. **Write tool authorized in tools/list**: After intent approval, verify `create_observation` appears without `[GATED]` prefix\n\n## Files Changed\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `app/src/server/tools/get-conversation-history.ts` | Extract `executeGetConversationHistory` |\n| `app/src/server/tools/resolve-decision.ts` | Extract `executeResolveDecisionReadOnly` |\n| `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tool-definitions.ts` | **NEW** — tool definitions (pure) |\n| `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tools-handler.ts` | **NEW** — dispatch handler (effect boundary) |\n| `app/src/server/mcp/scope-engine.ts` | Add `computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools` |\n| `app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts` | Extend `buildToolsList` for brain tools |\n| `app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts` | Wire brain tool dispatch |\n| `tests/acceptance/agent-mcp-brain-tools.test.ts` | **NEW** — acceptance tests |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `bun run typecheck` — no type errors\n2. `bun test tests/unit/` — existing unit tests pass\n3. `bun test tests/acceptance/agent-mcp-brain-tools.test.ts` — new acceptance tests pass\n4. `bun test tests/acceptance/` — full acceptance suite passes (no regressions)\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Expose Brain-Native Tools in MCP Agent Endpoint

## Context

The MCP agent endpoint (`/mcp/agent/:sessionId`) only exposes 2 brain-native tools (`get_context`, `create_intent`). All other tools come from external MCP server discovery. Meanwhile, `app/src/server/tools/` has 17 tools the chat agent uses but coding agents can't access via MCP. This makes the MCP agent far less capable than the web chat agent.

The fix: expose Brain tools directly in the MCP agent endpoint. Read tools are always available. Write tools require intent authorization (the existing `create_intent` flow).

## Tool Tiers

**Read (always available, no intent needed) — 7 tools:**
- `search_entities` — has `executeSearchEntities`
- `list_workspace_entities` — has `executeListWorkspaceEntities`
- `get_entity_detail` — has `executeGetEntityDetail`
- `get_project_status` — has `executeGetProjectStatus`
- `get_conversation_history` — needs `execute*` extraction
- `check_constraints` — has `executeCheckConstraints`
- `resolve_decision` — read-only variant (search only, no record creation)

**Write (gated, require intent via `create_intent provider="brain"`) — 10 tools:**
- `create_provisional_decision`, `create_question`, `create_observation`, `create_suggestion`
- `create_work_item`, `edit_work_item`, `move_items_to_project`
- `acknowledge_observation`, `resolve_observation`
- `suggest_work_items`

**Excluded (chat-only) — 4 tools:**
- `invoke_pm_agent` / `invoke_analytics_agent` — need subagent models
- `confirm_decision` — requires `humanPresent` + `chat_agent` actor
- `show_relationship_graph` — UI component rendering

## Key Design Decisions

1. **Brain tools bypass the external tool pipeline.** They don't go through `resolveToolsForIdentity` / `can_use` edges. They're appended separately to the tools list and dispatched in the call handler before external tool classification.

2. **Write tools skip extraction provenance.** MCP sessions lack `conversationRecord`/`currentMessageRecord`. This matches the existing CLI MCP route pattern (`mcp-route.ts`), which calls underlying query functions directly without provenance edges. Tool traces (`recordToolTrace`) still provide audit traceability.

3. **Intent resource format:** `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}` — e.g. `mcp_tool:brain:create_observation`. Agents call `create_intent` with `provider: "brain"`, `action: "create_observation"`.

4. **`resolve_decision` is read-only in MCP.** The chat version creates inferred decisions (with provenance). The MCP version returns search results only. Agents use `create_provisional_decision` explicitly for writes.

## Implementation Steps

### Step 1: Extract `executeGetConversationHistory`

**File:** `app/src/server/tools/get-conversation-history.ts`

Extract the inline execute logic into a standalone function:
```ts
export async function executeGetConversationHistory(
  surreal: Surreal,
  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
  input: { query: string },
)
```
Move the BM25 message search + entity enrichment logic. The AI SDK wrapper calls this function.

### Step 2: Extract `executeResolveDecision` (read-only)

**File:** `app/src/server/tools/resolve-decision.ts`

Extract a read-only variant that does BM25 search and returns matches without creating records or provenance edges:
```ts
export async function executeResolveDecisionReadOnly(
  surreal: Surreal,
  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
  input: { question: string; kinds?: string[]; project?: string; feature?: string },
)
```
Returns `{ status: "resolved"|"unresolved", candidates, suggestProvisional }`. No record creation.

### Step 3: Create brain tool definitions module

**New file:** `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tool-definitions.ts` (pure, no IO)

Exports:
- `BRAIN_READ_TOOLS: readonly McpToolDefinition[]` — 7 read tool definitions with JSON Schema inputSchema (mirrored from Zod schemas in each tool file)
- `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOLS: readonly McpToolDefinition[]` — 10 write tool definitions
- `BRAIN_READ_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string>`
- `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string>`
- `ALL_BRAIN_TOOL_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string>` — union of read + write + `get_context` + `create_intent`

### Step 4: Add `computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools` to scope engine

**File:** `app/src/server/mcp/scope-engine.ts`

Add pure helper:
```ts
export function computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools(
  effectiveScope: EffectiveScope,
  brainWriteToolNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
): ReadonlySet<string>
```
Scans `effectiveScope.authorizedActions` for resources matching `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}` and returns the set of authorized write tool names.

### Step 5: Extend `buildToolsList` to include brain tools

**File:** `app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts`

- Import brain tool definitions from step 3
- Update `BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOL_NAMES` to include all brain tool names
- Change `buildToolsList` signature to accept `authorizedBrainWriteTools: ReadonlySet<string>`
- Append read tools (always, clean description)
- Append write tools: clean description if authorized, `[GATED]` prefix if not
- Keep existing `get_context` and `create_intent` as-is

### Step 6: Create brain tools dispatch handler

**New file:** `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tools-handler.ts` (effect boundary)

```ts
export type BrainToolCallContext = {
  workspaceId: string;
  identityId: string;
  sessionId: string;
};

export async function handleBrainToolCall(
  toolName: string,
  args: Record<string, unknown>,
  context: BrainToolCallContext,
  deps: { surreal: Surreal; inflight: InflightTracker },
): Promise<ToolCallResult>
```

Dispatches by tool name:

**Read tools** → call `execute*` functions with `new RecordId("workspace", context.workspaceId)`:
- `search_entities` → `executeSearchEntities(surreal, ws, args)`
- `list_workspace_entities` → `executeListWorkspaceEntities(surreal, ws, args)`
- `get_entity_detail` → `executeGetEntityDetail(surreal, ws, args.entityId)`
- `get_project_status` → `executeGetProjectStatus(surreal, ws, args.projectId)`
- `get_conversation_history` → `executeGetConversationHistory(surreal, ws, args)`
- `check_constraints` → `executeCheckConstraints(surreal, ws, args.proposed_action)`
- `resolve_decision` → `executeResolveDecisionReadOnly(surreal, ws, args)`

**Write tools** → call underlying query functions directly (pattern from `mcp-route.ts`):
- `create_observation` → `createObservation({ surreal, workspaceRecord, text, severity, sourceAgent: "mcp", now })`
- `create_provisional_decision` → `createDecisionRecord({ surreal, summary, status: "provisional", now, workspaceRecord })`
- `create_question` → `createQuestionRecord({ surreal, text, status: "open", now, workspaceRecord })`
- `create_suggestion` → `createSuggestion({ surreal, workspaceRecord, ... })`
- `create_work_item` → direct SurrealDB create + relation edges
- `edit_work_item` → direct `surreal.query` update + workspace scope check
- `move_items_to_project` → edge manipulation (delete/create belongs_to)
- `acknowledge_observation` / `resolve_observation` → status update queries
- `suggest_work_items` → BM25 duplicate detection, return triage buckets

### Step 7: Wire into agent-mcp-route

**File:** `app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts`

**In `handleToolsList`:**
1. After computing `classifiedTools`, also compute `effectiveScope` (already done in `resolveClassifiedTools`)
2. Call `computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools(effectiveScope, BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_NAMES)`
3. Pass result to `buildToolsList(classifiedTools, authorizedBrainWriteTools)`

**In `tools/call` handler:**
1. Before the existing `create_intent` special case, add brain tool dispatch:
   - If `toolName` is in `BRAIN_READ_TOOL_NAMES` → call `handleBrainToolCall` directly, record trace
   - If `toolName` is in `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_NAMES` → compute scope, check for matching intent (`mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}`). If authorized → call `handleBrainToolCall`, record trace with intentId. If not authorized → return `intent_required` error
   - Otherwise → fall through to existing external tool dispatch

Refactor `resolveClassifiedTools` to also return `effectiveScope` so it can be reused.

### Step 8: Acceptance tests

**File:** `tests/acceptance/agent-mcp-brain-tools.test.ts` (new)

Using existing `acceptance-test-kit.ts` patterns:

1. **Read tool in tools/list**: Verify `tools/list` includes `search_entities`, `list_workspace_entities`, etc.
2. **Write tool gated in tools/list**: Verify write tools appear with `[GATED]` prefix when no intent exists
3. **Read tool execution**: Seed a task, call `search_entities` via `tools/call`, verify results
4. **Write tool blocked without intent**: Call `create_observation` via `tools/call`, verify `intent_required` error
5. **Write tool succeeds after intent**: Call `create_intent` with `provider: "brain"`, `action: "create_observation"`, then call `create_observation`, verify success
6. **Write tool authorized in tools/list**: After intent approval, verify `create_observation` appears without `[GATED]` prefix

## Files Changed

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `app/src/server/tools/get-conversation-history.ts` | Extract `executeGetConversationHistory` |
| `app/src/server/tools/resolve-decision.ts` | Extract `executeResolveDecisionReadOnly` |
| `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tool-definitions.ts` | **NEW** — tool definitions (pure) |
| `app/src/server/mcp/brain-tools-handler.ts` | **NEW** — dispatch handler (effect boundary) |
| `app/src/server/mcp/scope-engine.ts` | Add `computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools` |
| `app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts` | Extend `buildToolsList` for brain tools |
| `app/src/server/mcp/agent-mcp-route.ts` | Wire brain tool dispatch |
| `tests/acceptance/agent-mcp-brain-tools.test.ts` | **NEW** — acceptance tests |

## Verification

1. `bun run typecheck` — no type errors
2. `bun test tests/unit/` — existing unit tests pass
3. `bun test tests/acceptance/agent-mcp-brain-tools.test.ts` — new acceptance tests pass
4. `bun test tests/acceptance/` — full acceptance suite passes (no regressions)

};\n        }),\n      );\n\n      return {\n        messageId: `message:${message.id}`,\n        conversationId: `conversation:${message.conversationId}`,\n        excerpt: message.text.slice(0, 280),\n        confidence: Number(message.score.toFixed(4)),\n        linkedEntities: linkedEntities.filter((value) => value !== undefined),\n      };\n    }),\n  );\n\n  return {\n    query: input.query,\n    results: enrichedMessages,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function createGetConversationHistoryTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {\n  return tool({\n    description:\n      \"Search past conversations for discussions about a topic and return relevant message excerpts with linked entities.\",\n    inputSchema: z.object({\n      query: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Topic to search for\"),\n      projectId: z.string().optional().describe(\"Optional project scope filter\"),\n    }),\n    execute: async (input, options) => {\n      const context = requireToolContext(options);\n      return executeGetConversationHistory(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, input);\n    },\n  });\n}"
}

Use create_provisional_decision to create a new decision if this doesn't match.`,\n      sources: sources.map((s) => ({ id: `${s.kind}:${s.id}`, name: s.name, score: Number(s.score.toFixed(4)) })),\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    status: \"unresolved\" as const,\n    relatedContext: candidates.slice(0, 6).map((s) => ({\n      id: `${s.kind}:${s.id}`, kind: s.kind, name: s.name, score: Number(s.score.toFixed(4)),\n    })),\n    suggestProvisional: true,\n  };\n}\n\nconst resolveDecisionInputSchema = z.object({"
}

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { SUGGESTION_CATEGORIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { isEntityInWorkspace, parseRecordIdString } from "../graph/queries";
     5→import { createSuggestion } from "../suggestion/queries";
     6→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
     7→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     8→
     9→export function createCreateSuggestionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    10→  return tool({
    11→    description:
    12→      "Create a suggestion for the user — a proactive, actionable proposal with rationale. Use when you identify optimizations, risks, opportunities, conflicts, missing elements, or potential pivots that the user should consider. Observations are for agent-to-agent signals; suggestions are agent-to-human proposals.",
    13→    inputSchema: z.object({
    14→      text: z.string().min(1).describe("The suggestion itself — what you propose the user should consider or do"),
    15→      category: z
    16→        .enum(SUGGESTION_CATEGORIES)
    17→        .describe(
    18→          "optimization: improve existing approach. risk: potential problem to address. opportunity: beneficial possibility. conflict: contradictory elements detected. missing: gap in current plan. pivot: fundamental direction change worth considering.",
    19→        ),
    20→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why you are making this suggestion — the reasoning and evidence behind it"),
    21→      confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1).describe("How confident you are in this suggestion (0-1)"),
    22→      target_entity_id: z
    23→        .string()
    24→        .optional()
    25→        .describe("Optional target entity this suggestion is about (format: table:id, e.g. project:uuid or feature:uuid)"),
    26→      evidence_entity_ids: z
    27→        .array(z.string())
    28→        .optional()
    29→        .describe("Optional entity IDs that support the rationale (format: table:id). Can include observations, decisions, tasks, etc."),
    30→    }),
    31→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    32→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_suggestion", deps);
    33→
    34→      const targetTables = ["project", "feature", "task", "question", "decision"] as const;
    35→      const targetRecord = input.target_entity_id
    36→        ? parseRecordIdString(input.target_entity_id, [...targetTables])
    37→        : undefined;
    38→
    39→      if (targetRecord) {
    40→        const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, targetRecord);
    41→        if (!scoped) {
    42→          throw new Error("target entity is outside the current workspace scope");
    43→        }
    44→      }
    45→
    46→      const evidenceTables = ["workspace", "project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"] as const;
    47→      const evidenceRecords = input.evidence_entity_ids?.map((id) =>
    48→        parseRecordIdString(id, [...evidenceTables]),
    49→      );
    50→
    51→      const suggestionRecord = await createSuggestion({
    52→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    53→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    54→        text: input.text,
    55→        category: input.category,
    56→        rationale: input.rationale,
    57→        suggestedBy: context.actor,
    58→        confidence: input.confidence,
    59→        now: new Date(),
    60→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    61→        ...(targetRecord ? { targetRecord } : {}),
    62→        ...(evidenceRecords ? { evidenceRecords } : {}),
    63→      });
    64→
    65→      return {
    66→        suggestion_id: `suggestion:${suggestionRecord.id as string}`,
    67→        text: input.text,
    68→        category: input.category,
    69→        rationale: input.rationale,
    70→        confidence: input.confidence,
    71→        status: "pending",
    72→        ...(input.target_entity_id ? { target: input.target_entity_id } : {}),
    73→      };
    74→    },
    75→  });
    76→}
    77→
