     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import {
     5→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     6→  isEntityInWorkspace,
     7→  parseRecordIdString,
     8→  readEntityName,
     9→} from "../graph/queries";
    10→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    11→import { seedDescriptionEntry } from "../descriptions/persist";
    12→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    13→
    14→const editableWorkItemTableSchema = z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]);
    15→
    16→export function createEditWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    17→  return tool({
    18→    description:
    19→      "Edit an existing task/feature/project by id. Use for rename or metadata updates. Do NOT use this to create new entities.",
    20→    inputSchema: z.object({
    21→      id: z.string().min(1).describe("Existing work item id in table:id format (task:..., feature:..., project:...)"),
    22→      title: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("New title/name"),
    23→      status: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("Optional new status"),
    24→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional category (task/feature only)"),
    25→      priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("Optional priority (task only)"),
    26→      rationale: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("Optional rationale note appended as a description entry"),
    27→    }),
    28→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    29→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_task", deps);
    30→      const now = new Date();
    31→      const workItemRecord = parseRecordIdString(input.id, ["task", "feature", "project"]);
    32→
    33→      const inWorkspace = await isEntityInWorkspace(
    34→        deps.surreal,
    35→        context.workspaceRecord,
    36→        workItemRecord,
    37→      );
    38→      if (!inWorkspace) {
    39→        throw new Error(`work item is not in workspace: ${input.id}`);
    40→      }
    41→
    42→      const tableName = editableWorkItemTableSchema.parse(workItemRecord.table.name);
    43→      const patch: Record<string, unknown> = { updated_at: now };
    44→      const updatedFields: string[] = [];
    45→
    46→      if (input.title) {
    47→        if (tableName === "task") {
    48→          patch.title = input.title;
    49→        } else {
    50→          patch.name = input.title;
    51→        }
    52→        updatedFields.push("title");
    53→      }
    54→
    55→      if (input.status) {
    56→        patch.status = input.status;
    57→        updatedFields.push("status");
    58→      }
    59→
    60→      if (input.category) {
    61→        if (tableName === "project") {
    62→          throw new Error("category is not editable on project");
    63→        }
    64→        patch.category = input.category;
    65→        updatedFields.push("category");
    66→      }
    67→
    68→      if (input.priority) {
    69→        if (tableName !== "task") {
    70→          throw new Error("priority is only editable on task");
    71→        }
    72→        patch.priority = input.priority;
    73→        updatedFields.push("priority");
    74→      }
    75→
    76→      if (updatedFields.length === 0 && !input.rationale) {
    77→        throw new Error("at least one editable field must be provided");
    78→      }
    79→
    80→      if (updatedFields.length > 0) {
    81→        await deps.surreal.update(workItemRecord).merge(patch);
    82→      }
    83→
    84→      if (input.rationale) {
    85→        await seedDescriptionEntry({
    86→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    87→          targetRecord: workItemRecord,
    88→          text: input.rationale,
    89→        });
    90→      }
    91→
    92→      await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    93→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    94→        sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    95→        targetRecord: workItemRecord,
    96→        now,
    97→        confidence: 0.95,
    98→        model: deps.extractionModelId,
    99→        fromText: input.title ?? input.id,
   100→        evidence: context.latestUserText,
   101→        evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   102→      });
   103→
   104→      const updatedName = await readEntityName(deps.surreal, workItemRecord);
   105→
   106→      return {
   107→        entity_id: `${tableName}:${workItemRecord.id as string}`,
   108→        kind: tableName,
   109→        title: updatedName ?? input.title ?? input.id,
   110→        updated_fields: updatedFields,
   111→        ...(input.rationale ? { rationale_added: true } : {}),
   112→      };
   113→    },
   114→  });
   115→}
   116→

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type EntityCategory } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
     5→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
     6→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     7→
     8→type SuggestedWorkItem = {
     9→  kind: "task" | "feature" | "project";
    10→  title: string;
    11→  rationale: string;
    12→  category?: EntityCategory;
    13→  project?: string;
    14→  priority?: string;
    15→  possible_duplicate?: {
    16→    id: string;
    17→    name: string;
    18→    similarity: number;
    19→  };
    20→};
    21→
    22→type UpdatedWorkItem = {
    23→  existing_id: string;
    24→  title: string;
    25→  changes: string;
    26→};
    27→
    28→type DiscardedWorkItem = {
    29→  title: string;
    30→  reason: string;
    31→};
    32→
    33→const CLOSED_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "completed", "closed", "resolved", "superseded"]);
    34→
    35→export function createSuggestWorkItemsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    36→  return tool({
    37→    description:
    38→      "Process a batch of proposed work items and return issue-style PM triage buckets: suggestions, updated, discarded.",
    39→    inputSchema: z.object({
    40→      items: z.array(
    41→        z.object({
    42→          kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe("project: named product area (MUST exist before features/tasks). feature: capability within an existing project. task: concrete executable work."),
    43→          title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise work item title"),
    44→          rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this work item is needed"),
    45→          category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional work item category"),
    46→          project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    47→          priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important. medium: normal. low: nice-to-have."),
    48→        }),
    49→      ).min(1).max(25),
    50→    }),
    51→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    52→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_suggestion", deps);
    53→      const suggestions: SuggestedWorkItem[] = [];
    54→      const updated: UpdatedWorkItem[] = [];
    55→      const discarded: DiscardedWorkItem[] = [];
    56→
    57→      for (const item of input.items) {
    58→        const suggestion: SuggestedWorkItem = {
    59→          kind: item.kind,
    60→          title: item.title,
    61→          rationale: item.rationale,
    62→          ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),
    63→          ...(item.project ? { project: item.project } : {}),
    64→          ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),
    65→        };
    66→
    67→        // BM25 text search for duplicate detection -- replaces embedding similarity
    68→        const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    69→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    70→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    71→          query: item.title,
    72→          kinds: [item.kind],
    73→          limit: 5,
    74→        });
    75→
    76→        const top = candidates[0];
    77→        if (!top) {
    78→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    79→          continue;
    80→        }
    81→
    82→        const normalizedTopStatus = top.status ? top.status.trim().toLowerCase() : undefined;
    83→        if (normalizedTopStatus && CLOSED_STATUSES.has(normalizedTopStatus)) {
    84→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    85→          continue;
    86→        }
    87→
    88→        // BM25 duplicate detection: exact title match = discard, partial match = merge
    89→        const normalizedTopName = top.name.trim().toLowerCase();
    90→        const normalizedTitle = item.title.trim().toLowerCase();
    91→
    92→        if (normalizedTopName === normalizedTitle) {
    93→          discarded.push({
    94→            title: item.title,
    95→            reason: `Exact duplicate of existing item ${top.kind}:${top.id} (${top.name})`,
    96→          });
    97→          continue;
    98→        }
    99→
   100→        // BM25 match means significant keyword overlap -- suggest merge
   101→        updated.push({
   102→          existing_id: `${top.kind}:${top.id}`,
   103→          title: top.name,
   104→          changes: `Merge context from suggested item '${item.title}'`,
   105→        });
   106→      }
   107→
   108→      return {
   109→        suggestions,
   110→        updated,
   111→        discarded,
   112→      };
   113→    },
   114→  });
   115→}

     1→import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { tool } from "ai";
     4→import { z } from "zod";
     5→import {
     6→  parseRecordIdString,
     7→  readEntityName,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     9→  isEntityInWorkspace,
    10→} from "../graph/queries";
    11→import { ensureProjectFeatureEdge } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    12→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    13→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    14→
    15→type MoveResult = {
    16→  entity_id: string;
    17→  title: string;
    18→};
    19→
    20→type MoveFailure = {
    21→  entity_id: string;
    22→  reason: string;
    23→};
    24→
    25→export function createMoveItemsToProjectTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    26→  return tool({
    27→    description:
    28→      "Move existing features or tasks to a different project. Use when reorganizing — e.g. user says items belong under a different project. Deletes the old project edge and creates a new one. Does NOT duplicate entities.",
    29→    inputSchema: z.object({
    30→      entity_ids: z
    31→        .array(z.string().min(1))
    32→        .min(1)
    33→        .describe("Polymorphic entity IDs to move, e.g. ['feature:uuid', 'task:uuid']"),
    34→      target_project: z
    35→        .string()
    36→        .min(1)
    37→        .describe("Target project name or 'project:uuid' to move entities into"),
    38→    }),
    39→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    40→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_task", deps);
    41→      const now = new Date();
    42→
    43→      let projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>;
    44→      try {
    45→        projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    46→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    47→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    48→          projectInput: input.target_project,
    49→        });
    50→      } catch (err) {
    51→        return {
    52→          error: `Target project not found: ${input.target_project}`,
    53→          moved: [],
    54→          failed: input.entity_ids.map((id) => ({ entity_id: id, reason: "target project resolution failed" })),
    55→        };
    56→      }
    57→
    58→      const moved: MoveResult[] = [];
    59→      const failed: MoveFailure[] = [];
    60→
    61→      for (const rawId of input.entity_ids) {
    62→        try {
    63→          const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(rawId, ["feature", "task"]);
    64→          const table = entityRecord.table.name;
    65→
    66→          const inWorkspace = await isEntityInWorkspace(
    67→            deps.surreal,
    68→            context.workspaceRecord,
    69→            entityRecord,
    70→          );
    71→          if (!inWorkspace) {
    72→            failed.push({ entity_id: rawId, reason: "entity not found in workspace" });
    73→            continue;
    74→          }
    75→
    76→          const title = await readEntityName(deps.surreal, entityRecord) ?? rawId;
    77→
    78→          if (table === "feature") {
    79→            // Delete old has_feature edges (project → feature)
    80→            await deps.surreal.query(
    81→              "DELETE FROM has_feature WHERE out = $feature;",
    82→              { feature: entityRecord },
    83→            );
    84→
    85→            // Create new has_feature edge to target project
    86→            await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(
    87→              deps.surreal,
    88→              projectRecord,
    89→              entityRecord as RecordId<"feature", string>,
    90→              now,
    91→            );
    92→
    93→            moved.push({ entity_id: rawId, title });
    94→          } else if (table === "task") {
    95→            // Delete old belongs_to edges to projects only (preserve feature edges)
    96→            await deps.surreal.query(
    97→              "DELETE FROM belongs_to WHERE `in` = $task AND record::tb(out) = 'project';",
    98→              { task: entityRecord },
    99→            );
   100→
   101→            // Create new belongs_to edge to target project
   102→            await deps.surreal
   103→              .relate(
   104→                entityRecord,
   105→                new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()),
   106→                projectRecord,
   107→                { added_at: now },
   108→              )
   109→              .output("after");
   110→
   111→            moved.push({ entity_id: rawId, title });
   112→          } else {
   113→            failed.push({ entity_id: rawId, reason: `unsupported entity type: ${table}` });
   114→          }
   115→        } catch (err) {
   116→          failed.push({
   117→            entity_id: rawId,
   118→            reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "unknown error",
   119→          });
   120→        }
   121→      }
   122→
   123→      return { moved, failed };
   124→    },
   125→  });
   126→}
   127→
```

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import {
     4→  createDecisionRecord,
     5→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     6→  resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord,
     7→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     8→} from "../graph/queries";
     9→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    10→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    11→
    12→export function createCreateProvisionalDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    13→  return tool({
    14→    description:
    15→      "Create a provisional decision when no existing answer exists. Use after resolve_decision returns unresolved and the user wants to proceed.",
    16→    inputSchema: z.object({
    17→      name: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise decision name"),
    18→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this decision was made"),
    19→      context: z
    20→        .object({
    21→          project: z.string().optional(),
    22→          feature: z.string().optional(),
    23→        })
    24→        .optional(),
    25→      options_considered: z.array(z.string().min(1)).optional(),
    26→    }),
    27→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    28→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_decision", deps);
    29→      const now = new Date();
    30→
    31→      const projectRecord = input.context?.project
    32→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    33→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    34→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    35→            projectInput: input.context.project,
    36→          })
    37→        : undefined;
    38→
    39→      const featureRecord = input.context?.feature
    40→        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({
    41→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    42→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    43→            featureInput: input.context.feature,
    44→          })
    45→        : undefined;
    46→
    47→      const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({
    48→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    49→        summary: input.name,
    50→        status: "provisional",
    51→        now,
    52→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    53→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    54→        rationale: input.rationale,
    55→        ...(input.options_considered && input.options_considered.length > 0
    56→          ? { optionsConsidered: input.options_considered }
    57→          : {}),
    58→        decidedByName: "chat_agent",
    59→        ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    60→        ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),
    61→      });
    62→
    63→      await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    64→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    65→        sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    66→        targetRecord: decisionRecord,
    67→        now,
    68→        confidence: 0.9,
    69→        model: deps.extractionModelId,
    70→        fromText: input.name,
    71→        evidence: context.latestUserText,
    72→        evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    73→      });
    74→
    75→      return {
    76→        decision_id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
    77→        status: "provisional",
    78→        review_required: true,
    79→      };
    80→    },
    81→  });
    82→}
    83→

    45→            featureInput: input.context.feature,
    46→          })
    47→        : undefined;
    48→
    49→      const questionRecord = await createQuestionRecord({

    53→        now,
    54→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,

    56→        ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
    57→        ...(input.priority ? { priority: input.priority } : {}),
    58→        ...(input.assigned_to ? { assignedToName: input.assigned_to } : {}),
    59→        ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),

```
     1→/**
     2→ * Error Response Builder — Pure functions for building structured
     3→ * MCP error responses and enriching gated tool descriptions.
     4→ *
     5→ * Pure core: no IO, no DB, no side effects.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9→// Types
    10→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11→
    12→export type ActionSpecTemplate = {
    13→  readonly provider: string;
    14→  readonly action: string;
    15→  readonly parameterSchema?: Record<string, unknown>;
    16→};
    17→
    18→export type IntentRequiredError = {
    19→  readonly code: -32403;
    20→  readonly message: "intent_required";
    21→  readonly data: {
    22→    readonly tool: string;
    23→    readonly action_spec_template: ActionSpecTemplate;
    24→  };
    25→};
    26→
    27→export type ConstraintViolationError = {
    28→  readonly code: -32403;
    29→  readonly message: "constraint_violation";
    30→  readonly data: {
    31→    readonly field: string;
    32→    readonly requested: unknown;
    33→    readonly authorized: unknown;
    34→  };
    35→};
    36→
    37→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38→// Pure functions
    39→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    40→
    41→/** Build an intent-required error response for a gated tool invocation. */
    42→export function buildIntentRequiredError(
    43→  toolName: string,
    44→  toolkit: string,
    45→  parameterSchema?: Record<string, unknown>,
    46→): IntentRequiredError {
    47→  const actionSpecTemplate: ActionSpecTemplate = {
    48→    provider: toolkit,
    49→    action: toolName,
    50→    ...(parameterSchema ? { parameterSchema } : {}),
    51→  };
    52→
    53→  return {
    54→    code: -32403,
    55→    message: "intent_required",
    56→    data: {
    57→      tool: toolName,
    58→      action_spec_template: actionSpecTemplate,
    59→    },
    60→  };
    61→}
    62→
    63→/** Build a constraint-violation error response. */
    64→export function buildConstraintViolationError(
    65→  field: string,
    66→  requested: unknown,
    67→  authorized: unknown,
    68→): ConstraintViolationError {
    69→  return {
    70→    code: -32403,
    71→    message: "constraint_violation",
    72→    data: { field, requested, authorized },
    73→  };
    74→}
    75→
    76→/** Prepend escalation instructions to a gated tool's description. */
    77→export function enrichGatedDescription(
    78→  originalDescription: string,
    79→  toolkit: string,
    80→  action: string,
    81→): string {
    82→  return (
    83→    `[GATED] This tool requires an approved intent for ${toolkit}:${action}. ` +
    84→    `Request an intent before calling this tool.\n\n${originalDescription}`
    85→  );
    86→}
    87→

  "new_string": "/**\n * Compute the set of brain write tools that have an approved intent.\n * Checks for resources matching `mcp_tool:brain:{toolName}`.\n */\nexport function computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools(\n  effectiveScope: EffectiveScope,\n  brainWriteToolNames: ReadonlySet<string>,\n): ReadonlySet<string> {\n  const authorized = new Set<string>();\n  for (const toolName of brainWriteToolNames) {\n    const resource = `mcp_tool:brain:${toolName}`;\n    if (effectiveScope.authorizedActions.some((a) => a.resource === resource)) {\n      authorized.add(toolName);\n    }\n  }\n  return authorized;\n}\n\n/**\n * Find the first intent whose authorization_details match a brain write tool.\n * Returns the intent summary or undefined if no match.\n */\nexport function findBrainWriteIntent(\n  toolName: string,\n  effectiveScope: EffectiveScope,\n): AuthorizedIntentSummary | undefined {\n  const resource = `mcp_tool:brain:${toolName}`;\n  return effectiveScope.intents.find((intent) =>\n    intent.authorizationDetails.some((action) => action.resource === resource),\n  );\n}\n\n/** Classifies each granted tool as authorized, gated, or brain_native. */\nexport function classifyTools("

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts",
  "old_string": "/**\n * Build the MCP ListToolsResult from classified tools.\n *\n * Pipeline:\n *   classifiedTools -> filter out brain_native -> map to definitions -> append brain-native tools\n */\nexport function buildToolsList(\n  classifiedTools: readonly ClassifiedTool[],\n): ListToolsResult {\n  // Convert granted tools (authorized + gated), excluding brain_native from the classified set\n  // because we append canonical brain-native definitions separately\n  const grantedToolDefinitions = classifiedTools\n    .filter((ct) => ct.classification.kind !== \"brain_native\")\n    .map(classifiedToolToDefinition);\n\n  return {\n    tools: [...grantedToolDefinitions, ...BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOLS],\n  };\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Build gated description for a brain write tool.\n */\nfunction buildBrainGatedToolDescription(\n  originalDescription: string,\n  toolName: string,\n): string {\n  return (\n    `[GATED] This tool requires an approved intent. ` +\n    `Call create_intent with provider=\"brain\" and action=\"${toolName}\" to request authorization.\\n\\n` +\n    originalDescription\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Build the MCP ListToolsResult from classified tools.\n *\n * Pipeline:\n *   classifiedTools -> filter out brain_native -> map to definitions\n *   -> append brain read tools (always available)\n *   -> append brain write tools (gated or authorized based on intent)\n *   -> append infrastructure tools (get_context, create_intent)\n */\nexport function buildToolsList(\n  classifiedTools: readonly ClassifiedTool[],\n  authorizedBrainWriteTools: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(),\n): ListToolsResult {\n  // Convert granted tools (authorized + gated), excluding brain_native from the classified set\n  // because we append canonical brain-native definitions separately\n  const grantedToolDefinitions = classifiedTools\n    .filter((ct) => ct.classification.kind !== \"brain_native\")\n    .map(classifiedToolToDefinition);\n\n  // Brain write tools: gated or authorized based on intent\n  const brainWriteToolDefinitions = BRAIN_WRITE_TOOLS.map((tool) => {\n    if (authorizedBrainWriteTools.has(tool.name)) {\n      return tool; // authorized — show with clean description\n    }\n    return {\n      ...tool,\n      description: buildBrainGatedToolDescription(tool.description, tool.name),\n    };\n  });\n\n  return {\n    tools: [\n      ...grantedToolDefinitions,\n      ...BRAIN_READ_TOOLS,\n      ...brainWriteToolDefinitions,\n      ...BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOLS,\n    ],\n  };\n}"
}

args.query as string,\n        kinds: args.kinds as string[] | undefined,\n        limit: (args.limit as number) ?? 10,\n      });\n\n    case \"list_workspace_entities\":\n      return executeListWorkspaceEntities(surreal, ws, {\n        kind: args.kind as string,\n        status: args.status as string | undefined,\n        project: args.project as string | undefined,\n        limit: (args.limit as number) ?? 25,\n      });\n\n    case \"get_entity_detail\":\n      return executeGetEntityDetail(surreal, ws, args.entityId as string);\n\n    case \"get_project_status\":\n      return executeGetProjectStatus(surreal, ws, args.projectId as string);\n\n    case \"get_conversation_history\":\n      return executeGetConversationHistory(surreal, ws, {\n        query: args.query as string,\n      });\n\n    case \"check_constraints\":\n      return executeCheckConstraints(surreal, ws, args.proposed_action as string);\n\n    case \"resolve_decision\":\n      return executeResolveDecisionReadOnly(surreal, ws, {\n        question: args.question as string,\n        project: args.project as string | undefined,\n        feature: args.feature as string | undefined,\n      });\n\n    default:\n      throw new Error(`Unknown brain read tool: ${toolName}`);\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Write tool dispatch\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function dispatchWriteTool(\n  toolName: string,\n  args: Record<string, unknown>,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  ws: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n  context: BrainToolCallContext,\n): Promise<unknown> {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const sessionRecord = new RecordId(\"agent_session\", context.sessionId);\n\n  switch (toolName) {\n    case \"create_provisional_decision\": {\n      const projectRecord = (args.context as { project?: string })?.project\n        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, projectInput: (args.context as { project: string }).project })\n        : undefined;\n      const featureRecord = (args.context as { feature?: string })?.feature\n        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, featureInput: (args.context as { feature: string }).feature })\n        : undefined;\n\n      const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({\n        surreal,\n        summary: args.name as string,\n        status: \"provisional\",\n        now,\n        workspaceRecord: ws,\n        rationale: args.rationale as string,\n        ...(args.options_considered ? { optionsConsidered: args.options_considered as string[] } : {}),\n        decidedByName: \"mcp\",\n        ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),\n        ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),\n      });\n\n      return {\n        decision_id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,\n        status: \"provisional\",\n        review_required: true,\n      };\n    }\n\n    case \"create_question\": {\n      const projectRecord = (args.context as { project?: string })?.project\n        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, projectInput: (args.context as { project: string }).project })\n        : undefined;\n      const featureRecord = (args.context as { feature?: string })?.feature\n        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, featureInput: (args.context as { feature: string }).feature })\n        : undefined;\n\n      const questionRecord = await createQuestionRecord({\n        surreal,\n        text: args.text as string,\n        status: \"open\",\n        now,\n        workspaceRecord: ws,\n        ...(args.category ? { category: args.category as string } : {}),\n        ...(args.priority ? { priority: args.priority as string } : {}),\n        ...(args.assigned_to ? { assignedToName: args.assigned_to as string } : {}),\n        ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),\n        ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),\n      });\n\n      return {\n        question_id: `question:${questionRecord.id as string}`,\n        status: \"open\",\n      };\n    }\n\n    case \"create_observation\": {\n      const relatedRecord = (args.related_entity_id as string | undefined)\n        ? parseRecordIdString(\n            args.related_entity_id as string,\n            [\"project\", \"feature\", \"task\", \"decision\", \"question\"],\n          ) as RecordId<\"project\" | \"feature\" | \"task\" | \"decision\" | \"question\", string>\n        : undefined;\n\n      if (relatedRecord) {\n        const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(surreal, ws, relatedRecord);\n        if (!scoped) {\n          throw new Error(\"related entity is outside the current workspace scope\");\n        }\n      }\n\n      const observationRecord = await createObservation({\n        surreal,\n        workspaceRecord: ws,\n        text: args.text as string,\n        severity: args.severity as \"info\" | \"warning\" | \"conflict\",\n        ...(args.category ? { category: args.category as EntityCategory } : {}),\n        sourceAgent: \"mcp\",\n        now,\n        sourceSessionRecord: sessionRecord,\n        ...(relatedRecord ? { relatedRecords: [relatedRecord] } : {}),\n      });\n\n      return {\n        observation_id: `observation:${observationRecord.id as string}`,\n        severity: args.severity,\n        status: \"open\",\n      };\n    }\n\n    case \"create_suggestion\": {\n      const targetTables = [\"project\", \"feature\", \"task\", \"question\", \"decision\"] as const;\n      const targetRecord = (args.target_entity_id as string | undefined)\n        ? parseRecordIdString(args.target_entity_id as string, [...targetTables])\n        : undefined;\n\n      if (targetRecord) {\n        const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(surreal, ws, targetRecord);\n        if (!scoped) {\n          throw new Error(\"target entity is outside the current workspace scope\");\n        }\n      }\n\n      const evidenceTables = [\"workspace\", \"project\", \"person\", \"feature\", \"task\", \"decision\", \"question\", \"observation\"] as const;\n      const evidenceRecords = (args.evidence_entity_ids as string[] | undefined)?.map((id) =>\n        parseRecordIdString(id, [...evidenceTables]),\n      );\n\n      const suggestionRecord = await createSuggestion({\n        surreal,\n        workspaceRecord: ws,\n        text: args.text as string,\n        category: args.category as SuggestionCategory,\n        rationale: args.rationale as string,\n        suggestedBy: \"mcp\",\n        confidence: args.confidence as number,\n        now,\n        sourceSessionRecord: sessionRecord,\n        ...(targetRecord ? { targetRecord } : {}),\n        ...(evidenceRecords ? { evidenceRecords } : {}),\n      });\n\n      return {\n        suggestion_id: `suggestion:${suggestionRecord.id as string}`,\n        text: args.text,\n        category: args.category,\n        rationale: args.rationale,\n        confidence: args.confidence,\n        status: \"pending\",\n        ...(args.target_entity_id ? { target: args.target_entity_id } : {}),\n      };\n    }\n\n    case \"create_work_item\":\n      return dispatchCreateWorkItem(args, surreal, ws);\n\n    case \"edit_work_item\":\n      return dispatchEditWorkItem(args, surreal, ws);\n\n    case \"move_items_to_project\":\n      return dispatchMoveItemsToProject(args, surreal, ws);\n\n    case \"acknowledge_observation\": {\n      const observationRecord = parseRecordIdString(\n        args.observation_id as string,\n        [\"observation\"],\n        \"observation\",\n      );\n      await acknowledgeObservation({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, observationRecord, now });\n      return {\n        observation_id: `observation:${observationRecord.id as string}`,\n        status: \"acknowledged\",\n      };\n    }\n\n    case \"resolve_observation\": {\n      const observationRecord = parseRecordIdString(\n        args.observation_id as string,\n        [\"observation\"],\n        \"observation\",\n      );\n      await resolveObservation({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, observationRecord, now });\n      return {\n        observation_id: `observation:${observationRecord.id as string}`,\n        status: \"resolved\",\n      };\n    }\n\n    case \"suggest_work_items\":\n      return dispatchSuggestWorkItems(args, surreal, ws);\n\n    default:\n      throw new Error(`Unknown brain write tool: ${toolName}`);\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Complex write tool helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst CLOSED_STATUSES = new Set([\"done\", \"completed\", \"closed\", \"resolved\", \"superseded\"]);\n\nasync function dispatchCreateWorkItem(\n  args: Record<string, unknown>,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  ws: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n) {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const kind = args.kind as \"task\" | \"feature\" | \"project\";\n  const title = args.title as string;\n  const rationale = args.rationale as string;\n\n  if (kind === \"project\") {\n    const [wsRow] = await surreal.query<[{ name: string } | undefined]>(\n      \"SELECT name FROM ONLY $ws;\",\n      { ws },\n    );\n    if (wsRow && title.toLowerCase().trim() === wsRow.name.toLowerCase().trim()) {\n      return {\n        error: `\"${title}\" is the workspace name, not a project. Create the user's described items as projects instead.`,\n      };\n    }\n\n    const projectRecord = await createProjectRecord({\n      surreal,\n      name: title,\n      status: \"active\",\n      now,\n      workspaceRecord: ws,\n    });\n\n    await seedDescriptionEntry({ surreal, targetRecord: projectRecord, text: rationale }).catch(() => undefined);\n\n    return {\n      entity_id: `project:${projectRecord.id as string}`,\n      kind: \"project\",\n      title,\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (kind === \"task\") {\n    const entityId = randomUUID();\n    const taskRecord = new RecordId(\"task\", entityId);\n    await surreal.create(taskRecord).content({\n      title,\n      status: \"open\",\n      workspace: ws,\n      ...(args.category ? { category: args.category } : {}),\n      ...(args.priority ? { priority: args.priority } : {}),\n      created_at: now,\n      updated_at: now,\n    });\n\n    if (args.project) {\n      try {\n        const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, projectInput: args.project as string });\n        await surreal.relate(taskRecord, new RecordId(\"belongs_to\", randomUUID()), projectRecord, { added_at: now }).output(\"after\");\n      } catch { /* project link failed, non-fatal */ }\n    }\n\n    if (args.feature) {\n      try {\n        const featureRecord = await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, featureInput: args.feature as string });\n        await surreal.relate(featureRecord, new RecordId(\"has_task\", randomUUID()), taskRecord, { added_at: now }).output(\"after\");\n        await surreal.relate(taskRecord, new RecordId(\"belongs_to\", randomUUID()), featureRecord, { added_at: now }).output(\"after\");\n      } catch { /* feature link failed, non-fatal */ }\n    }\n\n    await seedDescriptionEntry({ surreal, targetRecord: taskRecord, text: rationale }).catch(() => undefined);\n\n    return { entity_id: `task:${entityId}`, kind: \"task\", title };\n  }\n\n  // feature\n  const entityId = randomUUID();\n  const featureRecord = new RecordId(\"feature\", entityId);\n  await surreal.create(featureRecord).content({\n    name: title,\n    status: \"active\",\n    workspace: ws,\n    ...(args.category ? { category: args.category } : {}),\n    created_at: now,\n    updated_at: now,\n  });\n\n  if (args.project) {\n    try {\n      const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, projectInput: args.project as string });\n      await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(surreal, projectRecord, featureRecord, now);\n    } catch { /* project link failed, non-fatal */ }\n  }\n\n  await seedDescriptionEntry({ surreal, targetRecord: featureRecord, text: rationale }).catch(() => undefined);\n\n  return { entity_id: `feature:${entityId}`, kind: \"feature\", title };\n}\n\nasync function dispatchEditWorkItem(\n  args: Record<string, unknown>,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  ws: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n) {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const workItemRecord = parseRecordIdString(args.id as string, [\"task\", \"feature\", \"project\"]);\n\n  const inWorkspace = await isEntityInWorkspace(surreal, ws, workItemRecord);\n  if (!inWorkspace) {\n    throw new Error(`work item is not in workspace: ${args.id}`);\n  }\n\n  const tableName = workItemRecord.table.name;\n  if (tableName !== \"task\" && tableName !== \"feature\" && tableName !== \"project\") {\n    throw new Error(`unsupported entity type: ${tableName}`);\n  }\n\n  const patch: Record<string, unknown> = { updated_at: now };\n  const updatedFields: string[] = [];\n\n  if (args.title) {\n    if (tableName === \"task\") {\n      patch.title = args.title;\n    } else {\n      patch.name = args.title;\n    }\n    updatedFields.push(\"title\");\n  }\n\n  if (args.status) {\n    patch.status = args.status;\n    updatedFields.push(\"status\");\n  }\n\n  if (args.category) {\n    if (tableName === \"project\") throw new Error(\"category is not editable on project\");\n    patch.category = args.category;\n    updatedFields.push(\"category\");\n  }\n\n  if (args.priority) {\n    if (tableName !== \"task\") throw new Error(\"priority is only editable on task\");\n    patch.priority = args.priority;\n    updatedFields.push(\"priority\");\n  }\n\n  if (updatedFields.length === 0 && !args.rationale) {\n    throw new Error(\"at least one editable field must be provided\");\n  }\n\n  if (updatedFields.length > 0) {\n    await surreal.update(workItemRecord).merge(patch);\n  }\n\n  if (args.rationale) {\n    await seedDescriptionEntry({\n      surreal,\n      targetRecord: workItemRecord,\n      text: args.rationale as string,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const updatedName = await readEntityName(surreal, workItemRecord);\n\n  return {\n    entity_id: `${tableName}:${workItemRecord.id as string}`,\n    kind: tableName,\n    title: updatedName ?? (args.title as string) ?? args.id,\n    updated_fields: updatedFields,\n    ...(args.rationale ? { rationale_added: true } : {}),\n  };\n}\n\nasync function dispatchMoveItemsToProject(\n  args: Record<string, unknown>,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  ws: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n) {\n  const now = new Date();\n  const entityIds = args.entity_ids as string[];\n\n  let projectRecord: RecordId<\"project\", string>;\n  try {\n    projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({\n      surreal,\n      workspaceRecord: ws,\n      projectInput: args.target_project as string,\n    });\n  } catch {\n    return {\n      error: `Target project not found: ${args.target_project}`,\n      moved: [],\n      failed: entityIds.map((id) => ({ entity_id: id, reason: \"target project resolution failed\" })),\n    };\n  }\n\n  const moved: Array<{ entity_id: string; title: string }> = [];\n  const failed: Array<{ entity_id: string; reason: string }> = [];\n\n  for (const rawId of entityIds) {\n    try {\n      const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(rawId, [\"feature\", \"task\"]);\n      const table = entityRecord.table.name;\n\n      const inWorkspace = await isEntityInWorkspace(surreal, ws, entityRecord);\n      if (!inWorkspace) {\n        failed.push({ entity_id: rawId, reason: \"entity not found in workspace\" });\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const title = await readEntityName(surreal, entityRecord) ?? rawId;\n\n      if (table === \"feature\") {\n        await surreal.query(\"DELETE FROM has_feature WHERE out = $feature;\", { feature: entityRecord });\n        await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(surreal, projectRecord, entityRecord as RecordId<\"feature\", string>, now);\n        moved.push({ entity_id: rawId, title });\n      } else if (table === \"task\") {\n        await surreal.query(\"DELETE FROM belongs_to WHERE `in` = $task AND record::tb(out) = 'project';\", { task: entityRecord });\n        await surreal.relate(entityRecord, new RecordId(\"belongs_to\", randomUUID()), projectRecord, { added_at: now }).output(\"after\");\n        moved.push({ entity_id: rawId, title });\n      } else {\n        failed.push({ entity_id: rawId, reason: `unsupported entity type: ${table}` });\n      }\n    } catch (err) {\n      failed.push({ entity_id: rawId, reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"unknown error\" });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { moved, failed };\n}\n\nasync function dispatchSuggestWorkItems(\n  args: Record<string, unknown>,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  ws: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n) {\n  const items = args.items as Array<{\n    kind: \"task\" | \"feature\" | \"project\";\n    title: string;\n    rationale: string;\n    category?: string;\n    project?: string;\n    priority?: string;\n  }>;\n\n  const suggestions: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];\n  const updated: Array<{ existing_id: string; title: string; changes: string }> = [];\n  const discarded: Array<{ title: string; reason: string }> = [];\n\n  for (const item of items) {\n    const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({\n      surreal,\n      workspaceRecord: ws,\n      query: item.title,\n      kinds: [item.kind],\n      limit: 5,\n    });\n\n    const top = candidates[0];\n    if (!top) {\n      suggestions.push(item);\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const normalizedTopStatus = top.status ? top.status.trim().toLowerCase() : undefined;\n    if (normalizedTopStatus && CLOSED_STATUSES.has(normalizedTopStatus)) {\n      suggestions.push(item);\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const normalizedTopName = top.name.trim().toLowerCase();\n    const normalizedTitle = item.title.trim().toLowerCase();\n\n    if (normalizedTopName === normalizedTitle) {\n      discarded.push({ title: item.title, reason: `Exact duplicate of existing item ${top.kind}:${top.id} (${top.name})` });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    updated.push({\n      existing_id: `${top.kind}:${top.id}`,\n      title: top.name,\n      changes: `Merge context from suggested item '${item.title}'`,\n    });\n  }\n\n  return { suggestions, updated, discarded };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Public API\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Execute a Brain-native tool call.\n * Read tools are dispatched directly. Write tools assume authorization\n * has already been verified by the caller.\n */\nexport async function handleBrainToolCall(\n  toolName: string,\n  args: Record<string, unknown>,\n  isReadTool: boolean,\n  context: BrainToolCallContext,\n  deps: BrainToolCallDeps,\n): Promise<ToolCallResult> {\n  const ws = new RecordId(\"workspace\", context.workspaceId);\n\n  try {\n    const result = isReadTool\n      ? await dispatchReadTool(toolName, args, deps.surreal, ws)\n      : await dispatchWriteTool(toolName, args, deps.surreal, ws, context);\n\n    return { kind: \"success\", result };\n  } catch (error) {\n    return {\n      kind: \"error\",\n      code: -32000,\n      message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    };\n  }\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "/**\n * Resolve intents, compute scope, and classify tools for a session.\n * Shared between tools/list and tools/call handlers.\n */\nasync function resolveClassifiedTools(\n  context: AgentSessionContext,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  queryTools: QueryGrantedTools,\n  toolCache: ToolResolutionCache,\n): Promise<readonly ClassifiedTool[]> {\n  const intents = await querySessionIntents(surreal, context.sessionId);\n  const effectiveScope = computeEffectiveScope(intents);\n  const grantedTools = await resolveToolsForIdentity(\n    context.identityId,\n    context.workspaceId,\n    queryTools,\n    toolCache,\n  );\n  return classifyTools(grantedTools, effectiveScope, BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOL_NAMES);\n}",
  "new_string": "type ResolvedToolsAndScope = {\n  readonly classifiedTools: readonly ClassifiedTool[];\n  readonly effectiveScope: EffectiveScope;\n};\n\n/**\n * Resolve intents, compute scope, and classify tools for a session.\n * Shared between tools/list and tools/call handlers.\n */\nasync function resolveClassifiedTools(\n  context: AgentSessionContext,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  queryTools: QueryGrantedTools,\n  toolCache: ToolResolutionCache,\n): Promise<ResolvedToolsAndScope> {\n  const intents = await querySessionIntents(surreal, context.sessionId);\n  const effectiveScope = computeEffectiveScope(intents);\n  const grantedTools = await resolveToolsForIdentity(\n    context.identityId,\n    context.workspaceId,\n    queryTools,\n    toolCache,\n  );\n  const classifiedTools = classifyTools(grantedTools, effectiveScope, BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOL_NAMES);\n  return { classifiedTools, effectiveScope };\n}"
}

  "new_string": "async function handleToolsList(\n  context: AgentSessionContext,\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  queryTools: QueryGrantedTools,\n  toolCache: ToolResolutionCache,\n): Promise<unknown> {\n  const { classifiedTools, effectiveScope } = await resolveClassifiedTools(context, surreal, queryTools, toolCache);\n  const authorizedBrainWriteTools = computeAuthorizedBrainWriteTools(effectiveScope, BRAIN_WRITE_TOOL_NAMES);\n  return buildToolsList(classifiedTools, authorizedBrainWriteTools);\n}"
}

setupAcceptanceSuite(\"agent_mcp_brain_tools\", {\n  configOverrides: {\n    sandboxAgentEnabled: true,\n    sandboxAgentType: \"claude\",\n    orchestratorMockAgent: true,\n  },\n});\n\n// ── Test Helpers ──\n\nfunction mcpRequest(\n  baseUrl: string,\n  sessionId: string,\n  proxyToken: string,\n  method: string,\n  params?: Record<string, unknown>,\n): Promise<Response> {\n  return JSON.stringify({\n      jsonrpc: \"2.0\",\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      method,\n      params: params ?? {},\n    }),\n  });\n}\n\nasync function setupSessionContext(surreal: ReturnType<typeof getRuntime>[\"surreal\"], suffix: string) {\n  const ws = await createWorkspaceDirectly(surreal, `brain_tools_${suffix}`);\n  const session = await createAgentSessionDirectly(surreal, ws.workspaceId);\n  const proxyToken = await seedProxyToken(\n    surreal, ws.identityId, ws.workspaceId, { sessionId: session.sessionId },\n  );\n  return { ...ws, ...session, proxyToken };\n}\n\n// ── Tests ──\n\ndescribe(\"Brain-Native Tools in MCP Agent Endpoint\", () => {\n\n  // ── tools/list ──\n\n  it(\"tools/list includes all brain read tools without any mcpRequest(baseUrl, ctx.sessionId, ctx.proxyToken, \"tools/list\");\n    expect(response.ok).toBe(true);\n\n    const body = (await response.json()) as ToolsListResult;\n    const toolNames = body.result!.tools.map((t) => t.name);\n\n    // All read tools present\n    for (const name of BRAIN_READ_TOOL_NAMES) {\n      expect(toolNames).toContain(name);\n    }\n\n    // Infrastructure tools ## Summary

I've completed a comprehensive search of the codebase for MCP tool definitions. Here's what I found:

### Locations with MCP Tool Definitions

#### 1. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/brain-tool-definitions.ts`** (367 lines)
   - **Purpose**: Pure core file with canonical Brain-native MCP tool definitions
   - **Format**: JSON Schema objects (McpToolDefinition type)
   - **Tools defined**: 17 total
     - **Read tools** (7): search_entities, list_workspace_entities, get_entity_detail, get_project_status, get_conversation_history, check_constraints, resolve_decision
     - **Write tools** (10): create_provisional_decision, create_question, create_observation, create_suggestion, create_work_item, edit_work_item, move_items_to_project, acknowledge_observation, resolve_observation, suggest_work_items
   - **Exported as**:
     - `BRAIN_READ_TOOLS` (ReadonlySet)
     - `BRAIN_WRITE_TOOLS` (ReadonlySet)
     - `ALL_BRAIN_TOOL_NAMES` (Set with "get_context", "create_intent")

#### 2. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/mcp/tools-list-handler.ts`** (177 lines)
   - **Purpose**: Builds MCP ListToolsResult for classified tools + infrastructure tools
   - **Format**: JSON Schema objects (McpToolDefinition type)
   - **Tools defined locally**: 2
     - `CREATE_INTENT_TOOL`: create_intent (infrastructure)
     - `GET_CONTEXT_TOOL`: get_context (infrastructure)
   - **Tools referenced**: Imports BRAIN_READ_TOOLS, BRAIN_WRITE_TOOLS from brain-tool-definitions.ts
   - **Key logic**: `buildToolsList()` function composes all tools and applies gating descriptions

#### 3. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/cli/mcp-server.ts`** (368 lines)
   - **Purpose**: CLI MCP server with Zod-based tool registration
   - **Format**: Zod schema objects via `server.tool()` API (Anthropic SDK)
   - **Tools defined**: 23 total (overlapping with Brain tools + legacy tools)
     - Primary: get_context
     - Tier 1 (legacy read tools): get_workspace_context, get_project_context, get_task_context, get_active_decisions, get_task_dependencies, get_architecture_constraints, get_recent_changes, get_entity_detail, list_pending_suggestions
     - Tier 2 (reasoning): resolve_decision, check_constraints
     - Tier 3 (write): create_provisional_decision, ask_question, update_task_status, create_subtask, log_implementation_note, log_observation, create_suggestion, accept_suggestion, dismiss_suggestion, convert_suggestion
   - **Note**: This is a legacy/standalone CLI MCP server, separate from the HTTP server

#### 4. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/tools/`** (20 tool files)
   - **Purpose**: AI SDK tool implementations (Zod-based)
   - **Format**: Vercel `ai` SDK `tool()` wrapper with Zod inputSchema
   - **Files**: One per tool (search-entities.ts, create-provisional-decision.ts, etc.)
   - **Key pattern**: Each exports a factory function `create<ToolName>Tool(deps)` that returns an AI SDK tool object
   - **Relationship**: These are the runtime implementations that power the Brain tool definitions

#### 5. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/tool-registry/discovery.ts`**
   - **Purpose**: Tool discovery from external MCP servers
   - **Types defined**:
     - `RemoteTool`: camelCase (inputSchema, outputSchema, annotations)
     - `ExistingToolRecord`: snake_case (input_schema, output_schema)
   - **Tools stored**: In SurrealDB `mcp_tool` table (populated via discovery)

#### 6. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/tool-registry/types.ts`**
   - **Purpose**: Type definitions for MCP tool storage
   - **Key type**: `McpToolRecord` with snake_case fields (input_schema, output_schema)

#### 7. **`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/el-paso-v1/app/src/server/proxy/tool-injector.ts`** & **tool-resolver.ts**
   - **Purpose**: Tool resolution and injection for proxy requests
   - **Types**: `ResolvedTool` (from database can_use graph queries)

### Duplication Analysis

**Critical Duplication Points:**

1. **Brain-native tools are defined in TWO places with DIFFERENT formats**:
   - `brain-tool-definitions.ts`: JSON Schema (for agent MCP endpoints)
   - `cli/mcp-server.ts`: Zod schemas (for CLI MCP server)
   - Example: `create_provisional_decision` has parallel definitions in both files

2. **Infrastructure tools (get_context, create_intent)**:
   - Defined in `tools-list-handler.ts` (JSON Schema)
   - Also defined in `cli/mcp-server.ts` (Zod schema via server.tool)

3. **Tool implementations vs. definitions**:
   - `tools/*.ts`: Zod-based AI SDK tool implementations
   - `brain-tool-definitions.ts`: JSON Schema mirrors of the same tools
   - Comment in brain-tool-definitions.ts says "JSON Schema inputSchema mirrors the Zod schemas in app/src/server/tools/*.ts"

4. **External MCP tools**:
   - Discovered from remote MCP servers via `discovery.ts`
   - Stored in SurrealDB `mcp_tool` table (snake_case: input_schema)
   - Resolved at request time via can_use edges in `tool-resolver.ts`

### Summary Table

| Location | Format | Tool Count | Primary Tools |
|----------|--------|------------|---|
| brain-tool-definitions.ts | JSON Schema | 17 | All Brain-native tools |
| tools-list-handler.ts | JSON Schema | 2 | get_context, create_intent |
| cli/mcp-server.ts | Zod schemas | 23 | Legacy + Brain tools |
| tools/*.ts | Zod (AI SDK) | 17+ | Runtime implementations |
| SurrealDB mcp_tool | snake_case | N (dynamic) | External MCP server tools |

Three duplication spots:

1. `get_context` + `create_intent` defined in `tools-list-handler.ts` — should live in `brain-tool-definitions.ts`
2. `brain-tool-definitions.ts` hand-writes JSON Schema that mirrors Zod in `tools/*.ts` — should derive from Zod

> AGENT

Zod v4's `toJSONSchema` works and is already available (Zod v4 ships alongside v3 in the same package). This is the path.

Now the plan: instead of hand-writing JSON Schema in `brain-tool-definitions.ts`, export Zod schemas from each tool file and convert them to JSON Schema at import time. This makes the Zod schemas the single source of truth.

   144→      const result = await client.listSuggestions(input);
   145→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   146→    },
   147→  );
   148→
   149→  // =========================================================================
   150→  // Tier 2 — Reasoning Tools
   151→  // =========================================================================
   152→
   153→  server.tool(
   154→    "resolve_decision",
   155→    "Check if an existing decision in the graph answers your question. Use before making a new decision — the graph may already have the answer from a previous agent or human.",
   156→    {
   157→      question: z.string().describe("The question you need answered (e.g. 'Should this endpoint use REST or tRPC?')"),
   158→      options: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("Options you've identified"),
   159→      context: z
   160→        .object({
   161→          project: z.string().optional(),
   162→          feature: z.string().optional(),
   163→        })
   164→        .optional()
   165→        .describe("Project/feature scope to search within"),
   166→    },
   167→    async (input) => {
   168→      const result = await client.resolveDecision(input);
   169→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   170→    },
   171→  );
   172→
   173→  server.tool(
   174→    "check_constraints",
   175→    "Check if a proposed action conflicts with existing decisions or constraints. Returns hard conflicts, soft tensions, and supporting context.",
   176→    {
   177→      proposed_action: z.string().describe("What you're proposing to do (e.g. 'Add Redis dependency for caching')"),
   178→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Project scope"),
   179→    },
   180→    async (input) => {
   181→      const result = await client.checkConstraints(input);
   182→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   183→    },
   184→  );
   185→
   186→  // =========================================================================
   187→  // Tier 3 — Write Tools
   188→  // =========================================================================
   189→
   190→  server.tool(
   191→    "create_provisional_decision",
   192→    "Create a provisional decision when you make an implementation choice the graph doesn't cover. This surfaces for human review in the feed. Use after resolve_decision returns unresolved.",
   193→    {
   194→      name: z.string().describe("Concise decision name (e.g. 'Use token bucket for rate limiting')"),
   195→      rationale: z.string().describe("Why this decision was made"),
   196→      context: z
   197→        .object({
   198→          project: z.string().optional(),
   199→          feature: z.string().optional(),
   200→        })
   201→        .optional()
   202→        .describe("Project/feature this decision belongs to"),
   203→      options_considered: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("Other options that were considered"),
   204→    },
   205→    async (input) => {
   206→      const result = await client.createProvisionalDecision(input);
   207→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   208→    },
   209→  );
   210→
   211→  server.tool(
   212→    "ask_question",
   213→    "Ask a question when genuinely uncertain. Creates a question entity for human review in the feed. Better than guessing or creating a provisional decision when you don't know the answer.",
   214→    {
   215→      text: z.string().describe("The question"),
   216→      context: z
   217→        .object({
   218→          project: z.string().optional(),
   219→          feature: z.string().optional(),
   220→          task: z.string().optional(),
   221→        })
   222→        .optional()
   223→        .describe("Scope for the question"),
   224→      options: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("Options you've identified (helps human answer faster)"),
   225→      blocking_task: z.string().optional().describe("Task ID this question blocks (creates BLOCKS edge)"),
   226→    },
   227→    async (input) => {
   228→      const result = await client.askQuestion(input);
   229→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   230→    },
   231→  );
   232→
   233→  server.tool(
   234→    "update_task_status",
   235→    "Update a task's status. Triggers automatic subtask rollup on parent tasks.",
   236→    {
   237→      task_id: z.string().describe("Task ID"),
   238→      status: z.string().describe("New status: todo, in_progress, blocked, completed, done"),
   239→      notes: z.string().optional().describe("Optional notes about the status change"),
   240→    },
   241→    async (input) => {
   242→      const result = await client.updateTaskStatus(input);
   243→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   244→    },
   245→  );
   246→
   247→  server.tool(
   248→    "create_subtask",
   249→    "Create a subtask under a parent task. Use to break work into smaller pieces. Includes semantic dedup — returns existing subtask if a similar one already exists.",
   250→    {
   251→      parent_task_id: z.string().describe("Parent task ID to add subtask under"),
   252→      title: z.string().describe("Subtask title"),
   253→      category: z.string().optional().describe("Category (inherits from parent if not specified)"),
   254→      rationale: z.string().optional().describe("Why this subtask is needed"),
   255→    },
   256→    async (input) => {
   257→      const result = await client.createSubtask(input);
   258→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   259→    },
   260→  );
   261→
   262→  server.tool(
   263→    "log_implementation_note",
   264→    "Append an implementation note to any entity (decision, task, feature). Use to record what was implemented and how.",
   265→    {
   266→      entity_id: z.string().describe("Entity ID in table:id format (e.g. decision:abc123)"),
   267→      note: z.string().describe("What was implemented and how"),
   268→      files_changed: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("Paths of files touched"),
   269→    },
   270→    async (input) => {
   271→      const result = await client.logImplementationNote(input);
   272→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   273→    },
   274→  );
   275→
   276→  server.tool(
   277→    "log_observation",
   278→    "Log a codebase observation to the knowledge graph. Use when you notice contradictions between code and decisions, duplicated logic, missing implementations, deprecated patterns, recurring patterns, or anomalies. Creates an observation entity visible in the feed and to other agents.",
   279→    {
   280→      text: z.string().describe("What you observed — include file paths and specifics so a human can act on it"),
   281→      category: z
   282→        .enum(["contradiction", "duplication", "missing", "deprecated", "pattern", "anomaly"])
   283→        .describe(
   284→          "contradiction: code contradicts a decision or spec. duplication: same logic in multiple places. missing: expected thing is absent (tests, error handling, docs). deprecated: outdated dependency or pattern. pattern: recurring pattern worth noting. anomaly: something unexpected.",
   285→        ),
   286→      severity: z
   287→        .enum(["info", "warning", "conflict"])
   288→        .describe(
   289→          "info: awareness-level, no action needed. warning: risk that should be addressed. conflict: contradiction needing human resolution.",
   290→        ),
   291→      target: z.string().optional().describe("Entity this observation is about, in table:id format (e.g. decision:abc123, task:def456)"),
   292→      session_id: z.string().optional().describe("Current agent session ID to link this observation to"),
   293→    },
   294→    async (input) => {
   295→      const result = await client.logObservation(input);
   296→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   297→    },
   298→  );
   299→
   300→  server.tool(
   301→    "create_suggestion",
   302→    "Create a suggestion for the human to review. Use when you notice an optimization, risk, opportunity, conflict, missing element, or potential pivot that the user should consider. Suggestions surface in the feed for human review.",
   303→    {
   304→      text: z.string().describe("The suggestion itself — what you propose the user should consider or do"),
   305→      category: z
   306→        .enum(["optimization", "risk", "opportunity", "conflict", "missing", "pivot"])
   307→        .describe(
   308→          "optimization: improve existing approach. risk: potential problem. opportunity: beneficial possibility. conflict: contradictory elements. missing: gap in plan. pivot: direction change worth considering.",
   309→        ),
   310→      rationale: z.string().describe("Why you are making this suggestion — reasoning and evidence"),
   311→      confidence: z.number().describe("Confidence in this suggestion (0-1)"),
   312→      target_entity_id: z.string().optional().describe("Entity this is about (format: table:id, e.g. project:uuid)"),
   313→      evidence_entity_ids: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("Supporting entity IDs (format: table:id)"),
   314→      session_id: z.string().optional().describe("Current agent session ID"),
   315→    },
   316→    async (input) => {
   317→      const result = await client.createSuggestion(input);
   318→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   319→    },
   320→  );
   321→
   322→  server.tool(
   323→    "accept_suggestion",
   324→    "Accept a pending suggestion, marking it as approved for action.",
   325→    {
   326→      suggestion_id: z.string().describe("Suggestion ID (raw UUID, no table prefix)"),
   327→    },
   328→    async (input) => {
   329→      const result = await client.suggestionAction({ suggestion_id: input.suggestion_id, action: "accept" });
   330→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   331→    },
   332→  );
   333→
   334→  server.tool(
   335→    "dismiss_suggestion",
   336→    "Dismiss a suggestion that is not relevant or actionable.",
   337→    {
   338→      suggestion_id: z.string().describe("Suggestion ID (raw UUID, no table prefix)"),
   339→    },
   340→    async (input) => {
   341→      const result = await client.suggestionAction({ suggestion_id: input.suggestion_id, action: "dismiss" });
   342→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   343→    },
   344→  );
   345→
   346→  server.tool(
   347→    "convert_suggestion",
   348→    "Convert an accepted or pending suggestion into a task, feature, decision, or project entity in the knowledge graph.",
   349→    {
   350→      suggestion_id: z.string().describe("Suggestion ID (raw UUID, no table prefix)"),
   351→      convert_to: z
   352→        .enum(["task", "feature", "decision", "project"])
   353→        .describe("Entity type to convert the suggestion into"),
   354→      title: z.string().optional().describe("Override title for the created entity (defaults to suggestion text)"),
   355→    },
   356→    async (input) => {
   357→      const result = await client.convertSuggestion(input);
   358→      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
   359→    },
   360→  );
   361→
   362→  // =========================================================================
   363→  // Connect via stdio
   364→  // =========================================================================
   365→
   366→  const transport = new StdioServerTransport();
   367→  await server.connect(transport);
   368→}
   369→
```

Only `resolve-decision.ts` has an extracted schema (`resolveDecisionInputSchema`). All other tools have their Zod schemas inline in the `tool()` call. I need to extract them.

Rather than touching every tool file to extract schemas, a cleaner approach: create a shared `tool-schemas.ts` file that defines all the Zod schemas, then both the tool files and `brain-tool-definitions.ts` import from it.

Actually, the simplest approach that minimizes churn: create `brain-tool-definitions.ts` as a module that exports Zod schemas and derives JSON Schema from them. Tool files can optionally import from it later. The key win is: one definition, two formats.

Tool files in tools/*.ts should import from\n * here rather than re-defining schemas.\n *\n * Pure core: no IO, no DB, no side effects.\n */\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport z4 from ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// McpToolDefinition type (self-contained, no circular import)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport type McpToolDefinition = {\n  readonly name: string;\n  readonly description: string;\n  readonly inputSchema: Record<string, unknown>;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Zod → MCP definition helper\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction defineTool(\n  name: string,\n  description: string,\n  schema: z.ZodType,\n): McpToolDefinition {\n  const jsonSchema = z4.toJSONSchema(schema);\n  // Strip $schema key — MCP protocol doesn't use it\n  const { $schema: _, ...inputSchema } = jsonSchema as Record<string, unknown>;\n  return { name, description, inputSchema };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Shared schema fragments\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport const createIntentSchema = z.object({\n  goal: z.string().describe(\"What you want to accomplish with this tool\"),\n  reasoning: z.string().describe(\"Why this tool is needed for the current task\"),\n  action_spec: z.object({\n    provider: z.string().describe(\"The toolkit/provider name (e.g. github, brain)\"),\n    action: z.string().describe(\"The tool action name\"),\n    params: z.record(z.unknown()).optional().describe(\"Parameters for the tool call\"),\n  }),\n});\n\nexport const getContextSchema = z.object({\n  intent: z.string().describe(\"Description of what you are working on\"),\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Read tool schemas\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport const searchEntitiesSchema = z.object({\n  query: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Search query e.g. decision:abc123\"),\n});\n\nexport const getProjectStatusSchema = z.object({\n  projectId: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Project record ID or project name\"),\n});\n\nexport const scope\"),\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Write tool schemas\n// name\"),\n  rationale: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Why this decision was made\"),\n  context: projectFeatureContext,\n  options_considered: z.array(z.string().min(1)).optional(),\n});\n\nexport const z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe(\"critical: blocking/urgent. high: important. medium: normal. low: nice-to-have.\"),\n  context: projectFeatureContext,\n  assigned_to: making this suggestion\"),\n  confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1).describe(\"How confident you are (0-1)\"),\n  target_entity_id: z.string().optional().describe(\"Optional target entity (format: table:id)\"),\n  evidence_entity_ids: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe(\"Optional supporting entity IDs\"),\n});\n\nexport const createWorkItemSchema = z.object({\n  kind: z.enum([\"task\", editWorkItemSchema = z.object({\n  id: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Existing work item id in table:id format\"),\n  title: z.string().min(1).optional().describe(\"New title/name\"),\n  status: z.string().min(1).optional().describe(\"New status\"),\n  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),\n  priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional(),\n  rationale: z.string().min(1).optional().describe(\"Rationale note appended as a description entry\"),\n});\n\nexport const moveItemsToProjectSchema = z.object({\n  entity_ids: z.array(z.string().min(1)).min(1)\n    .describe(\"Polymorphic entity IDs to move, e.g. ['feature:uuid', 'task:uuid']\"),\n  target_project: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Target project name or 'project:uuid'\"),\n});\n\nexport const acknowledgeObservationSchema = z.object({\n  observation_id: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Observation record ID, e.g. observation:abc123\"),\n});\n\nexport const resolveObservationSchema = z.object({\n  observation_id: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Observation record ID, e.g. observation:abc123\"),\n});\n\nexport const suggestWorkItemsSchema = z.object({\n  items: z.array(z.object({\n    kind: z.enum([\"task\", \"feature\", \"project\"]),\n    title: z.string().min(1),\n    rationale: z.string().min(1),\n    category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),\n    project: z.string().optional(),\n    priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional(),\n  })).min(1).max(25),\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// MCP tool definitions (derived from Zod schemas)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Infrastructure\nexport const CREATE_INTENT_TOOL = defineTool(\n  \"create_intent\",\n  \"Create an intent to request authorization for a gated tool. \" +\n  \"Use this when a tool you need is marked as [GATED]. \" +\n  \"Provide the goal, reasoning, and action_spec describing the tool you want to use.\",\n  createIntentSchema,\n);\n\nexport const GET_CONTEXT_TOOL = defineTool(\n  \"get_context\",\n  \"Get project context from the Brain knowledge graph. \" +\n  \"Returns decisions, tasks, observations, and other entities relevant to your current work.\",\n  getContextSchema,\n);\n\nexport const BRAIN_INFRASTRUCTURE_TOOLS: readonly McpToolDefinition[] = [\n  CREATE_INTENT_TOOL,\n  GET_CONTEXT_TOOL,\n];\n\n// Read\nexport const BRAIN_READ_TOOLS: readonly McpToolDefinition[] = [\n  defineTool(\"search_entities\",\n    \"Full-text search across the knowledge graph.     83→ * Build a description for a gated tool that includes escalation instructions

    86→function buildGatedToolDescription(
    87→  originalDescription: string,
    88→  toolkit: string,
    89→  toolName: string,
    90→): string {
    91→  return (
    92→    `[GATED] This tool requires an approved intent. ` +
    93→    `Call create_intent with provider="${toolkit}" and action="${toolName}" to request authorization.\n\n` +
    94→    originalDescription
    95→  );
    96→}
    97→
    98→/** Convert a classified tool to an MCP tool definition. */
    99→function classifiedToolToDefinition(classified: ClassifiedTool): McpToolDefinition {
   100→  const { tool, classification } = classified;
   101→
   102→  switch (classification.kind) {
   103→    case "authorized":
   104→      return {
   105→        name: tool.name,
   106→        description: tool.description,
   107→        inputSchema: tool.input_schema,
   108→      };
   109→    case "gated":
   110→      return {
   111→        name: tool.name,
   112→        description: buildGatedToolDescription(tool.description, tool.toolkit, tool.name),
   113→        inputSchema: tool.input_schema,
   114→      };

   116→      // Brain-native tools from the classified list are replaced by canonical definitions
   117→      return BRAIN_NATIVE_TOOLS.find((t) => t.name === tool.name) ?? {
   118→        name: tool.name,
   119→        description: tool.description,
   120→        inputSchema: tool.input_schema,
   121→      };
   122→  }
   123→}
   124→
   125→/**
   126→ * Build gated description for a brain write tool.
   127→ */
   128→function buildBrainGatedToolDescription(
   129→  originalDescription: string,
   130→  toolName: string,
   131→): string {
   132→  return (
   133→    `[GATED] This tool requires an approved intent. ` +
   134→    `Call create_intent with provider="brain" and action="${toolName}" to request authorization.\n\n` +
   135→    originalDescription
   136→  );
   137→}
   138→
   139→/**
   140→ * Build the MCP ListToolsResult from classified tools.
   141→ *
   142→ * Pipeline:
   143→ *   classifiedTools -> filter out brain_native -> map to definitions
   144→ *   -> append brain read tools (always available)
   145→ *   -> append brain write tools (gated or authorized based on intent)
   146→ *   -> append infrastructure tools (get_context, create_intent)
   147→ */
   148→export function buildToolsList(
   149→  classifiedTools: readonly ClassifiedTool[],
   150→  authorizedBrainWriteTools: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(),
   151→): ListToolsResult {
   152→  // Convert granted tools (authorized + gated), excluding brain_native from the classified set
   153→  // because we append canonical brain-native definitions separately
   154→  const grantedToolDefinitions = classifiedTools
   155→    .filter((ct) => ct.classification.kind !== "brain_native")
   156→    .map(classifiedToolToDefinition);

   158→  // Brain write tools: gated or authorized based on intent

   161→      return tool; // authorized — show with clean description
   162→    }
   163→    return {
   164→      ...tool,
   165→      description: buildBrainGatedToolDescription(tool.description, tool.name),
   166→    };
   167→  });
   168→
   169→  return {
   170→    tools: [
   171→      ...grantedToolDefinitions,
   172→      ...BRAIN_READ_TOOLS,

name: tool.name,\n        description: buildGatedToolDescription(tool.description, tool.toolkit, tool.name),\n        inputSchema: tool.input_schema,\n      };\n    case \"brain_native\":\n      // Done. The consolidation:

1. **`brain-tool-definitions.ts`** is now the single source of truth — Zod schemas define all 19 tools (7 read + 10 write + 2 infrastructure). JSON Schema is derived via `z4.toJSONSchema()`, not hand-written.

2. **`tools-list-handler.ts`** no longer defines any tool schemas — it imports everything from `brain-tool-definitions.ts`. The `McpToolDefinition` type is re-exported for backwards compat.

3. **Exported Zod schemas** (`searchEntitiesSchema`, `createObservationSchema`, etc.) can now be imported by `tools/*.ts` files and `cli/mcp-server.ts` to eliminate their own schema definitions — that's a follow-up if desired.

Net result: -385 lines, +249 lines (net -136 lines of duplicated JSON Schema removed).
