```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
     5→import {
     6→  listEntityNeighbors,
     7→  parseRecordIdString,
     8→  type GraphEntityTable,
     9→  type SearchEntityKind,
    10→} from "../graph/queries";
    11→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    12→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    13→
    14→/** Core search logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */
    15→export async function executeSearchEntities(
    16→  surreal: Surreal,
    17→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    18→  input: { query: string; kinds?: SearchEntityKind[]; limit: number },
    19→) {
    20→  const results = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    21→    surreal,
    22→    workspaceRecord,
    23→    query: input.query,
    24→    ...(input.kinds ? { kinds: input.kinds } : {}),
    25→    limit: input.limit,
    26→  });
    27→
    28→  const enriched = await Promise.all(
    29→    results.map(async (row) => {
    30→      const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(row.id, [row.kind as GraphEntityTable], row.kind as GraphEntityTable);
    31→      const neighbors = await listEntityNeighbors({
    32→        surreal,
    33→        workspaceRecord,
    34→        entityRecord,
    35→        limit: 8,
    36→      });
    37→
    38→      return {
    39→        id: `${row.kind}:${row.id}`,
    40→        kind: row.kind,
    41→        name: row.name,
    42→        confidence: Number(row.score.toFixed(4)),
    43→        ...(row.status ? { status: row.status } : {}),
    44→        related: neighbors.slice(0, 4).map((neighbor) => ({
    45→          id: `${neighbor.kind}:${neighbor.id}`,
    46→          kind: neighbor.kind,
    47→          name: neighbor.name,
    48→          relation: neighbor.relationKind,
    49→        })),
    50→      };
    51→    }),
    52→  );
    53→
    54→  return { query: input.query, results: enriched };
    55→}
    56→
    57→export function createSearchEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    58→  return tool({
    59→    description:
    60→      "Full-text search across the knowledge graph. Use for finding entities by keyword or topic (e.g. \"authentication decisions\", \"payment tasks\"). For listing entities by kind or status, use list_workspace_entities instead.",
    61→    inputSchema: z.object({
    62→      query: z.string().min(1).describe("Search query (keywords matched via BM25 full-text search)"),
    63→      kinds: z.array(z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "suggestion"]))
    64→        .optional()
    65→        .describe("Optional filter by entity kinds"),
    66→      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(25).default(10).describe("Maximum number of results"),
    67→    }),
    68→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    69→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    70→      return executeSearchEntities(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, {
    71→        query: input.query,
    72→        kinds: input.kinds as SearchEntityKind[] | undefined,
    73→        limit: input.limit,
    74→      });
    75→    },
    76→  });
    77→}
    78→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import {
     5→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
     6→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
     7→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     9→} from "../graph/queries";
    10→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../observation/queries";
    11→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    12→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    13→
    14→const entityKindEnum = z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"]);
    15→
    16→/** Core logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */
    17→export async function executeListWorkspaceEntities(
    18→  surreal: Surreal,
    19→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    20→  input: { kind: string; status?: string; project?: string; limit: number },
    21→) {
    22→  const projectRecord = input.project
    23→    ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord, projectInput: input.project })
    24→    : undefined;
    25→
    26→  if (input.kind === "project") {
    27→    const projects = await listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });
    28→    return {
    29→      kind: "project",
    30→      count: projects.length,
    31→      entities: projects.map((p) => ({ id: `project:${p.id}`, name: p.name, activeTaskCount: p.activeTaskCount })),
    32→    };
    33→  }
    34→
    35→  if (input.kind === "task") {
    36→    return listTasks(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);
    37→  }
    38→
    39→  if (input.kind === "feature") {
    40→    return listFeatures(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  if (input.kind === "decision") {
    44→    const decisions = await listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });
    45→    const filtered = input.status ? decisions.filter((d) => d.status === input.status) : decisions;
    46→    return {
    47→      kind: "decision",
    48→      count: filtered.length,
    49→      entities: filtered.map((d) => ({
    50→        id: `decision:${d.id}`, name: d.name, status: d.status,
    51→        ...(d.priority ? { priority: d.priority } : {}),
    52→        ...(d.project ? { project: d.project } : {}),
    53→      })),
    54→    };
    55→  }
    56→
    57→  if (input.kind === "question") {
    58→    const questions = await listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });
    59→    return {
    60→      kind: "question",
    61→      count: questions.length,
    62→      entities: questions.map((q) => ({
    63→        id: `question:${q.id}`, name: q.name,
    64→        ...(q.priority ? { priority: q.priority } : {}),
    65→        ...(q.project ? { project: q.project } : {}),
    66→      })),
    67→    };
    68→  }
    69→
    70→  if (input.kind === "observation") {
    71→    const observations = await listWorkspaceOpenObservations({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });
    72→    return {
    73→      kind: "observation",
    74→      count: observations.length,
    75→      entities: observations.map((o) => ({
    76→        id: `observation:${o.id}`, text: o.text, severity: o.severity, status: o.status,
    77→        ...(o.category ? { category: o.category } : {}),
    78→        sourceAgent: o.sourceAgent,
    79→      })),
    80→    };
    81→  }
    82→
    83→  throw new Error(`unsupported entity kind: ${input.kind}`);
    84→}
    85→
    86→export function createListWorkspaceEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    87→  return tool({
    88→    description:
    89→      "List workspace entities by kind. Use this to answer questions about what entities exist (e.g. \"what decisions are there?\", \"list open questions\", \"show all tasks\"). For semantic search by topic, use search_entities instead.",
    90→    inputSchema: z.object({
    91→      kind: entityKindEnum.describe("Entity kind to list."),
    92→      status: z.string().optional().describe("Optional status filter, e.g. 'provisional', 'confirmed', 'open', 'done'."),
    93→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project name or ID to scope results. Only use when the user explicitly mentions a project."),
    94→      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).default(25).describe("Maximum number of results."),
    95→    }),
    96→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    97→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    98→      return executeListWorkspaceEntities(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, {
    99→        kind: input.kind,
   100→        status: input.status,
   101→        project: input.project,
   102→        limit: input.limit,
   103→      });
   104→    },
   105→  });
   106→}
   107→
   108→async function listTasks(
   109→  surreal: Surreal,
   110→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   111→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string> | undefined,
   112→  status: string | undefined,
   113→  limit: number,
   114→) {
   115→  const conditions = ["workspace = $workspace"];
   116→  const params: Record<string, unknown> = { workspace: workspaceRecord, limit };
   117→
   118→  if (projectRecord) {
   119→    conditions.push(
   120→      "(id IN (SELECT VALUE `in` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project)"
   121→      + " OR id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_task WHERE `in` IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_feature WHERE `in` = $project)))",
   122→    );
   123→    params.project = projectRecord;
   124→  }
   125→
   126→  if (status) {
   127→    conditions.push("status = $status");
   128→    params.status = status;
   129→  }
   130→
   131→  const query = [
   132→    "SELECT id, title, status, priority, created_at",
   133→    "FROM task",
   134→    `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}`,
   135→    "ORDER BY created_at DESC",
   136→    "LIMIT $limit;",
   137→  ].join(" ");
   138→
   139→  const [rows] = await surreal
   140→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task", string>; title: string; status: string; priority?: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>(query, params)
   141→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task", string>; title: string; status: string; priority?: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>();
   142→
   143→  return {
   144→    kind: "task",
   145→    count: rows.length,
   146→    entities: rows.map((t) => ({
   147→      id: `task:${(t.id.id as string)}`,
   148→      name: t.title,
   149→      status: t.status,
   150→      ...(t.priority ? { priority: t.priority } : {}),
   151→    })),
   152→  };
   153→}
   154→
   155→async function listFeatures(
   156→  surreal: Surreal,
   157→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   158→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string> | undefined,
   159→  status: string | undefined,
   160→  limit: number,
   161→) {
   162→  const conditions = ["workspace = $workspace"];
   163→  const params: Record<string, unknown> = { workspace: workspaceRecord, limit };
   164→
   165→  if (projectRecord) {
   166→    conditions.push("id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_feature WHERE `in` = $project)");
   167→    params.project = projectRecord;
   168→  }
   169→
   170→  if (status) {
   171→    conditions.push("status = $status");
   172→    params.status = status;
   173→  }
   174→
   175→  const query = [
   176→    "SELECT id, name, status, created_at",
   177→    "FROM feature",
   178→    `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}`,
   179→    "ORDER BY created_at DESC",
   180→    "LIMIT $limit;",
   181→  ].join(" ");
   182→
   183→  const [rows] = await surreal
   184→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string>; name: string; status: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>(query, params)
   185→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string>; name: string; status: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>();
   186→
   187→  return {
   188→    kind: "feature",
   189→    count: rows.length,
   190→    entities: rows.map((f) => ({
   191→      id: `feature:${(f.id.id as string)}`,
   192→      name: f.name,
   193→      status: f.status,
   194→    })),
   195→  };
   196→}
   197→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import {
     4→  parseRecordIdString,
     5→  readEntityName,
     6→  type GraphEntityRecord,
     7→} from "../graph/queries";
     8→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     9→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    10→import { type RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    11→
    12→type RankedMessage = {
    13→  id: string;
    14→  conversationId: string;
    15→  text: string;
    16→  score: number;
    17→};
    18→
    19→/** Core logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and MCP handler. */
    20→export async function executeGetConversationHistory(
    21→  surreal: Surreal,
    22→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    23→  input: { query: string },
    24→) {
    25→  const trimmed = input.query.trim();
    26→  if (trimmed.length === 0) return { query: input.query, results: [] };
    27→
    28→  const [messageRows] = await surreal
    29→    .query<[Array<{
    30→      id: RecordId<"message", string>;
    31→      conversation: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    32→      text: string;
    33→      score: number;
    34→    }>]>(
    35→      `SELECT id, conversation, text, search::score(1) AS score
    36→       FROM message
    37→       WHERE text @1@ $query
    38→       AND conversation IN (SELECT VALUE id FROM conversation WHERE workspace = $workspace)
    39→       ORDER BY score DESC
    40→       LIMIT 8;`,
    41→      { workspace: workspaceRecord, query: trimmed },
    42→    )
    43→    .collect<[Array<{
    44→      id: RecordId<"message", string>;
    45→      conversation: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    46→      text: string;
    47→      score: number;
    48→    }>]>();
    49→
    50→  const messages: RankedMessage[] = (messageRows ?? []).map((row) => ({
    51→    id: row.id.id as string,
    52→    conversationId: row.conversation.id as string,
    53→    text: row.text,
    54→    score: row.score,
    55→  }));
    56→
    57→  const enrichedMessages = await Promise.all(
    58→    messages.map(async (message) => {
    59→      const messageRecord = parseRecordIdString(message.id, ["message"], "message");
    60→      const [entityRows] = await surreal
    61→        .query<[Array<GraphEntityRecord>]>(
    62→          "SELECT VALUE out FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` = $message LIMIT 8;",
    63→          { message: messageRecord },
    64→        )
    65→        .collect<[Array<GraphEntityRecord>]>();
    66→
    67→      const linkedEntities = await Promise.all(
    68→        entityRows.map(async (entityRecord) => {
    69→          const table = entityRecord.table.name;
    70→          if (
    71→            table !== "workspace" &&
    72→            table !== "project" &&
    73→            table !== "person" &&
    74→            table !== "feature" &&
    75→            table !== "task" &&
    76→            table !== "decision" &&
    77→            table !== "question"
    78→          ) {
    79→            return undefined;
    80→          }
    81→
    82→          const name = await readEntityName(surreal, entityRecord);
    83→          if (!name) {
    84→            return undefined;
    85→          }
    86→
    87→          return {
    88→            id: `${table}:${entityRecord.id as string}`,
    89→            kind: table,
    90→            name,
    91→          };
    92→        }),
    93→      );
    94→
    95→      return {
    96→        messageId: `message:${message.id}`,
    97→        conversationId: `conversation:${message.conversationId}`,
    98→        excerpt: message.text.slice(0, 280),
    99→        confidence: Number(message.score.toFixed(4)),
   100→        linkedEntities: linkedEntities.filter((value) => value !== undefined),
   101→      };
   102→    }),
   103→  );
   104→
   105→  return {
   106→    query: input.query,
   107→    results: enrichedMessages,
   108→  };
   109→}
   110→
   111→export function createGetConversationHistoryTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
   112→  return tool({
   113→    description:
   114→      "Search past conversations for discussions about a topic and return relevant message excerpts with linked entities.",
   115→    inputSchema: z.object({
   116→      query: z.string().min(1).describe("Topic to search for"),
   117→      projectId: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope filter"),
   118→    }),
   119→    execute: async (input, options) => {
   120→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
   121→      return executeGetConversationHistory(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, input);
   122→    },
   123→  });
   124→}
   125→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
     5→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     6→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     7→
     8→function normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {
     9→  return new Set(
    10→    value
    11→      .toLowerCase()
    12→      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
    13→      .split(/\s+/)
    14→      .map((token) => token.trim())
    15→      .filter((token) => token.length > 2),
    16→  );
    17→}
    18→
    19→function hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {
    20→  for (const value of a) {
    21→    if (b.has(value)) return true;
    22→  }
    23→  return false;
    24→}
    25→
    26→/** Core logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */
    27→export async function executeCheckConstraints(
    28→  surreal: Surreal,
    29→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    30→  proposedAction: string,
    31→) {
    32→  const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    33→    surreal,
    34→    workspaceRecord,
    35→    query: proposedAction,
    36→    kinds: ["decision"],
    37→    limit: 14,
    38→  });
    39→
    40→  const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(proposedAction);
    41→  const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    42→  const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    43→  const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    44→
    45→  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    46→    const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);
    47→    const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);
    48→    const status = candidate.status?.toLowerCase();
    49→
    50→    if (candidate.kind === "decision" && overlap && (status === "contested" || status === "superseded")) {
    51→      hardConflicts.push({
    52→        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,
    53→        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)), reason: `Decision is marked ${status}.`,
    54→      });
    55→      continue;
    56→    }
    57→
    58→    if (candidate.kind === "decision" && candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {
    59→      supporting.push({
    60→        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,
    61→        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)), reason: "High-similarity decision aligns with proposed action.",
    62→      });
    63→      continue;
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {
    67→      softTensions.push({
    68→        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,
    69→        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    70→        reason: overlap
    71→          ? "Related decision/question may require consistency checks."
    72→          : "Semantically related context may be affected.",
    73→      });
    74→    }
    75→  }
    76→
    77→  return { hard_conflicts: hardConflicts, soft_tensions: softTensions, supporting, proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0 };
    78→}
    79→
    80→export function createCheckConstraintsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    81→  return tool({
    82→    description:
    83→      "Check if a proposed action conflicts with existing decisions or constraints. Returns hard conflicts, soft tensions, supporting context, and proceed flag.",
    84→    inputSchema: z.object({
    85→      proposed_action: z.string().min(1).describe("What is being proposed"),
    86→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    87→    }),
    88→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    89→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    90→      return executeCheckConstraints(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, input.proposed_action);
    91→    },
    92→  });
    93→}
    94→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import {
     5→  createDecisionRecord,
     6→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     7→  getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage,
     8→  parseRecordIdString,
     9→  resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord,
    10→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
    11→  type GraphEntityRecord,
    12→} from "../graph/queries";
    13→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
    14→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    15→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    16→
    17→/**
    18→ * Read-only resolve: BM25 search for matching decisions, no record creation.
    19→ * Used by the MCP agent endpoint where conversation/message context is unavailable.
    20→ */
    21→export async function executeResolveDecisionReadOnly(
    22→  surreal: Surreal,
    23→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
    24→  input: { question: string; project?: string; feature?: string },
    25→) {
    26→  const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    27→    surreal,
    28→    workspaceRecord,
    29→    query: input.question,
    30→    kinds: ["decision"],
    31→    limit: 10,
    32→  });
    33→
    34→  const decisionCandidates = candidates.filter((c) => c.kind === "decision");
    35→  const topDecision = decisionCandidates[0];
    36→
    37→  if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.92) {
    38→    return {
    39→      status: "resolved" as const,
    40→      decision: { id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`, name: topDecision.name },
    41→      confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
    42→      sources: [{ id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`, name: topDecision.name, score: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)) }],
    43→    };
    44→  }
    45→
    46→  if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.78) {
    47→    const sources = candidates.slice(0, 5);
    48→    return {
    49→      status: "likely_match" as const,
    50→      decision: { id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`, name: topDecision.name },
    51→      confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
    52→      rationale: `Top matching decision: ${topDecision.name}. Use create_provisional_decision to create a new decision if this doesn't match.`,
    53→      sources: sources.map((s) => ({ id: `${s.kind}:${s.id}`, name: s.name, score: Number(s.score.toFixed(4)) })),
    54→    };
    55→  }
    56→
    57→  return {
    58→    status: "unresolved" as const,
    59→    relatedContext: candidates.slice(0, 6).map((s) => ({
    60→      id: `${s.kind}:${s.id}`, kind: s.kind, name: s.name, score: Number(s.score.toFixed(4)),
    61→    })),
    62→    suggestProvisional: true,
    63→  };
    64→}
    65→
    66→const resolveDecisionInputSchema = z.object({
    67→  question: z.string().min(1).describe("The decision question"),
    68→  options: z.array(z.string().min(1)).optional().describe("Known options being considered"),
    69→  context: z
    70→    .object({
    71→      project: z.string().optional(),
    72→      feature: z.string().optional(),
    73→    })
    74→    .optional()
    75→    .describe("Optional project/feature scope for searching decisions"),
    76→});
    77→
    78→export function createResolveDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    79→  return tool({
    80→    description:
    81→      "Infer an answer to a decision question from graph context. Returns resolved/inferred/unresolved with rationale and sources.",
    82→    inputSchema: resolveDecisionInputSchema,
    83→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    84→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_decision", deps);
    85→
    86→      const projectRecord = input.context?.project
    87→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    88→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    89→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    90→            projectInput: input.context.project,
    91→          })
    92→        : undefined;
    93→
    94→      const featureRecord = input.context?.feature
    95→        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({
    96→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    97→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    98→            featureInput: input.context.feature,
    99→          })
   100→        : undefined;
   101→
   102→      const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
   103→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   104→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   105→        query: input.question,
   106→        kinds: ["decision"],
   107→        limit: 10,
   108→      });
   109→
   110→      const decisionCandidates = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === "decision");
   111→      const topDecision = decisionCandidates[0];
   112→
   113→      if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.92) {
   114→        const existingDecision = parseRecordIdString(topDecision.id, ["decision"], "decision");
   115→        const resolvedFrom = await getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage({
   116→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   117→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   118→          decisionRecord: existingDecision,
   119→        });
   120→
   121→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   122→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   123→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   124→          targetRecord: existingDecision,
   125→          now: new Date(),
   126→          confidence: Math.max(topDecision.score, 0.6),
   127→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   128→          fromText: input.question,
   129→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   130→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   131→          ...(resolvedFrom ? { resolvedFromRecord: resolvedFrom } : {}),
   132→        });
   133→
   134→        return {
   135→          status: "resolved",
   136→          decision: {
   137→            id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`,
   138→            name: topDecision.name,
   139→          },
   140→          confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
   141→          sources: [
   142→            {
   143→              id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`,
   144→              name: topDecision.name,
   145→              score: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
   146→            },
   147→          ],
   148→        };
   149→      }
   150→
   151→      if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.78) {
   152→        const now = new Date();
   153→        const sources = candidates.slice(0, 5);
   154→        const basedOn = sources
   155→          .map((source) => {
   156→            try {
   157→              return parseRecordIdString(source.id, [source.kind as "project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question"], source.kind as any);
   158→            } catch {
   159→              return undefined;
   160→            }
   161→          })
   162→          .filter((value): value is GraphEntityRecord => value !== undefined);
   163→
   164→        const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({
   165→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   166→          summary: input.question,
   167→          status: "inferred",
   168→          now,
   169→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   170→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   171→          rationale: `Inferred from existing graph context with ${sources.length} related entities.`,
   172→          ...(input.options && input.options.length > 0 ? { optionsConsidered: input.options } : {}),
   173→          ...(basedOn.length > 0 ? { basedOn } : {}),
   174→          inferredBy: "chat_agent",
   175→          decidedByName: "chat_agent",
   176→          ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
   177→          ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),
   178→        });
   179→
   180→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   181→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   182→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   183→          targetRecord: decisionRecord,
   184→          now,
   185→          confidence: Math.max(topDecision.score, 0.6),
   186→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   187→          fromText: input.question,
   188→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   189→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   190→        });
   191→
   192→        return {
   193→          status: "inferred",
   194→          decision: {
   195→            id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
   196→            name: input.question,
   197→          },
   198→          confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
   199→          rationale: `Top matching decision: ${topDecision.name}. Related context was strong enough to infer a recommendation.`,
   200→          sources: sources.map((source) => ({
   201→            id: `${source.kind}:${source.id}`,
   202→            name: source.name,
   203→            score: Number(source.score.toFixed(4)),
   204→          })),
   205→        };
   206→      }
   207→
   208→      return {
   209→        status: "unresolved",
   210→        relatedContext: candidates.slice(0, 6).map((source) => ({
   211→          id: `${source.kind}:${source.id}`,
   212→          kind: source.kind,
   213→          name: source.name,
   214→          score: Number(source.score.toFixed(4)),
   215→        })),
   216→        suggestProvisional: true,
   217→      };
   218→    },
   219→  });
   220→}
   221→
```

```
     9→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    10→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    11→
    12→export function createCreateProvisionalDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    13→  return tool({
    14→    description:
    15→      "Create a provisional decision when no existing answer exists. Use after resolve_decision returns unresolved and the user wants to proceed.",
    16→    inputSchema: z.object({
    17→      name: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise decision name"),
    18→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this decision was made"),
    19→      context: z
    20→        .object({
    21→          project: z.string().optional(),
    22→          feature: z.string().optional(),
    23→        })
    24→        .optional(),
    25→      options_considered: z.array(z.string().min(1)).optional(),
    26→    }),
    27→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    28→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_decision", deps);
    29→      const now = new Date();
    30→
    31→      const projectRecord = input.context?.project
    32→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    33→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    34→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    35→            projectInput: input.context.project,
    36→          })
    37→        : undefined;
    38→
    39→      const featureRecord = input.context?.feature
    40→        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({
    41→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    42→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    43→            featureInput: input.context.feature,
    44→          })
    45→        : undefined;
    46→
    47→      const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({
    48→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    49→        summary: input.name,
    50→        status: "provisional",
    51→        now,
    52→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    53→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    54→        rationale: input.rationale,
    55→        ...(input.options_considered && input.options_considered.length > 0
    56→          ? { optionsConsidered: input.options_considered }
    57→          : {}),
    58→        decidedByName: "chat_agent",
    59→        ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    60→        ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),
    61→      });
    62→
    63→      await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    64→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    65→        sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    66→        targetRecord: decisionRecord,
    67→        now,
    68→        confidence: 0.9,
    69→        model: deps.extractionModelId,
    70→        fromText: input.name,
    71→        evidence: context.latestUserText,
    72→        evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    73→      });
    74→
    75→      return {
    76→        decision_id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
    77→        status: "provisional",
    78→        review_required: true,
    79→      };
    80→    },
    81→  });
    82→}
    83→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     5→import { isEntityInWorkspace, parseRecordIdString } from "../graph/queries";
     6→import { createObservation } from "../observation/queries";
     7→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
     8→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     9→
    10→export function createCreateObservationTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    11→  return tool({
    12→    description:
    13→      "Create an observation in the workspace graph. Use proactively when you notice cross-cutting concerns — risks, conflicts, gaps, or notable facts — even if the user didn't ask.",
    14→    inputSchema: z.object({
    15→      text: z.string().min(1).describe("Observation text"),
    16→      severity: z
    17→        .enum(["info", "warning", "conflict"])
    18→        .describe(
    19→          "info: awareness-level signals. warning: risks to address soon. conflict: contradictions needing human resolution.",
    20→        ),
    21→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional observation category"),
    22→      related_entity_id: z
    23→        .string()
    24→        .optional()
    25→        .describe("Optional related entity id (project/feature/task/decision/question)"),
    26→    }),
    27→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    28→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_observation", deps);
    29→
    30→      const relatedRecord = input.related_entity_id
    31→        ? parseRecordIdString(
    32→            input.related_entity_id,
    33→            ["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"],
    34→          ) as RecordId<"project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question", string>
    35→        : undefined;
    36→
    37→      if (relatedRecord) {
    38→        const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, relatedRecord);
    39→        if (!scoped) {
    40→          throw new Error("related entity is outside the current workspace scope");
    41→        }
    42→      }
    43→
    44→      const observationRecord = await createObservation({
    45→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    46→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    47→        text: input.text,
    48→        severity: input.severity,
    49→        ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
    50→        sourceAgent: context.actor,
    51→        now: new Date(),
    52→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    53→        ...(relatedRecord ? { relatedRecords: [relatedRecord] } : {}),
    54→      });
    55→
    56→      return {
    57→        observation_id: `observation:${observationRecord.id as string}`,
    58→        severity: input.severity,
    59→        status: "open",
    60→      };
    61→    },
    62→  });
    63→}
    64→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";

     3→import { SUGGESTION_CATEGORIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { isEntityInWorkspace, parseRecordIdString } from "../graph/queries";
     5→import { createSuggestion } from "../suggestion/queries";
     6→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
     7→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     8→
     9→export function createCreateSuggestionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    10→  return tool({
    11→    description:
    12→      "Create a suggestion for the user — a proactive, actionable proposal with rationale. Use when you identify optimizations, risks, opportunities, conflicts, missing elements, or potential pivots that the user should consider. Observations are for agent-to-agent signals; suggestions are agent-to-human proposals.",
    13→    inputSchema: z.object({
    14→      text: z.string().min(1).describe("The suggestion itself — what you propose the user should consider or do"),
    15→      category: z
    16→        .enum(SUGGESTION_CATEGORIES)
    17→        .describe(
    18→          "optimization: improve existing approach. risk: potential problem to address. opportunity: beneficial possibility. conflict: contradictory elements detected. missing: gap in current plan. pivot: fundamental direction change worth considering.",
    19→        ),
    20→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why you are making this suggestion — the reasoning and evidence behind it"),
    21→      confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1).describe("How confident you are in this suggestion (0-1)"),
    22→      target_entity_id: z
    23→        .string()
    24→        .optional()
    25→        .describe("Optional target entity this suggestion is about (format: table:id, e.g. project:uuid or feature:uuid)"),
    26→      evidence_entity_ids: z
    27→        .array(z.string())
    28→        .optional()
    29→        .describe("Optional entity IDs that support the rationale (format: table:id). Can include observations, decisions, tasks, etc."),
    30→    }),
    31→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    32→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_suggestion", deps);
    33→
    34→      const targetTables = ["project", "feature", "task", "question", "decision"] as const;
    35→      const targetRecord = input.target_entity_id
    36→        ? parseRecordIdString(input.target_entity_id, [...targetTables])
    37→        : undefined;
    38→
    39→      if (targetRecord) {
    40→        const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, targetRecord);
    41→        if (!scoped) {
    42→          throw new Error("target entity is outside the current workspace scope");
    43→        }
    44→      }
    45→
    46→      const evidenceTables = ["workspace", "project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"] as const;
    47→      const evidenceRecords = input.evidence_entity_ids?.map((id) =>
    48→        parseRecordIdString(id, [...evidenceTables]),
    49→      );
    50→
    51→      const suggestionRecord = await createSuggestion({
    52→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    53→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    54→        text: input.text,
    55→        category: input.category,
    56→        rationale: input.rationale,
    57→        suggestedBy: context.actor,
    58→        confidence: input.confidence,
    59→        now: new Date(),
    60→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    61→        ...(targetRecord ? { targetRecord } : {}),
    62→        ...(evidenceRecords ? { evidenceRecords } : {}),
    63→      });
    64→
    65→      return {
    66→        suggestion_id: `suggestion:${suggestionRecord.id as string}`,
    67→        text: input.text,
    68→        category: input.category,
    69→        rationale: input.rationale,
    70→        confidence: input.confidence,
    71→        status: "pending",
    72→        ...(input.target_entity_id ? { target: input.target_entity_id } : {}),
    73→      };
    74→    },
    75→  });
    76→}
    77→
```

```
    18→
    19→export function createCreateWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    20→  return tool({
    21→    description:
    22→      "Create a task, feature, or project directly in the knowledge graph. Use when the user explicitly requests creation (\"add a task for X\") or during onboarding entity seeding. For uncertain/brainstormed items, prefer suggest_work_items instead.",
    23→    inputSchema: z.object({
    24→      kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe(
    25→        "project: named product area or workstream (MUST create before features/tasks can belong to it). feature: capability within an existing project. task: concrete executable work with action verb. If no projects exist yet, create a project first.",
    26→      ),
    27→      title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise entity title"),
    28→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this entity is needed — seeds the description"),
    29→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Category classification"),
    30→      priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important, needs attention soon. medium: normal priority. low: nice-to-have, deferred."),
    31→      project: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("Project name to scope the entity under"),
    32→      feature: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("Feature name or record id to scope a task under"),
    33→    }),
    34→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    35→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_task", deps);
    36→      const now = new Date();
    37→
    38→      const entityId = randomUUID();
    39→
    40→      // Hard guard: never create a project with the same name as the workspace
    41→      if (input.kind === "project") {
    42→        const [ws] = await deps.surreal.query<[{ name: string } | undefined]>(
    43→          "SELECT name FROM ONLY $ws;",
    44→          { ws: context.workspaceRecord },
    45→        );
    46→        if (ws && input.title.toLowerCase().trim() === ws.name.toLowerCase().trim()) {
    47→          return {
    48→            error: `"${input.title}" is the workspace name, not a project. Create the user's described items (e.g. Dashboard, Inventory) as projects instead.`,
    49→          };
    50→        }
    51→
    52→        const projectRecord = await createProjectRecord({
    53→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    54→          name: input.title,
    55→          status: "active",
    56→          now,
    57→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    58→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    59→        });
    60→
    61→        await seedDescriptionEntry({
    62→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    63→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    64→          text: input.rationale,
    65→        }).catch(() => undefined);
    66→
    67→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    68→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    69→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    70→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    71→          now,
    72→          confidence: 0.95,
    73→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
    74→          fromText: input.title,
    75→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
    76→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    77→        });
    78→
    79→        return {
    80→          entity_id: `project:${projectRecord.id as string}`,
    81→          kind: "project" as const,
    82→          title: input.title,
    83→        };
    84→      }
    85→
    86→      if (input.kind === "task") {
    87→        const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", entityId);
    88→        await deps.surreal.create(taskRecord).content({
    89→          title: input.title,
    90→          status: "open",
    91→          workspace: context.workspaceRecord,
    92→          ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
    93→          ...(input.priority ? { priority: input.priority } : {}),
    94→          created_at: now,
    95→          updated_at: now,
    96→        });
    97→
    98→        if (input.project) {
    99→          try {
   100→            const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
   101→              surreal: deps.surreal,
   102→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   103→              projectInput: input.project,
   104→            });
   105→            await deps.surreal
   106→              .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {
   107→                added_at: now,
   108→              })
   109→              .output("after");
   110→          } catch (err) {
   111→            log.error("create_work_item", `failed to link task to project "${input.project}"`, err);
   112→          }
   113→        }
   114→
   115→        if (input.feature) {
   116→          try {
   117→            const featureRecord = await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({

   135→            log.error("create_work_item", `failed to link task to feature "${input.feature}"`, err);
   136→          }
   137→        }
   138→
   139→        await seedDescriptionEntry({
   140→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   141→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   142→          text: input.rationale,
   143→        }).catch(() => undefined);
   144→
   145→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   146→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   147→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   148→          targetRecord: taskRecord,

   150→          confidence: 0.95,
   151→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   152→          fromText: input.title,
   153→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   154→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   155→        });

   161→        };
   162→      }
   163→
   164→      // feature
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import {
     5→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     6→  isEntityInWorkspace,
     7→  parseRecordIdString,
     8→  readEntityName,
     9→} from "../graph/queries";
    10→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    11→import { seedDescriptionEntry } from "../descriptions/persist";
    12→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    13→
    14→const editableWorkItemTableSchema = z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]);
    15→
    16→export function createEditWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    17→  return tool({
    18→    description:
    19→      "Edit an existing task/feature/project by id. Use for rename or metadata updates. Do NOT use this to create new entities.",
    20→    inputSchema: z.object({
    21→      id: z.string().min(1).describe("Existing work item id in table:id format (task:..., feature:..., project:...)"),
    22→      title: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("New title/name"),
    23→      status: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("Optional new status"),
    24→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional category (task/feature only)"),
    25→      priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("Optional priority (task only)"),
    26→      rationale: z.string().min(1).optional().describe("Optional rationale note appended as a description entry"),
    27→    }),
    28→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    29→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_task", deps);
    30→      const now = new Date();
    31→      const workItemRecord = parseRecordIdString(input.id, ["task", "feature", "project"]);
    32→
    33→      const inWorkspace = await isEntityInWorkspace(
    34→        deps.surreal,
    35→        context.workspaceRecord,
    36→        workItemRecord,
    37→      );
    38→      if (!inWorkspace) {
    39→        throw new Error(`work item is not in workspace: ${input.id}`);
    40→      }
    41→
    42→      const tableName = editableWorkItemTableSchema.parse(workItemRecord.table.name);
    43→      const patch: Record<string, unknown> = { updated_at: now };
    44→      const updatedFields: string[] = [];
    45→
    46→      if (input.title) {
    47→        if (tableName === "task") {
    48→          patch.title = input.title;
    49→        } else {
    50→          patch.name = input.title;
    51→        }
    52→        updatedFields.push("title");
    53→      }
    54→
    55→      if (input.status) {
    56→        patch.status = input.status;
    57→        updatedFields.push("status");
    58→      }
    59→
    60→      if (input.category) {
    61→        if (tableName === "project") {
    62→          throw new Error("category is not editable on project");
    63→        }
    64→        patch.category = input.category;
    65→        updatedFields.push("category");
    66→      }
    67→
    68→      if (input.priority) {
    69→        if (tableName !== "task") {
    70→          throw new Error("priority is only editable on task");
    71→        }
    72→        patch.priority = input.priority;
    73→        updatedFields.push("priority");
    74→      }
    75→
    76→      if (updatedFields.length === 0 && !input.rationale) {
    77→        throw new Error("at least one editable field must be provided");
    78→      }
    79→
    80→      if (updatedFields.length > 0) {
    81→        await deps.surreal.update(workItemRecord).merge(patch);
    82→      }
    83→
    84→      if (input.rationale) {
    85→        await seedDescriptionEntry({
    86→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    87→          targetRecord: workItemRecord,
    88→          text: input.rationale,
    89→        });
    90→      }
    91→
    92→      await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    93→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    94→        sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    95→        targetRecord: workItemRecord,
    96→        now,
    97→        confidence: 0.95,
    98→        model: deps.extractionModelId,
    99→        fromText: input.title ?? input.id,
   100→        evidence: context.latestUserText,
   101→        evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   102→      });
   103→
   104→      const updatedName = await readEntityName(deps.surreal, workItemRecord);
   105→
   106→      return {
   107→        entity_id: `${tableName}:${workItemRecord.id as string}`,
   108→        kind: tableName,
   109→        title: updatedName ?? input.title ?? input.id,
   110→        updated_fields: updatedFields,
   111→        ...(input.rationale ? { rationale_added: true } : {}),
   112→      };
   113→    },
   114→  });
   115→}
   116→
```

```
     1→import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { tool } from "ai";
     4→import { z } from "zod";
     5→import {
     6→  parseRecordIdString,
     7→  readEntityName,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     9→  isEntityInWorkspace,
    10→} from "../graph/queries";
    11→import { ensureProjectFeatureEdge } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    12→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    13→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    14→
    15→type MoveResult = {
    16→  entity_id: string;
    17→  title: string;
    18→};
    19→
    20→type MoveFailure = {
    21→  entity_id: string;
    22→  reason: string;
    23→};
    24→
    25→export function createMoveItemsToProjectTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    26→  return tool({
    27→    description:
    28→      "Move existing features or tasks to a different project. Use when reorganizing — e.g. user says items belong under a different project. Deletes the old project edge and creates a new one. Does NOT duplicate entities.",
    29→    inputSchema: z.object({
    30→      entity_ids: z
    31→        .array(z.string().min(1))
    32→        .min(1)
    33→        .describe("Polymorphic entity IDs to move, e.g. ['feature:uuid', 'task:uuid']"),
    34→      target_project: z
    35→        .string()
    36→        .min(1)
    37→        .describe("Target project name or 'project:uuid' to move entities into"),
    38→    }),
    39→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    40→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_task", deps);
    41→      const now = new Date();
    42→
    43→      let projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string>;
    44→      try {
    45→        projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    46→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    47→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    48→          projectInput: input.target_project,
    49→        });
    50→      } catch (err) {
    51→        return {
    52→          error: `Target project not found: ${input.target_project}`,
    53→          moved: [],
    54→          failed: input.entity_ids.map((id) => ({ entity_id: id, reason: "target project resolution failed" })),
    55→        };
    56→      }
    57→
    58→      const moved: MoveResult[] = [];
    59→      const failed: MoveFailure[] = [];
    60→
    61→      for (const rawId of input.entity_ids) {
    62→        try {
    63→          const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(rawId, ["feature", "task"]);
    64→          const table = entityRecord.table.name;
    65→
    66→          const inWorkspace = await isEntityInWorkspace(
    67→            deps.surreal,
    68→            context.workspaceRecord,
    69→            entityRecord,
    70→          );
    71→          if (!inWorkspace) {
    72→            failed.push({ entity_id: rawId, reason: "entity not found in workspace" });
    73→            continue;
    74→          }
    75→
    76→          const title = await readEntityName(deps.surreal, entityRecord) ?? rawId;
    77→
    78→          if (table === "feature") {
    79→            // Delete old has_feature edges (project → feature)
    80→            await deps.surreal.query(
    81→              "DELETE FROM has_feature WHERE out = $feature;",
    82→              { feature: entityRecord },
    83→            );
    84→
    85→            // Create new has_feature edge to target project
    86→            await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(
    87→              deps.surreal,
    88→              projectRecord,
    89→              entityRecord as RecordId<"feature", string>,
    90→              now,
    91→            );
    92→
    93→            moved.push({ entity_id: rawId, title });
    94→          } else if (table === "task") {
    95→            // Delete old belongs_to edges to projects only (preserve feature edges)
    96→            await deps.surreal.query(
    97→              "DELETE FROM belongs_to WHERE `in` = $task AND record::tb(out) = 'project';",
    98→              { task: entityRecord },
    99→            );
   100→
   101→            // Create new belongs_to edge to target project
   102→            await deps.surreal
   103→              .relate(
   104→                entityRecord,
   105→                new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()),
   106→                projectRecord,
   107→                { added_at: now },
   108→              )
   109→              .output("after");
   110→
   111→            moved.push({ entity_id: rawId, title });
   112→          } else {
   113→            failed.push({ entity_id: rawId, reason: `unsupported entity type: ${table}` });
   114→          }
   115→        } catch (err) {
   116→          failed.push({
   117→            entity_id: rawId,
   118→            reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "unknown error",
   119→          });
   120→        }
   121→      }
   122→
   123→      return { moved, failed };
   124→    },
   125→  });
   126→}
   127→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { parseRecordIdString } from "../graph/queries";
     4→import { acknowledgeObservation } from "../observation/queries";
     5→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
     6→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     7→
     8→export function createAcknowledgeObservationTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
     9→  return tool({
    10→    description:
    11→      "Mark an observation as acknowledged — reviewed but still needs resolution. Transitions from open to acknowledged.",
    12→    inputSchema: z.object({
    13→      observation_id: z.string().min(1).describe("Observation record ID, e.g. observation:abc123"),
    14→    }),
    15→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    16→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "acknowledge_observation", deps);
    17→      const observationRecord = parseRecordIdString(input.observation_id, ["observation"], "observation");
    18→
    19→      await acknowledgeObservation({
    20→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    21→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    22→        observationRecord,
    23→        now: new Date(),
    24→      });
    25→
    26→      return {
    27→        observation_id: `observation:${observationRecord.id as string}`,
    28→        status: "acknowledged",
    29→      };
    30→    },
    31→  });
    32→}
    33→
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { parseRecordIdString } from "../graph/queries";
     4→import { resolveObservation } from "../observation/queries";
     5→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
     6→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     7→
     8→export function createResolveObservationTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
     9→  return tool({
    10→    description:
    11→      "Resolve an observation — the concern has been addressed. Transitions from open or acknowledged to resolved.",
    12→    inputSchema: z.object({
    13→      observation_id: z.string().min(1).describe("Observation record ID, e.g. observation:abc123"),
    14→    }),
    15→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    16→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "resolve_observation", deps);
    17→      const observationRecord = parseRecordIdString(input.observation_id, ["observation"], "observation");
    18→
    19→      await resolveObservation({
    20→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    21→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    22→        observationRecord,
    23→        now: new Date(),
    24→        ...(context.workspaceOwnerRecord ? { resolvedByRecord: context.workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
    25→      });
    26→
    27→      return {
    28→        observation_id: `observation:${observationRecord.id as string}`,
    29→        status: "resolved",
    30→      };
    31→    },
    32→  });
    33→}
```

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type EntityCategory } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
     5→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
     6→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     7→
     8→type SuggestedWorkItem = {
     9→  kind: "task" | "feature" | "project";
    10→  title: string;
    11→  rationale: string;
    12→  category?: EntityCategory;
    13→  project?: string;
    14→  priority?: string;
    15→  possible_duplicate?: {
    16→    id: string;
    17→    name: string;
    18→    similarity: number;
    19→  };
    20→};
    21→
    22→type UpdatedWorkItem = {
    23→  existing_id: string;
    24→  title: string;
    25→  changes: string;
    26→};
    27→
    28→type DiscardedWorkItem = {
    29→  title: string;
    30→  reason: string;
    31→};
    32→
    33→const CLOSED_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "completed", "closed", "resolved", "superseded"]);
    34→
    35→export function createSuggestWorkItemsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    36→  return tool({
    37→    description:
    38→      "Process a batch of proposed work items and return issue-style PM triage buckets: suggestions, updated, discarded.",
    39→    inputSchema: z.object({
    40→      items: z.array(
    41→        z.object({
    42→          kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe("project: named product area (MUST exist before features/tasks). feature: capability within an existing project. task: concrete executable work."),
    43→          title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise work item title"),
    44→          rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this work item is needed"),
    45→          category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional work item category"),
    46→          project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    47→          priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important. medium: normal. low: nice-to-have."),
    48→        }),
    49→      ).min(1).max(25),
    50→    }),
    51→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    52→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_suggestion", deps);
    53→      const suggestions: SuggestedWorkItem[] = [];
    54→      const updated: UpdatedWorkItem[] = [];
    55→      const discarded: DiscardedWorkItem[] = [];
    56→
    57→      for (const item of input.items) {
    58→        const suggestion: SuggestedWorkItem = {
    59→          kind: item.kind,
    60→          title: item.title,
    61→          rationale: item.rationale,
    62→          ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),
    63→          ...(item.project ? { project: item.project } : {}),
    64→          ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),
    65→        };
    66→
    67→        // BM25 text search for duplicate detection -- replaces embedding similarity
    68→        const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    69→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    70→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    71→          query: item.title,
    72→          kinds: [item.kind],
    73→          limit: 5,
    74→        });
    75→
    76→        const top = candidates[0];
    77→        if (!top) {
    78→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    79→          continue;
    80→        }
    81→
    82→        const normalizedTopStatus = top.status ? top.status.trim().toLowerCase() : undefined;
    83→        if (normalizedTopStatus && CLOSED_STATUSES.has(normalizedTopStatus)) {
    84→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    85→          continue;
    86→        }
    87→
    88→        // BM25 duplicate detection: exact title match = discard, partial match = merge
    89→        const normalizedTopName = top.name.trim().toLowerCase();
    90→        const normalizedTitle = item.title.trim().toLowerCase();
    91→
    92→        if (normalizedTopName === normalizedTitle) {
    93→          discarded.push({
    94→            title: item.title,
    95→            reason: `Exact duplicate of existing item ${top.kind}:${top.id} (${top.name})`,
    96→          });
    97→          continue;
    98→        }
    99→
   100→        // BM25 match means significant keyword overlap -- suggest merge
   101→        updated.push({
   102→          existing_id: `${top.kind}:${top.id}`,
   103→          title: top.name,
   104→          changes: `Merge context from suggested item '${item.title}'`,
   105→        });
   106→      }
   107→
   108→      return {
   109→        suggestions,
   110→        updated,
   111→        discarded,
   112→      };
   113→    },
   114→  });
   115→}
   116→
```

```
   461→export const LEARNING_TYPES = ["constraint", "instruction", "precedent"] as const;
   462→export type LearningType = (typeof LEARNING_TYPES)[number];
   463→
   464→export const LEARNING_STATUSES = ["active", "pending_approval", "dismissed", "superseded", "deactivated"] as const;
   465→export type LearningStatus = (typeof LEARNING_STATUSES)[number];
   466→
   467→export const LEARNING_SOURCES = ["human", "agent"] as const;
   468→export type LearningSource = (typeof LEARNING_SOURCES)[number];
   469→
   470→export const KNOWN_LEARNING_TARGET_AGENTS = [
   471→  { value: "chat_agent", label: "Chat Agent" },
   472→  { value: "pm_agent", label: "PM Agent" },
   473→  { value: "observer_agent", label: "Observer Agent" },
   474→  { value: "mcp", label: "MCP (Coding Agents)" },
   475→] as const;
   476→
   477→export type KnownLearningTargetAgent = (typeof KNOWN_LEARNING_TARGET_AGENTS)[number]["value"];
   478→
   479→export type LearningSummary = {
   480→  id: string;
   481→  text: string;
   482→  learningType: LearningType;
   483→  status: LearningStatus;
   484→  source: LearningSource;
   485→  priority: EntityPriority;
   486→  targetAgents: string[];
   487→  suggestedBy?: string;
   488→  patternConfidence?: number;
   489→  createdAt: string;
   490→  approvedAt?: string;
   491→  dismissedAt?: string;
   492→  dismissedReason?: string;
   493→  deactivatedAt?: string;
   494→};
   495→
   496→// --- Tool Registry contract types ---
   497→
   498→export const TOOL_RISK_LEVELS = ["low", "medium", "high", "critical"] as const;
   499→export type ToolRiskLevel = (typeof TOOL_RISK_LEVELS)[number];
   500→
   501→export const TOOL_STATUSES = ["active", "disabled"] as const;
   502→export type ToolStatus = (typeof TOOL_STATUSES)[number];
   503→
   504→export const MCP_TRANSPORTS = ["sse", "streamable-http"] as const;
   505→export type McpTransport = (typeof MCP_TRANSPORTS)[number];
   506→
   507→export const TOOL_SYNC_ACTIONS = ["create", "update", "disable", "unchanged"] as const;
   508→export type ToolSyncAction = (typeof TOOL_SYNC_ACTIONS)[number];
   509→
   510→export type ToolListItemResponse = {
   511→  id: string;
   512→  name: string;
   513→  toolkit: string;
   514→  description: string;
   515→  risk_level: ToolRiskLevel;
   516→  status: ToolStatus;
   517→  grant_count: number;
   518→  governance_count: number;
   519→  created_at: string;
   520→};
   521→
   522→export type ToolListResponse = {
   523→  tools: ToolListItemResponse[];
   524→};
   525→
   526→export type GrantDetailResponse = {
   527→  identity_id: string;
   528→  identity_name: string;
   529→  max_calls_per_hour?: number;
   530→  granted_at: string;
   531→};
   532→
   533→export type GovernancePolicyDetailResponse = {
   534→  policy_title: string;
   535→  policy_status: string;
   536→  conditions?: string;
   537→  max_per_call?: number;
   538→  max_per_day?: number;
   539→};
   540→
   541→export type ToolDetailResponse = ToolListItemResponse & {
   542→  input_schema: Record<string, unknown>;
   543→  grants: GrantDetailResponse[];
   544→  governance_policies: GovernancePolicyDetailResponse[];
   545→};
   546→
   547→export type McpServerListItemResponse = {
   548→  id: string;
   549→  name: string;
   550→  url: string;
   551→  transport: McpTransport;
   552→  last_status?: string;
   553→  tool_count: number;

   555→};
   556→
   557→export type McpServerListResponse = {

   559→};
   560→
   561→export type ToolSyncDetailResponse = {
   562→  name: string;
   563→  description: string;
   564→  input_schema: Record<string, unknown>;
   565→  action: ToolSyncAction;
   566→  risk_level: ToolRiskLevel;
   567→};
   568→
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's the summary:

**2 commits pushed:**

1. **`c116982b`** — Consolidated `McpToolDefinition` type and infrastructure tools (`get_context`, `create_intent`) into `brain-tool-definitions.ts`. Rewrote all definitions from hand-written JSON Schema to Zod schemas with `z4.toJSONSchema()` derivation.

2. **`cbe47006`** — 16 tool files now import their `inputSchema` from the shared definitions instead of duplicating Zod schemas inline. Net **-151 lines** of removed duplication.

**What's consolidated:**
- `brain-tool-definitions.ts` is the single source of truth for 19 tool schemas (Zod) + MCP definitions (derived JSON Schema)
- `tools-list-handler.ts` imports everything from there (no more inline definitions)
- 16 tool files in `tools/` import shared schemas

**Left intentionally separate:**
- `resolve-decision.ts` — chat agent uses nested `context.project/feature` + `options`; MCP uses flat `project/feature` (genuinely different schemas for different use cases)
- `cli/mcp-server.ts` — different tool names (`ask_question` vs `create_question`, `log_observation` vs `create_observation`), extra CLI-only tools, minimal overlap
